### 第 2 席


#### [G1] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:逐格核对全部命中。(1) 新闻稿 USDL-25-1324 抬头逐字:"For release 10:00 a.m. (ET) Thursday, August 28, 2025 USDL-25-1324";正文逐字:"The U.S. economy is projected to add 5.2 million jobs from 2024 to 2034... Total employment is projected to increase to 175.2 million and grow 3.1 percent, which is slower than the 13.0-percent growth recorded over the 2014-24 decade."(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.nr0.htm ,经 Wayback id_ 原始快照 20251228054323 取得,BLS 对 curl/WebFetch 一律 Akamai 403,最终用真实浏览器直取现网复核)。(2) Table 2.1 Employment by major industry sector 逐格:Total employment 2014=150,436.3;2024=169,956.1;2034=175,167.9;numeric change 2024–34=5,211.8;percent 2024–34=3.1;percent 2014–24=13.0(https://www.bls.gov/emp/tables/employment-by-major-industry-sector.htm)。三个数字全对。(3) Table 3.1 Civilian labor force 逐格:Total, 16 years and older 2024=168,104;2034=173,454;change=5,350;percent change=3.2(https://www.bls.gov/emp/tables/civilian-labor-force-summary.htm)。全对。(4) Technical Note 逐字核对无截断,原文用破折号而非连字符:"BLS does not project an overall labor shortage or surplus because in the BLS projections data framework, labor supply (the labor force) and labor demand (employment) are linked — a projected increase in labor supply necessarily results in an increase in employment."(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.tn.htm)。(5) 关于"数岗位 vs 数人"口径差,BLS 自己写得比论断更明确,原文:"employment in the projected year will be roughly equivalent to the projected labor force minus a level of frictional unemployment (the relationship is not exact because labor force is a count of people, while employment is a count of jobs, and individual people can hold more than one job)"。这解释了为何 2024 年就业(169,956.1 千个岗位)反而高于劳动力(168,104 千人)。备注(不构成修正):"3.1% 在数学上基本等同于 3.2%"是作者自己的算术推论,BLS 未写过这句;但 BLS 明文陈述的联动机制正好支撑该推论,且两个百分比确实只差 0.1 点,故承重成立。


#### [G2] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:三处口径需修:(1) 文章题名不全。原表述"MLR 2025-02(Machovec, Rieley & Rolen《Incorporating AI impacts in BLS employment projections》)" → 正确表述"《Incorporating AI impacts in BLS employment projections: occupational case studies》,Monthly Labor Review,2025 年 2 月,DOI 10.21916/mlr.2025.1"。副标题"occupational case studies"不可省——它限定了该文只是若干职业案例研究,不是全面方法论评估。(2) "BLS projection methods are not designed to capture extremely rapid technological change"是从句中截出,原句首尾各有一段被切掉。原表述(独立引语)→ 正确表述(补全整句)"Although it is always possible that future developments will deviate from historical patterns, BLS projection methods are not designed to capture extremely rapid technological change and, therefore, assume that the overall pace of technological change will be consistent with past experience." 语义未被扭曲,但被截掉的后半句正是 BLS 的自辩,单引前半句会读成 BLS 承认方法失效,实际 BLS 是在说"因此我们假定技术变化速度与历史一致"。(3) 卡车司机案例的职业口径过宽。原表述"卡车司机 2012 约 170 万 → 2023 逾 220 万" → 正确表述"heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers(重型及牵引挂车司机,53-3032)2012 年约 170 万 → 2023 年逾 220 万"。原文只讲这一个细分职业,不是全部卡车司机。另需标注利益相关:三位作者(Christine Machovec、Michael J. Rieley、Emily Rolen)均为 BLS 就业预测项目(Office of Occupational Statistics and Employment Projections)本部门经济学家,在本机构自办刊物上评价本项目方法,属自评而非独立评估。

**核验依据**:五段引语全部回一手逐字核对通过。Technical Note(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.tn.htm)逐字命中:"The projections focus on long-term structural trends of the economy and do not try to anticipate future business cycle activity." / "The projections are not intended to be a forecast of what the future will be but instead are a description of what would be expected to happen under these specific assumptions and circumstances." / "In a future state where technology advances much more rapidly than it has historically, it is unlikely that historical relationships would hold, and therefore BLS projection methods are unlikely to yield reasonable results." / "BLS methods could capture this, but BLS has no data on which to base these differential productivity impacts."(注:末句原文前面紧接"BLS methods could capture this, but",论断只引后半句,不影响语义)。MLR 原文(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2025/article/incorporating-ai-impacts-in-bls-employment-projections.htm)逐字命中:"The timing and scale of many potential impacts of GenAI are too uncertain to be reflected in BLS projections."(完整独立句,无截断)。两个案例数字逐字核对全中:"EP projected that employment in the occupation would decline 23.6 percent from 2004 to 2014. Indeed, employment in the occupation started to fall precipitously in the early 2000s, declining from a peak of 86,300 in 2004 to 28,800 in 2014, with a further decline to only 9,200 in 2023." 及 "EP did not make any adjustments to its models for another occupation, truck drivers... Employment of heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers was about 1.7 million in 2012 and had grown to over 2.2 million by 2023." 同行评审问题:MLR 确有同行评审流程(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/for-authors.htm 载明投稿经 peer review 后再进入 editorial review),故"MLR 是 BLS 官方刊物"成立且非无评审;但本文作者即被评价项目的在编人员,评审仍在机构内部体系内。另注:数码相机案例在原文中是被当作"研究驱动的主观调整成功案例"来写的(EP 提前调低了预测),而数字本身显示 BLS 仍大幅低估了跌幅(预测 −23.6%,实际 86,300→28,800 即 −66.6%),引用时不宜只取其一。


#### [G3] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:方框引语本身完全无误,但附加论断被证伪,必须删改。原表述"BLS 只发点估计,从不发布预测的置信区间或情景区间" → 正确表述"BLS 的常规十年预测只发布点估计,不附置信区间/预测区间;但 BLS 确曾发布过情景区间——2021 年初 BLS 就 2019–29 预测另行发布了两套替代情景(moderate impact 与 strong impact 两个疫情情景)的 alternate projections,明确用于标识哪些行业与职业的就业轨迹不确定性更高;更早年代 BLS 还例行发布过 low/moderate/high 三档增长替代预测"。"从不"二字是硬错误,必须去掉。另建议补全方框:论断只引了方框第一段,方框还有被略去的第二段,原文为 "The EP program also conducts research on factors that are expected to affect employment, which may not be reflected in historical data, such as emerging technologies and new legislation. Adjustments based on this research are generally applied conservatively such as when there is convincing evidence for a long-term structural change." 该段是 BLS 的自辩,略去会让方框显得比原文更像自我否定。方框标题为 "Interpreting the Employment Projections"。

**核验依据**:方框逐字核对完全命中,一字不差(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.nr0.htm,Wayback id_ 原始快照 20251228054323):"The Employment Projections (EP) program estimates specific values for projected employment levels and growth rates. However, this precision in the data does not account for the inherent uncertainty of predicting long-term changes in the labor market. Focusing on the direction and relative size of projected changes, rather than on the precise value estimates, may yield similar insights into employment trends and themes across occupations and industries." 论断的省略号处原文为 "into employment trends and themes across occupations and industries",省略无害。反证(这是本条的关键产出):MLR 2022-02《Expected pandemic-driven employment changes: a comparison of 2019–29 and 2020–30 projection sets》(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2022/article/expected-pandemic-driven-employment-changes.htm)摘要逐字:"Because these projections did not reflect the potential long-term impacts of the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic, BLS developed alternate 2019–29 projections in early 2021, capturing those impacts for selected industries and occupations. These latter projections, based on two alternate pandemic scenarios (moderate impact and strong impact), were followed (in September 2021) by BLS projections for the 2020–30 decade." 正文并逐字写明其不确定性量化用途:"these scenarios sought to identify industries and occupations whose employment trajectories might be subject to higher levels of uncertainty because of the pandemic." 另 MLR 1983/1985 年代预测文(如 https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/1985/11/art1full.pdf)载有 low/moderate/high 三档替代增长预测。按论断自设的判定规则("如果找到了,这条要 REFUTED"),该附加论断确已被证伪;此处仍判 CORRECTED 而非 REFUTED,是因为 G3 主体(方框引语)逐字无误,整条判 REFUTED 会误导读者以为方框也不可用——请将附加论断视为已被明确推翻。


#### [G4] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:核心论点(误差方向会翻转,单向偏差断言都不成立)成立且被强化,但四处口径必须修:(1) 2010–20 那句引语的出处已失效。原表述(暗示为现行页面)→ 正确表述"该句出自 BLS 预测评估首页的历史版本(页面 Last Modified Date: August 29, 2024;Wayback 快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20250601/https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/projections-evaluations.htm),现行页面(Last modified date: April 10, 2026)已删除此句";且原引语被截了后半句,完整为 "Due to the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on data for 2020, BLS did not evaluate the 2010–20 projections. BLS is in the process of evaluating previous evaluations." 现行页面改写为另一句意思完全不同的话:"With the release of the 2014–24 Projections Evaluations, BLS did not evaluate the 2014–24 Labor Force Projections or the Macro Projections." 若照原样引用并链现网,读者点进去找不到。(2) 57.9%(475/820)不是 BLS 发布的数字。原表述"BLS 在细分职业层面只赢过 naive model 57.9%(475/820)" → 正确表述"BLS 2014–24 职业评估 Table 2 公布的是 Count of better score:BLS 475、Naïve 345;820 与 57.9% 是据此自行推算,非 BLS 口径"。且该表述单边取材:同一张表 BLS 在总量指标上是赢的(Sum of absolute differences of employment shares:BLS 19.6 vs Naïve 21.1),大类层面 13:9 胜出。(3) 三组参与率的"系统性高估"需加限定。原表述"BLS 高估劳动参与率"(暗示劳动力预测整体偏乐观)→ 正确表述"BLS 高估的是劳动参与率,但同一篇评估明确认定其劳动力人数(level)预测三次都准确,因为 BLS 有意用上调参与率去抵消人口普查局的人口低估"。原文逐字:"BLS anticipated underprojections of population from the U.S. Census Bureau and offset them with slight adjustments to the BLS-projected labor force participation rates. These adjustments resulted in accurate projections of the labor force for each of the three sets of projections to 2006, 2008, and 2010." 不加这句会把"参与率口径的高估"误传成"劳动力供给预测的高估"。(4) 三个准确率数字的分母应写明:86% 的分母是 22 个 major occupational groups(2014–24);77% 同为 occupational groups(2012–22);69% 的分母是 major industry sectors(2014–24,评估表列 16 个),三者互不可比,不能并列成一组"准确率"。

**核验依据**:逐项回一手。(a) 参与率三组数字逐一核对全中,且核的是勘误后的版本:MLR 2015-11 Byun/Henderson/Toossi《Evaluation of BLS employment, labor force and macroeconomic projections to 2006, 2008, and 2010》(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2015/article/evaluation-of-bls-employment-labor-force-and-macroeconomic-projections.htm)。该文页首挂有勘误,且勘误对象恰是本论断依赖的图:"This article was corrected on December 10, 2015. In figure 2B, several of the projected labor force participation rates for 1996–2006 and 2000–10 originally shown in the figure were somewhat lower than the rates BLS actually projected. In figure 3C, the base year labor force participation rate for 2000–10 was corrected to 67.1 instead of 67.2." 我用页面 Highcharts 对象直接取 figure 3C 的现行(已更正)数据:Base year [66.8, 67.1, 67.1];Projected [67.6, 67.6, 67.5];Actual [66.2, 66.0, 64.7],分别对应 1996–2006 / 1998–2008 / 2000–10。论断三组数字与更正后版本完全一致。(b) 制造业:原文逐字 "However, the magnitude of the decline in manufacturing was greatly underestimated." 及 "BLS determined that the market was at its bottom and projected that very slight increases would occur in manufacturing employment between 1998–2008 and 2000–10." 成立。(c) 86%:https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2014-2024-occupational.htm 逐字 "BLS correctly projected whether an occupational group would grow or decline 86 percent of the time.";同页并载 projected average growth 6.5% vs actual 12.9%(2014–24 低估)。(d) 77%:https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2012-2022-occupational.htm 逐字 "BLS correctly projected whether an occupational group would grow or decline 77 percent of the time.";同页 projected 10.8% vs actual 13.2%(2012–22 低估)。(e) 69%:https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2014-2024-industry.htm 逐字 "BLS correctly projected which major industry sectors would grow and which would decline 69 percent of the time."(f) 475/345 见 2014–24 职业评估 Table 2。(g) 2010–20 那句:现行 https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/projections-evaluations.htm 已无此句(实测浏览器直取,页面注明 Last modified date: April 10, 2026);Wayback 2025-06 快照(页面自注 Last Modified Date: August 29, 2024)含完整两句。


#### [G5] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:我下载了 https://www.bls.gov/emp/projections-archive/2014-24.zip(9,823,242 字节)并解出其中 2014-24/occupation.xlsx 的 Table 1.2 逐格核对,又比对了官方 OMB/SOCPC 的 2010→2018 SOC crosswalk。结论:7 行里 4 行可直接承重,3 行必须改写。

【必须改写 1:Software developers 这一行,作为"BLS 预测行"是伪造的,判 REFUTED】原表述"Software developers: 1,114.0 / 1,300.7(+16.8%)/ 1,693.8(+52.0%)——实际增幅是预测的 3.1 倍" → 正确表述"2010 SOC 中不存在 'Software developers' 这一职业,2014–24 表里没有这一行。1,114.0 与 1,300.7 是把两个独立行相加得来的:15-1132 Software developers, applications(718.4 / 853.7)+ 15-1133 Software developers, systems software(395.6 / 447.0)。加总算术无误,但必须标明为自行合成,不能写成 BLS 行。且该合成口径与 2018 SOC 的 15-1252 不等价:官方 crosswalk 显示 15-1132 与 15-1133 各自都同时流向 15-1252 和 15-1253,而 15-1253 另有一部分来自 15-1199,因此 1,114.0→1,693.8 是下界、1,114.0→(1,693.8+201.7=1,895.5)是上界,真值在 +52.0% 与 +70.2% 之间。相应地'实际增幅是预测的 3.1 倍'应改为'至少 3.1 倍,上界约 4.2 倍'(52.0/16.8=3.10;70.2/16.8=4.18)。原文把下界当点估计,方向对但低报了自己的论点。"

【必须改写 2:Data scientists】原表述"Data scientists (15-2051):2010 SOC 中不存在该代码,2024 实际 245.9" → 正确表述补一句因果"15-2051 系 2018 SOC 新设,官方 crosswalk 显示其来源为 2010 SOC 的 15-2099 Mathematical Science Occupations, All Other;而 2014–24 表中对应桶 15-2090 Miscellaneous mathematical science occupations 全年仅 3.0 千人。故 2024 年的 245.9 千绝大部分是分类重编(把原先编在别处的人重新归类),不是可测量的职业增长,任何'数据科学家十年暴涨'的读法都是错的。"

【必须改写 3:Computer and mathematical occupations 组行】原表述"4,068.3 / 4,599.7(+13.1%)/ 5,416.7(+33.1%)" → 正确表述加限定"约 +33%(非严格可比)"。理由:15-0000 大组边界在 2018 SOC 双向都动过——流出:15-1199 Computer Occupations, All Other 的一部分被划到 13-1082 Project Management Specialists(离开 15-0000);流入:43-9011 Computer Operators 整体并入 15-1299(进入 15-0000)。两笔相对于 1,348.4 千的增量都不大,故 +33.1% 方向与量级仍可用,但不能当作严格同口径。

【可直接承重、无需修改的 4 行——官方 crosswalk 证实为严格 1:1,无拆分无合并】Computer programmers 15-1131→15-1251(328.6 / 302.2 −8.0% / 121.2 −63.1%);Computer systems analysts 15-1121→15-1211(567.8 / 686.3 +20.9% / 521.1 −8.2%,方向判反);Information security analysts 15-1122→15-1212(82.9 / 97.7 +17.9% / 182.8 +120.5%);Network and computer systems administrators 15-1142→15-1244(382.6 / 412.8 +7.9% / 331.5 −13.4%,方向判反)。特别说明:Computer programmers 那一行经 crosswalk 检验完全干净,是全表最可承重的一行,"BLS 大幅低估崩塌"成立,不必弱化。

【踩坑点复核】论断自列的两个踩坑点均属实:(a) 15-1253 Software quality assurance analysts and testers 2024 年 201.7 千,确为 2018 SOC 新设;(b) 15-1134 Web developers 确被拆为 15-1254 Web developers(86.0)+ 15-1255 Web and digital interface designers(128.9),单看前者会得出"148.5→86.0 腰斩"的错误结论,合并后为 214.9。

**核验依据**:BLS 对脚本一律 403(Akamai bot 拦截,返回 'Access Denied ... Automated retrieval programs'),故改用真实浏览器同源 fetch 取二进制并在页内解压解析。(1) 2014–24 预测值,取自 https://www.bls.gov/emp/projections-archive/2014-24.zip 内 2014-24/occupation.xlsx 的工作表 "Table 1.2 Employment by detailed occupation, 2014 and projected 2024 (Numbers in thousands)",逐格读出(列 D=2014、E=2024、I=percent):15-0000 Computer and mathematical occupations 4068.3 / 4599.7 / 13.1;15-1121 Computer systems analysts 567.8 / 686.3 / 20.9;15-1122 Information security analysts 82.9 / 97.7 / 17.9;15-1131 Computer programmers 328.6 / 302.2 / −8.0;15-1132 Software developers, applications 718.4 / 853.7 / 18.8;15-1133 Software developers, systems software 395.6 / 447.0 / 13.0;15-1134 Web developers 148.5 / 188.0 / 26.6;15-1142 Network and computer systems administrators 382.6 / 412.8 / 7.9;15-1199 Computer occupations, all other 233.0 / 240.8 / 3.3;15-2000 Mathematical science occupations 152.2 / 195.1 / 28.2(其下仅 Actuaries、Mathematicians、Operations research analysts、Statisticians、Miscellaneous 15-2090=3.0,确无 15-2051)。全表 1096 行,无任何名为 "Software developers" 的行。(2) 2024 实际值,取自现行 https://www.bls.gov/emp/tables/emp-by-detailed-occupation.htm(表题 "Table 1.2 Occupational projections, 2024–2034, and worker characteristics, 2024",2018 SOC):15-0000 5,416.7;15-1211 521.1;15-1212 182.8;15-1244 331.5;15-1251 121.2;15-1252 1,693.8;15-1253 201.7;15-1254 86.0;15-1255 128.9;15-1299 472.0;15-2051 245.9。论断所列 2024 实际值逐一命中。(3) 口径断层判定依据官方 crosswalk https://www.bls.gov/soc/2018/soc_2010_to_2018_crosswalk.xlsx(BLS 代 OMB 与 SOC Policy Committee 发布,2017 年 11 月),逐行读出:15-1121→15-1211、15-1122→15-1212、15-1131→15-1251、15-1142→15-1244 四对均无 (#)/(##) 拆分合并标记,严格一对一;15-1132 (#)→15-1252 (##) 与 15-1253 (##);15-1133 (#)→15-1252 (##) 与 15-1253 (##);15-1199 (#)→15-1253 (##)、15-1243、15-1255、15-1299、13-1082;15-1134 (#)→15-1254 与 15-1255 (##);15-2099 (#)→15-2051。我并全表扫描了跨 15-0000 大组边界的条目,结果仅两条:流出 15-1199→13-1082 Project Management Specialists,流入 43-9011 Computer Operators→15-1299。(4) 佐证 BLS 自己承认此类比较有困难:2014–24 职业评估页(https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2014-2024-occupational.htm)逐字 "The Employment Projections program relies on various data sources. Changes in methodology and classifications in these programs and surveys result in challenges with evaluating occupational projections at a detailed level.";MLR 2015 评估文更直接:"Evaluation of employment by occupation for these sets of projections was omitted entirely because of the revised classification system." 即 BLS 曾因分类改版而整个放弃做职业层面的评估。


### 第 3 席


#### [G1] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:三个数字与引文逐格/逐字无误,但机制表述需修正。原表述:「因为 BLS 模型强制令劳动供给=劳动需求」→ 正确表述:「因为 BLS 假设目标年(2034)处于充分就业、劳动力市场出清,而基期(2024)本身已接近充分就业;两端都无闲置产能可吸收,增速才趋同」。BLS 原文用的是 linked / necessarily results in,不是「强制相等」,且在同一段紧接着写明该关系并不精确:『the relationship is not exact because labor force is a count of people, while employment is a count of jobs, and individual people can hold more than one job』。第二处必须补:该文章若暗示「就业增速不可能超过劳动力增速」即为错误——BLS 自己的数据显示 2014→2024 劳动力实际 +7.8%(155,922→168,104),而就业实际 +13.0%(150,436.3→169,956.1),差 5.2 个百分点,原因正是 2014 年是宽松劳动力市场。第三处(精度):5,211.8/169,956.1 = 3.07%,5,350/168,104 = 3.18%,BLS 各自四舍五入为 3.1% 与 3.2%,「基本等同」成立但差 0.11pp。另注:169,956.1 与 168,104 口径不同(前者数岗位含自雇与第二职业,后者数人),两者不可直接相减。

**核验依据**:新闻稿 USDL-25-1324、2025年8月28日 10:00 ET 发布,逐字:「Total employment is projected to increase to 175.2 million and grow 3.1 percent, which is slower than the 13.0-percent growth recorded over the 2014-24 decade.」(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.nr0.htm,经 Wayback id_ 原始快照 20250902004042 取得)。EP Table 2.1「Total employment」逐格:2014=150,436.3 / 2024=169,956.1 / 2034=175,167.9 / 变动 19,519.8 与 5,211.8 / 13.0 与 3.1(https://www.bls.gov/emp/tables/employment-by-major-industry-sector.htm)。EP Table 3.1「Total, 16 years and older」逐格:2004=147,402 / 2014=155,922 / 2024=168,104 / 2034=173,454;变动 8,520 / 12,182 / 5,350;百分比 5.8 / 7.8 / 3.2(https://www.bls.gov/emp/tables/civilian-labor-force-summary.htm)。Technical Note 全段原文(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.tn.htm):「BLS also assumes that the economy will be at full employment in the projected year, with the labor market at equilibrium. That is, employment in the projected year will be roughly equivalent to the projected labor force minus a level of frictional unemployment (the relationship is not exact because labor force is a count of people, while employment is a count of jobs, and individual people can hold more than one job). BLS does not project an overall labor shortage or surplus because in the BLS projections data framework, labor supply (the labor force) and labor demand (employment) are linked — a projected increase in labor supply necessarily results in an increase in employment.」论断所引末句逐字无误、无前后限定语被截断,但论断省略了紧邻的 full-employment 假设句与「not exact」括注。注:bls.gov 对通用 UA 返回 403,需用含联系邮箱的 UA 才能取一手页面。


#### [G2] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:五处引文全部逐字核对无误、无断章。需修正三点。(1)原表述:「卡车司机 2012 约 170 万 → 2023 逾 220 万」→ 正确表述:「重型及牵引挂车卡车司机(heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers)2012 约 170 万 → 2023 逾 220 万」。MLR 原文限定为 heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers,并非全部卡车司机(全口径含轻型送货司机等,规模约为其两倍),不加限定会放大该案例的说服力。(2)原表述若把「BLS has no data on which to base these differential productivity impacts」当作 BLS 承认对 AI 整体无数据 → 正确表述:该句仅限于「分行业的差异化生产率影响」,完整句为「BLS methods could capture this, but BLS has no data on which to base these differential productivity impacts.」;BLS 明确表示若生产率提速在各行业均匀,则对就业预测无影响。(3)关于同行评审:MLR 是 BLS 自办官方刊物,内部编审,非独立同行评审;三位作者 Christine Machovec、Michael J. Rieley、Emily Rolen 均为 BLS 就业与失业统计办公室经济学家。即 BLS 雇员在 BLS 刊物上评估 BLS 方法——属自我评估,引用时应标明利益相关,不可称「同行评审文献」。另:摄影案例的 86,300 在原文是「a peak of 86,300 in 2004」(2004 年峰值),非普通基期值。

**核验依据**:Technical Note(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.tn.htm)逐字确认:「The projections focus on long-term structural trends of the economy and do not try to anticipate future business cycle activity.」;「The projections are not intended to be a forecast of what the future will be but instead are a description of what would be expected to happen under these specific assumptions and circumstances.」(其后紧接一句论断未引:「When these assumptions are not realized, actual values will differ from projections.」);「In a future state where technology advances much more rapidly than it has historically, it is unlikely that historical relationships would hold, and therefore BLS projection methods are unlikely to yield reasonable results.」;「BLS methods could capture this, but BLS has no data on which to base these differential productivity impacts.」。MLR 2025年2月 Machovec/Rieley/Rolen《Incorporating AI impacts in BLS employment projections: occupational case studies》(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2025/article/incorporating-ai-impacts-in-bls-employment-projections.htm)逐字:「Although it is always possible that future developments will deviate from historical patterns, BLS projection methods are not designed to capture extremely rapid technological change and, therefore, assume that the overall pace of technological change will be consistent with past experience.」;「The timing and scale of many potential impacts of GenAI are too uncertain to be reflected in BLS projections.」;摄影案例逐字:「EP projected that employment in the occupation would decline 23.6 percent from 2004 to 2014. Indeed, employment in the occupation started to fall precipitously in the early 2000s, declining from a peak of 86,300 in 2004 to 28,800 in 2014, with a further decline to only 9,200 in 2023.」;卡车案例逐字:「Employment of heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers was about 1.7 million in 2012 and had grown to over 2.2 million by 2023.」作者简介页同文标注三人职务均为 economist, Office of Employment and Unemployment Statistics, U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics。


#### [G3] REFUTED · confidence=high

**修正**:方框引文本身逐字无误(HOLDS),但附加论断「BLS 只发点估计,从不发布预测的置信区间或情景区间」为错,必须删除或改写。原表述:「BLS 从不发布预测的置信区间或情景区间」→ 正确表述:「BLS 从不发布统计意义上的置信区间,当期 2024–34 预测也只有点估计;但 BLS 有发布情景区间的先例——2019–29 那一轮除基线外另发布了 moderate impact 与 strong impact 两套 alternate projections,明确用于标识不确定性最高的行业」。BLS Career Outlook 逐字:『The alternate sets of projections are not intended as precise estimates of employment change over the 2019–29 decade; instead, they identify industries in which employment is subject to the most pandemic-related uncertainty.』这正是情景式不确定性量化。第二处:论断只引了方框第一段,漏掉第二段——「The EP program also conducts research on factors that are expected to affect employment, which may not be reflected in historical data, such as emerging technologies and new legislation. Adjustments based on this research are generally applied conservatively such as when there is convincing evidence for a long-term structural change.」这一段说明 BLS 确实会做前瞻性调整,只是很保守;略去它会把 BLS 描绘成纯外推模型,对读者不公。

**核验依据**:USDL-25-1324 内嵌方框「Interpreting the Employment Projections」逐字核对完全一致:「The Employment Projections (EP) program estimates specific values for projected employment levels and growth rates. However, this precision in the data does not account for the inherent uncertainty of predicting long-term changes in the labor market. Focusing on the direction and relative size of projected changes, rather than on the precise value estimates, may yield similar insights into employment trends and themes across occupations and industries.」(https://www.bls.gov/news.release/ecopro.nr0.htm)。反证一手来源:MLR 2021《Employment projections in a pandemic environment》逐字:「examines the impact of the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic on the U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics 2019–29 employment projections through two alternate scenarios: a moderate impact scenario and a strong impact scenario」「Two alternate scenarios, moderate impact and strong impact, were modeled as possible paths for the U.S. economy for 2019–29」(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2021/article/employment-projections-in-a-pandemic-environment.htm);BLS Career Outlook《Effects of the pandemic on projected employment in selected industries, 2019–29》逐字:「BLS developed alternate projections scenarios to analyze how pandemic-related circumstances are expected to affect long-term employment.」及上引不确定性识别句(https://www.bls.gov/careoutlook/... 实际 URL:https://www.bls.gov/careeroutlook/2021/data-on-display/alternate-projections.htm)。


#### [G4] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:全部数字与引文均已找到一手出处并逐字核对无误,含此前无出处的 57.9%。需修正三点口径。(1)原表述:「86%、77%、69% 是三个分母不同的数字」→ 正确表述:「是两个分母:86%(2014–24)与 77%(2012–22)分母相同,都是 22 个大类职业组(major occupational groups),只是预测轮次不同;69% 分母不同,是 16 个大类产业部门(major industry sectors)」。(2)原表述:「1996–06/1998–2008/2000–10 三版 BLS 高估劳动参与率」为真,但不可外推为「高估了劳动力规模」→ 需补:MLR 原文明确说这三版的劳动力总量预测是准的,因为 BLS 是为抵消预期中的人口普查低估而主动上调参与率,两个误差相互抵消:「These adjustments resulted in accurate projections of the labor force for each of the three sets of projections to 2006, 2008, and 2010.」真正被系统性高估的是就业总量与商品生产部门(2006 高估约 7%、2008 高估 15.8%、2010 高估 33.4%)。(3)原表述引 2014–24「实际 12.9%」时若与 G1 的「13.0%」混用即错→ 12.9% 是各职业平均增长率(对应预测 6.5%),13.0% 是总就业增长率,两者是不同测度,数值接近纯属巧合,不可互换。另补两点背景:57.9%(475/820)的 820 是 BLS 对 SOC 2010→2018 做了映射后纳入全部职业的结果;而 2012–22 那次只保留 1:1 匹配的 748 个职业,BLS 胜出 432/748 = 57.8%,两轮几乎相同。「2010–20 未评估」那句原文位于 Methods of Projections Evaluation 页,不在 evaluations 首页(首页当前只写「With the release of the 2014–24 Projections Evaluations, BLS did not evaluate the 2014–24 Labor Force Projections or the Macro Projections」——即 2014–24 的劳动力与宏观评估也被跳过,这是论断未提及的第二处评估缺口)。

**核验依据**:2014–24 职业评估逐字:「BLS correctly projected whether an occupational group would grow or decline 86 percent of the time.」「The projected average growth rate for occupations from 2014 to 2024 was 6.5 percent.」「The actual average growth rate for occupations from 2014 to 2024 was 12.9 percent.」「For the 22 major occupational groups...」;Table 2「Detailed occupations, combined scorecard — Count of better score: BLS 475, Naïve 345」(475+345=820,475/820=57.93%)(https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2014-2024-occupational.htm)。2012–22 职业评估逐字:「BLS correctly projected whether an occupational group would grow or decline 77 percent of the time.」「projected average growth rate ... 10.8 percent」「actual ... 13.2 percent」;Table 2 Count of better score: BLS 432 / Naïve 316(=748)(https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2012-2022-occupational.htm)。2014–24 产业评估逐字:「BLS correctly projected which major industry sectors would grow and which would decline 69 percent of the time.」Table 2 列出 16 个 major sectors(Mining…State and local government)(https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2014-2024-industry.htm)。方法页逐字:「Due to the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on data for 2020, BLS did not evaluate the 2010–20 projections.」及 SOC 断层处理说明「For the 2014–24 evaluation ... BLS applied a mapping between the two SOC classification systems that allowed for all occupations to be included in the comparison.」(https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/methods.htm)。评估首页确认评估序列由 2008–18 直接跳至 2012–22,无 2010–20(https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/projections-evaluations.htm,最后修改 2026-04-10)。参与率三组数字来自 MLR 2015年11月 Byun、Henderson、Toossi《Evaluation of BLS employment, labor force and macroeconomic projections to 2006, 2008, and 2010》逐字:「The participation rate projected for 2006 was 67.6 but the actual rate was 66.2. ... For 2008, BLS projected a 67.6-percent participation rate, higher than the 66.0 percent realized. The 2010 rate also was overprojected at 67.5 percent since the actual rate fell more substantially to 64.7 percent after the great recession of 2007–09.」;制造业逐字:「the magnitude of the decline in manufacturing was greatly underestimated」及「The largest directional error was the belief, in all three sets of projections, that manufacturing had reached its trough and would stay nearly flat」;高估逐字:「Since the macroeconomic model overprojected U.S. employment, industry sectors also tended to be projected higher than what was realized」「overprojected the goods sectors by nearly 7 percent in 2006, more severely by 15.8 percent in 2008, and 33.4 percent in 2010」(https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2015/article/evaluation-of-bls-employment-labor-force-and-macroeconomic-projections.htm)。


#### [G5] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:我已下载 2014-24.zip 与当期 occupation.xlsx 逐格核对:表中 14 个数字全部准确无误(4,068.3/4,599.7/5,416.7;1,114.0/1,300.7/1,693.8;82.9/97.7/182.8;328.6/302.2/121.2;567.8/686.3/521.1;382.6/412.8/331.5;245.9;201.7;86.0;128.9),百分比亦复算一致。再用 OMB/BLS 官方 2010→2018 SOC 交叉表逐码判定可比性,结论如下,须据此改写。【可全额承重(1:1 干净映射,两向唯一,无拆无并)】computer programmers 15-1131→15-1251、computer systems analysts 15-1121→15-1211、information security analysts 15-1122→15-1212、network and computer systems administrators 15-1142→15-1244。即文章最重的四行(程序员 −63.1% vs 预测 −8.0%、系统分析师方向判反、信息安全 +120.5% vs +17.9%、网络管理员方向判反)完全成立,不受 SOC 断层影响。【须加限定但方向不变】software developers:原表述「1,114.0 / 1,300.7」→ 须注明这不是 BLS 单一行,而是 2010 SOC 两码 15-1132(718.4/853.7)+15-1133(395.6/447.0)之和。更关键:2018 SOC 的 15-1252 只从 15-1132、15-1133 取值,但这两码同时还分流给了新设的 15-1253 software quality assurance analysts and testers(2024 年 201.7)。故 15-1252 是 2010 口径的真子集,「+52.0%、实际增幅是预测的 3.1 倍」是保守下界;把 15-1253 全部计入的同口径上界为 1,114.0→1,895.5 = +70.2%,即 4.2 倍。结论:该行不必 REFUTED,论断误在偏保守一侧,但必须写成「至少 +52%(同口径可比区间 +52%~+70%)」。【须加脚注】computer and mathematical 15-0000 存在两条跨大类流动:43-9011 computer operators(2014 年 61.1,占基期 1.5%)并入 15-1299;15-1199 的一部分流出至 13-1082 project management specialists(15-1199 2014 年共 233.0)。净影响为个位数百分点,+33.1% vs 预测 +13.1% 的落差仍成立,但须标注非严格可比。【必须弃用或彻底改写】data scientists 15-2051:「2010 SOC 中不存在该代码」正确,但官方交叉表显示其名义来源仅为 15-2099 mathematical science occupations, all other——2014 年该码只有 1.8 千,而 2024 年 data scientists 为 245.9 千。即这 245.9 千绝大部分是从其他职业码重新归类而来,不是可测量的新增就业;若把 245.9 当作「数据科学家岗位增长」呈现给准大学生,是重大误导,必须删除或明确标注为分类变更产物。【论断自带的踩坑提示经核对属实】web developers:15-1134(2014 年 148.5)确实被拆为 15-1254(86.0)+15-1255(128.9)=214.9,即 +44.7% 而非「腰斩」;但 15-1255 同时还从 15-1199 取值,故 214.9 是上界。最后补一条对论断有利的旁证:BLS 自己也承认此处必须做 crosswalk——其评估方法页写明 2014–24 评估「applied a mapping between the two SOC classification systems ... aggregating employment from multiple occupations or applying ratios based on previous employment, depending on how the occupations were split or combined between the 2010 and 2018 SOC systems」。

**核验依据**:一手文件一:https://www.bls.gov/emp/projections-archive/2014-24.zip → 2014-24/occupation.xlsx, Table 1.2「Employment by detailed occupation」,逐格读出 15-0000 4068.3/4599.7/+13.1、15-1121 567.8/686.3/+20.9、15-1122 82.9/97.7/+17.9、15-1131 328.6/302.2/−8.0、15-1132 718.4/853.7、15-1133 395.6/447.0、15-1134 148.5/188.0、15-1142 382.6/412.8/+7.9、15-1199 233.0、15-2099 1.8;另 43-9011 computer operators 2014=61.1。一手文件二:https://www.bls.gov/emp/ind-occ-matrix/occupation.xlsx(2024–34 当期),Table 1.2,逐格读出 15-0000 5416.7、15-1211 521.1、15-1212 182.8、15-1251 121.2、15-1252 1693.8、15-1253 201.7、15-1254 86.0、15-1255 128.9、15-1244 331.5、15-1299 472.0、15-2051 245.9、13-1082 1046.3。一手文件三(判定可比性的决定性证据):OMB/BLS 官方交叉表 https://www.bls.gov/soc/2018/soc_2010_to_2018_crosswalk.xlsx,逐行显示:15-1131→15-1251(无 # 标记,未拆分);15-1121→15-1211;15-1122→15-1212;15-1142→15-1244;15-1132(#)→15-1252(##) 与 15-1253(##);15-1133(#)→15-1252(##) 与 15-1253(##);15-1199(#)→15-1253/15-1255/15-1243/15-1299/13-1082;15-1134(#)→15-1254 与 15-1255(##);15-2099(#)→15-2051 与 15-2099;43-9011→15-1299(##)。我以程序穷举了「非 15 类流入 15 类」与「15 类流出非 15 类」,结果各仅一条(43-9011 流入、15-1199→13-1082 流出),据此界定 15-0000 的可比性缺口。BLS 自陈需做映射见 https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/methods.htm。


## 批次 A2 — verify:openings陷阱


### 第 1 席


#### [G6] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:BLS www.bls.gov blocks direct download (403). Retrieved the live 2024–34 occupation.xlsx via Wayback raw snapshot https://web.archive.org/web/20260612175342id_/https://www.bls.gov/emp/ind-occ-matrix/occupation.xlsx (CDX digest 7KSX3BRWFCEZTIXTWMSOTGWE6U6Y42NF is identical across all snapshots 2025-09-10 → 2026-06-12, i.e. no revision since the 2024–34 release). Opened with openpyxl. Sheet 'Table 1.10', title cell verbatim: 'Table 1.10 Occupational separations and openings, projected 2024–34 (Employment in thousands)'. Row 3, 'Total, all occupations' / 00-0000 / Summary reads cell-for-cell: Employment 2024=169956.1; Employment 2034=175167.9; change numeric=5211.8; change pct=3.1; exit rate=4.7; transfer rate=5.9; total sep rate=10.6; Labor force exits=8155; Occupational transfers=10187.1; Total occupational separations=18342.1; Occupational openings=18863.3. Column header verbatim: 'Occupational openings, 2024–34 annual average' — the annual-average framing is correct. Identity: 18342.1 + 5211.8/10 = 18863.28 ≈ 18863.3 ✓. Shares: 5211.8/10/18863.3 = 2.763% → 2.8%; 18342.1/18863.3 = 97.237% → 97.2% ✓. 52.12万/年 and 1834.21万/年 ✓. Definition sentence verified verbatim on https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations.htm (Wayback 20260612174136): 'To project occupational openings, the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) calculates an estimate of separations caused by workers exiting the labor force, due to retirement or other reasons, and separations caused by workers transferring to different occupations. Projections of separations are combined with projections of employment change to determine occupational openings. This estimate of openings does not count workers who change jobs but remain in the same occupation.' JOLTS-vs-EP distinction confirmed by BLS's own separations FAQ (https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations-faqs.htm, Wayback 2026): 'The JOLTS job openings metric is a stock measure of the number of job openings, or positions that are open, not filled, on the last business day of a month. In contrast, occupational openings are projections of the number of positions that are created and filled on an annual basis... There is therefore no way to create an annual average metric of job openings from JOLTS that could be compared with the annual average number of occupational openings.' Two distinct constructs confirmed. ONE FRAMING CAVEAT the article should carry (not an error in G6): of the 18,342.1 separations, 10,187.1 (55.5%) are occupational TRANSFERS and only 8,155.0 (44.5%) are labor-force EXITS — so the '97.2% 补缺' is majority job-switching churn, not retirement backfill. Readers will otherwise imagine boomers retiring.


#### [G7] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:数据部分逐格无误,不动。需要修的是那句 BLS Career Outlook 引语的射程。原表述:「另:BLS Career Outlook 自己承认 'Each of the occupations in chart 1, like most at this education level, had wages below the median for all occupations.'」——放在 top-15 openings 榜之后,读起来像 BLS 在承认「openings 榜整体低薪」。→ 正确表述:该句只针对 chart 1,而 chart 1 是这篇文章里「No formal educational credential(无需正规学历)」这一档的 openings 前几名,不是全职业 openings 前 15 名;'this education level' 指的就是无学历档。要么改写为「BLS 自己在按学历拆分 openings 时承认:无学历档的头部 openings 职业『had wages below the median for all occupations』」,要么直接删掉、只留 Table 1.2 自算的 13/15。另外补一句口径:$49,500 是 OEWS 2024 年 5 月全职业中位年薪,只覆盖非农工薪雇员,不含自雇者(Table 1.2 脚注 [1]);排名是在 832 个 'Line item' 明细职业内排的,不含 Summary 汇总行。

**核验依据**:同一份 occupation.xlsx。基准值:Table 1.2 row 3 'Total, all occupations' 的 'Median annual wage, dollars, 2024[1]' = 49500 ✓,并被 Career Outlook 文中独立复述('the median annual wage for all occupations was $49,500 in 2024')。我把 Table 1.2 的 832 条 Line item 按 openings 降序排,再与 Table 1.10 按 SOC code 连接,15 行逐格全中,无一例外:Fast food and counter workers 904.3/$30,480/sep 881.0=97.4%/net +233.2 十年=+23.32 千每年;Home health and personal care aides 765.8/34,900/90.3%;Retail salespersons 555.8/34,580/100.4%;Cashiers 542.6/31,190/105.8%;Stockers and order fillers 472.3/37,090/95.0%;Waiters and waitresses 456.7/33,760/100.4%;Laborers and freight, stock, and material movers, hand 384.3/38,940/98.9%;Janitors and cleaners 351.3/35,930/98.6%;Customer service representatives 341.7/42,830/104.5%;General and operations managers 308.7/102,950/94.7%;Office clerks, general 282.4/43,630/106.3%;Cooks, restaurant 250.7/36,830/91.3%;Heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers 237.6/57,440/96.3%;Nursing assistants 204.1/39,530/98.4%;Secretaries and administrative assistants, except legal, medical, and executive 202.8/46,290/101.5%。低于 $49,500 的正好 13 个,高于的只有 General and operations managers($102,950)和 Heavy truck drivers($57,440)✓。'>100% 即萎缩' 不只是相关,是恒等式:openings = 年均 separations + 十年 change/10,我在全 832 条上验过(残差全部 ≤0.1,纯四舍五入)。所以 sep/openings>100% ⟺ change<0,充分必要。五个 >100% 的职业十年净变化实测:retail −19.6、cashiers −313.6、customer service reps −153.7、office clerks −177.8、secretaries −30.8,全部为负 ✓。Career Outlook 原文取自 https://www.bls.gov/careeroutlook/2025/article/education-level-and-openings-2024-34.htm(Wayback 20260612174813)第 269 行,前一段第 266 行写明 'The occupations in chart 1 account for nearly 7 out of 10 openings projected at this education level.',而 chart 1 所在小节是无学历档——同文第 333/401/454/529 行分别对 chart 2–5 给出相反结论(chart 4、chart 5 '每一个' 都高于全职业中位数)。引语本身逐字无误,错的是射程。


#### [G8] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:我用 openpyxl 从同一份 occupation.xlsx 的 Table 1.2 里取 832 条 'Line item',按第 13 列 'Typical education needed for entry' 分组独立重算(不看采集者的数)。年均 openings(千人 / 占比):High school diploma or equivalent 6753.6 / 35.81%;No formal educational credential 6287.8 / 33.34%;Bachelor's degree 3333.5 / 17.67%;Postsecondary nondegree award 1161.1 / 6.16%;Some college, no degree 440.2 / 2.33%;Associate's degree 332.3 / 1.76%;Master's degree 316.8 / 1.68%;Doctoral or professional degree 236.3 / 1.25%。八个数与论断逐个吻合 ✓。三学位档 openings 合计 3886.6 千 ✓,占比 20.61%(明细行分母 18861.6)或 20.60%(published Total 18863.3)——两种分母都是 20.6% ✓。2024 就业:三学位档 42469.6+3828.8+4511.3 = 50809.7 千 ✓ = 29.90%(published Total 169956.1)✓。十年净增:Bachelor's 2378.5(45.65%)+ Master's 391.7(7.52%)+ Doctoral 255.0(4.89%)= 3025.2 千 ✓;3025.2/5211.8(published Total)= 58.05% → 58.0% ✓(若用明细行加总 5210.7 作分母是 58.06% → 58.1%,故写 58.0% 需锁定 published Total 分母;差异不影响结论)。Some college, no degree = −157.0 千,是八档中唯一为负的 ✓(其余七档全为正)。机制论断我用数据独立验了,成立且梯度单调:按学历档的员工加权年均总 separation rate 为 Doctoral 4.67% < Master's 7.25% < Bachelor's 7.29% < Associate's 9.10% < Postsecondary nondegree 10.52% < Some college 10.83% < High school 10.96% < No formal credential 15.47%(拆开看 exit rate 2.72%→7.08%、transfer rate 1.96%→8.38%,两个分量同向)。指派字段定义在 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/definitions.htm 逐字核对:'The Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) education and training classification system consists of three categories of information that BLS analysts have assigned to each detailed occupation. The categories are: 1) typical education needed for entry...' 及 'Typical education needed for entry. Occupations are assigned one of the following eight education levels:'——确为 BLS 分析师指派给职业的属性,不是在岗者实际学历分布 ✓,该限定语必须保留。提醒一处读者容易误读、但论断本身没说错的地方:58.0% 的分母是净增(已被衰退职业对冲后的余额,+5211.8 千),不是新增岗位总量;三学位档的 openings 少+净增占比高,两个数说的是不同的东西,并列时要写清。


#### [G9] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:两个 vintage 我都从 BLS 官方 archive zip 解出原始 xlsx 后逐格核。2014–24(projections-archive/2014-24.zip → 2014-24/occupation.xlsx):Table 1.2 表头第 2 行第 10 列逐字为 'Job openings due to growth and replacements, 2014-24'(无 annual average 字样),第 5 行 'Total, all occupations' 00-0000 Summary = 150539.9 / 160328.8 / 100 / 100 / 9788.9 / 6.5 / **46506.9** ✓。2016–26(2016-26.zip → 2016-26/occupation.xlsx):表头逐字为 'Occupational openings, 2016-26 annual average',第 5 行 Total = 156063.8 / 167582.3 / 100 / 100 / 11518.6 / 7.4 / **18981.5** ✓。46506.9 确为十年累计而非年均,有两重独立证据:(a) 同一工作簿 Table 1.10 标题为 'Table 1.10 Employment change, replacement needs, and job openings projected 2014-2024',Total 行给出 '2014-24 Replacement rate' = 23.5(十年口径的百分比)与 'Replacement needs' = 35326.0,而 35326.0 + 正增长部分 = 46506.9;(b) 46506.9/150539.9 = 30.9%/十年,若当作年均则等于每年 31% 的开口,与任何口径都不合。年化后 46506.9/10 = 4650.69;18981.5/4650.69 = **4.081 倍**,'约 4.1 倍' ✓。BLS 自我判决逐字核对无误,出处 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/replacements.htm(Wayback 20260613002033,页面 Last Modified Date: August 28, 2025),原文:'BLS used a cohort-component method for estimating job openings due to replacement needs from the 1991 through the 2014–24 projections. This method is no longer in use because BLS identified statistical and conceptual issues with the implementation of this method that compromised the accuracy and validity of the resulting estimates.' ✓ 第三方质疑我主动找了:没有找到对新 separations 法本身的公开学术质疑,但找到两条独立佐证跳升幅度的从业者记录——Chmura Economics 2018-02-28 'Occupational Separations: Components and Applications' 逐字写道 'When the BLS switched from replacement rates to separation rates, it was quite a shock for many of us. On average, these rates measuring this type of labor demand more than quadrupled!';另有州级 LMI(华盛顿州 ESD)自 2017 年起改用本州自定的替代分离法而非直接套用 BLS 分离率,属于对新法在州层面适用性的实务保留。两处技术细节供撰稿时留意(不构成论断错误):(1) 老口径的 openings 是 replacement needs + **仅正增长**(46506.9 − 35326.0 = 11180.9 > 净增 9788.9,差额 1392 即衰退职业的降幅未被扣减),新口径则是 separations + **净变化**(可为负),所以两者不只是分离法不同,增长项的处理也变了;(2) 若先把两个 vintage 各自除以其基期就业再比(3.089%/年 vs 12.163%/年),倍数是 3.94 而非 4.08——把 4.1 倍全部归因于方法变更略有高估,幅度约 4%,在'约'字覆盖范围内。


#### [G-extra] REFUTED · confidence=high

**修正**:原表述:「公开文献中基本不存在非 BLS 作者对 BLS 职业预测准确度的系统性量化评估」;「唯一找到的独立学者批评是 Peter Cappelli」。→ 正确表述:至少有两项非 BLS 作者做的、带正式误差指标和朴素模型基准的系统性量化评估,且都能拿到全文。(1) H.O. Stekler(乔治华盛顿大学经济系研究教授)与 Rupin Thomas,《Evaluating BLS labor force, employment, and occupation projections for 2000》,Monthly Labor Review, July 2005, pp.46–56——对 1988→2000 的 9 个大类和 338 个细类职业算了 MAPE、相异指数、Spearman 秩相关和列联表,并用两个朴素模型做基准。(2) John H. Bishop 与 Shani Carter(康奈尔 ILR),《How Accurate Are Recent BLS Occupational Projections?》,Monthly Labor Review, October 1991——评估 BLS 1980 年代职业预测的准确度,BLS 编者按明确将其定位为对本局方法的 comment/criticism。两文都发在 MLR(BLS 自家刊物),但作者均非 BLS,论断自己给的标准是「非 BLS 作者」,该标准已被满足。另需修 Cappelli 那条的射程:原文那句 'Governments do not have a particularly good record of forecasting where jobs will be years in advance, however, and students and their families would bear the costs when those forecasts are wrong.' 出现在讨论佛罗里达州按雇主意愿调拨专业经费的段落里,主语是 Governments 泛指,全文没有在这句上下文里点名或引用 BLS Employment Projections——把它当作「独立学者对 BLS 职业预测的批评」是移花接木,应改写为「对政府主导的专业—岗位对口预测的一般性批评」。BLS 那句自陈方向正确、可保留。

**核验依据**:(a) 主动搜反证并取到一手全文。Stekler & Thomas 原文 PDF 我从 Wayback 取到(https://web.archive.org/web/2020id_/https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2005/07/art5full.pdf,11 页,pdftotext 全文),作者栏逐字:'H.O. Stekler is a research professor in—and Rupin Thomas, a graduate of—the Department of Economics, George Washington University.' 量化结论摘要:大类 9 组的 MAPE 显著小于朴素模型且方向判对 8/9(农业除外);但 338 个细类的未加权 MAPE 高达 45.2%(加权 15.0%,说明大误差集中在小职业);BLS 预测的「增长最快 20 个职业」实际只有 6 个真的最快;338 个职业预测增速与实际增速的 Spearman 秩相关仅 0.43,原文写 'the projections for many occupations were clearly inaccurate and explains why the Spearman rank correlation coefficient is only 0.43'。这正是论断说「不存在」的那种系统性量化评估。Bishop & Carter 我从康奈尔 eCommons 的 DSpace REST API 取到元数据与原始 PDF(item 3afb9f5d-9a38-47be-8454-be64c770565f,handle https://hdl.handle.net/1813/75634,bitstream Bishop45_How_Accurate_Are_Recent.pdf,7 页):dc.date.issued=1991-10-01,dc.rights 注明 'Material published in the Monthly Labor Review is in the public domain';正文自述 'This communication offers an evaluation of the accuracy of the BLS projections of employment growth in the 1980's by major occupational groups. It also considers the accuracy of earlier projections covering the 1960's and 1970's...',作者栏 'John H. Bishop is associate professor, and Shani Carter is a graduate student, New York State School of Industrial and Labor Relations, Cornell University.',并有 BLS 编者按 'The Bureau is always receptive to comment or criticism of its data or methods in this or any other program. In that spirit, the following communication by John Bishop and Shani Carter comments on the Bureau's 1990 occupational projections.' (b) Cappelli 已发表版**未能核到**:ILR Review 68(2), 251–290, DOI 10.1177/0019793914564961 的确存在,但 SagePub 全文付费墙,ResearchGate 403,PhilArchive 403,semanticscholar 那个 PDF 打开是同题幻灯片不是论文。WP 版(60 余页)到 published 版(40 页)有实质删减的可能性存在,**该句是否保留、页码为何我无法确认,这一子项判 UNVERIFIABLE**;建议正文只引 NBER WP 20382 (Aug 2014) 并标明是工作论文,或改引已发表版的其他可核段落。WP 版原句我逐字核过,在本地 PDF 的 p.48–49 交界处,与论断给的引文完全一致。(c) BLS 自陈那句找到出处并逐字核对,全句为:'An important way to evaluate any projection is to compare it against other, similar projections. This is not possible for occupational projections because there are no comparable projections which are not in some way derived from BLS projections.' 出处 https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2014-2024-occupational.htm(Wayback 20260613063741),同句亦见于 https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2012-2022-occupational.htm。该句还被 Stekler & Thomas 独立佐证:'no other organization made projections of these variables. Consequently, there is no benchmark for judging the BLS forecasts.' 利益相关备注:BLS 是被评估方本身,其「无可比预测」的自陈同时也是其免于外部对标的理由,引用时宜配上 Stekler 的独立同向表述。


### 第 2 席


#### [G6] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:一手核对 occupation.xlsx（2024–34 版，经 Wayback id_ 快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20260612175342id_/https://www.bls.gov/emp/ind-occ-matrix/occupation.xlsx 取得，digest 7KSX… 自 2025-09-10 起即为 2024–34 vintage）。Table 1.10 表头逐字为 'Occupational openings, 2024–34 annual average'；'Total, all occupations'(00-0000, Summary) 行逐格为 169956.1 / 175167.9 / 5211.8 / 3.1 / 4.7 / 5.9 / 10.6 / 8155 / 10187.1 / 18342.1 / 18863.3——与论断完全一致。恒等式复算：18342.1 + 5211.8/10 = 18863.28 ≈ 18863.3（表内四舍五入），且我对全部 832 个 Line item 逐行验证 openings = separations + change/10，偏差 >0.1 者为 0 例，最大偏差 0.000。占比复算：521.18/18863.3 = 2.763% → 2.8%；18342.1/18863.3 = 97.237% → 97.2%。定义句逐字命中 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations.htm：'Projections of separations are combined with projections of employment change to determine occupational openings. This estimate of openings does not count workers who change jobs but remain in the same occupation.' JOLTS 与 EP openings 确为两个构念，BLS 自己在 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations-faqs.htm 明写：'The JOLTS job openings metric is a stock measure of the number of job openings, or positions that are open, not filled, on the last business day of a month. In contrast, occupational openings are projections of the number of positions that are created and filled on an annual basis'，并说明 'There is therefore no way to create an annual average metric of job openings from JOLTS that could be compared with the annual average number of occupational openings.' 补充提醒（不影响判决）：97.2% 中有 10187.1 千（占 openings 54.0%）是 occupational transfers，即转去别的职业的人，不是退休/退出劳动力；BLS 同页另有使用限定 'projections of openings are best used to give a general sense of the scale of expected openings, especially in relation to other occupations'。


#### [G7] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:1) 关键论断的枚举漏了一个。原表述：'sep 占比 >100% 意味着该职业就业在萎缩（retail salespersons、cashiers、customer service reps、office clerks、secretaries 均净减少）' → 正确表述：'前 15 名中有 6 个 sep 占比 >100%、即十年净减少：retail salespersons(100.4%, −19.6 千)、cashiers(105.8%, −313.6 千)、waiters and waitresses(100.4%, −16.3 千)、customer service representatives(104.5%, −153.7 千)、office clerks, general(106.3%, −177.8 千)、secretaries and administrative assistants(101.5%, −30.8 千)'。Waiters 在原文的分行数据里已给出 100.4%，却未被列入萎缩名单，前后自相矛盾。2) 基准值需写明口径。原表述：'低于全职业中位数' → 正确表述：'低于全职业 2024 年中位年薪 49,500 美元（OEWS 口径，仅覆盖非农工资薪金雇员，不含自雇者与农场工人）'。3) BLS Career Outlook 那句引文的指涉范围要写明。原表述（易被读成 BLS 承认整张 openings 榜低薪）→ 正确表述：'该句出自 BLS Career Outlook《Education level and projected openings, 2024–34》，chart 1 指的是「No formal educational credential」这一学历档内 openings 前 10 的职业，不是全经济 openings 前 15 榜'。

**核验依据**:Table 1.10（Line item）按 openings 降序前 15 行逐格复算，与论断 15 行数字完全一致（Fast food 904.3/881.0/97.42%/+23.32 千每年；Home health 765.8/90.3%；Retail 555.8/100.36%；Cashiers 542.6/105.79%；Stockers 472.3/95.0%；Waiters 456.7/100.37%；Laborers 384.3/98.9%；Janitors 351.3/98.6%；CSR 341.7/104.51%；GOM 308.7/94.7%；Office clerks 282.4/106.30%；Cooks 250.7/91.3%；Truck drivers 237.6/96.3%；Nursing assistants 204.1/98.4%；Secretaries 202.8/101.53%）。工资取自同一工作簿 Table 1.2 第 12 列 'Median annual wage, dollars, 2024[1]'，15 个值逐格与论断一致；'Total, all occupations' 该列 = 49500。以 49500 为界，仅 General and operations managers(102,950) 与 Heavy and tractor-trailer truck drivers(57,440) 在其上，其余 13 个在其下——'13 个'准确。'>100% 即萎缩'的推论在数学上成立：openings ≡ separations + 年均就业变动（我对全部 832 个 Line item 验证，零例外），故 sep/openings > 1 ⟺ 年均就业变动 < 0。但枚举漏了 Waiters and waitresses（35-3031，2024=2329.7、2034=2313.5、change=−16.3、−0.7%、sep%=100.37%）。Career Outlook 原文逐字（https://www.bls.gov/careeroutlook/2025/article/education-level-and-openings-2024-34.htm，Wayback 20260612174813）：'Each of the occupations in chart 1, like most at this education level, had wages below the median for all occupations.'，同页并写明 'For comparison, the median annual wage for all occupations was $49,500 in 2024.'，而 chart 1 明确是 'No formal educational credential' 档下 openings 前 10（BLS 称该档共 109 个职业，与我按 Table 1.2 分组计数的 109 完全吻合）。Table 1.2 脚注 [1] 逐字：'Data are from the Occupational Employment and Wage Statistics program… Wage data cover non-farm wage and salary workers and do not cover the self-employed…'


#### [G8] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:我用 openpyxl 独立下载并按 Table 1.2 第 13 列 'Typical education needed for entry' 对全部 832 个 Line item 分组加总（Line item 合计 emp=169,956.4 千、openings=18,861.6 千、change=5,210.7 千，与官方 Total 行 169,956.1/18,863.3/5,211.8 仅差四舍五入，说明无覆盖缺口）。年均 openings 占比复算：High school 6,753.6=35.81%；No formal credential 6,287.8=33.34%；Bachelor's 3,333.5=17.67%；Postsecondary nondegree 1,161.1=6.16%；Some college 440.2=2.33%；Associate's 332.3=1.76%；Master's 316.8=1.68%；Doctoral/professional 236.3=1.25%——八档逐个与论断一致。三档学位合计 openings 3,886.6 千 = 20.61%（用官方 Total 18,863.3 作分母则 20.60%），2024 就业 50,809.7 千 = 29.90%，十年净增 2,378.5+391.7+255.0 = 3,025.2 千 = 58.06%（用官方 5,211.8 作分母则 58.05%）；Bachelor's 45.65%、Master's 7.52%、Doctoral 4.89% 亦逐个吻合。Some college, no degree = −157.0 千（−3.01%）确为八档中唯一净减少档。机制论断成立且量级可观：按分组加权的 total occupational separations rate 为 Doctoral 4.67% < Master's 7.25% < Bachelor's 7.29% < Associate's 9.10% < Postsecondary nondegree 10.52% < Some college 10.83% < High school 10.96% < No formal credential 15.47%。指派字段定义逐字见 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/nem-definitions.htm：'Typical education needed for entry. This category best describes the typical level of education that most workers need to enter the occupation. Occupations are assigned one of the following eight education levels…'，确为 BLS 指派而非在岗者学历分布；BLS 另在 MLR《Employment trends by typical entry-level education requirement》(2017) 明说该字段与劳动者实际受教育程度是不同概念。旁证：BLS Career Outlook 自述该档职业数为 No formal credential 109 个、High school 326 个，与我的分组计数逐个吻合。唯一可加的口径注脚（不构成修正）：58.0% 的分母是净增量（已扣掉 Some college 的 −157.0），若改用「仅正增长之和 5,367.7 千」作分母，三档学位占 56.4%。


#### [G9] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:归因口径需收紧。原表述：'同口径年化：465 万/年 → 1,898 万/年，方法变更导致约 4.1 倍跳升' → 正确表述：'两版水平比为 4.08 倍（18,981.5 ÷ 4,650.69）；但两版基期不同（2014 vs 2016），全职业就业从 150,539.9 千升至 156,063.8 千（+3.7%）。若换成占就业比的口径，旧法 3.09%/年、新法 12.16%/年，可归因于方法变更的跳升约为 3.9 倍。'另外原文把 BLS 自我判决那段标为「逐字」——它确实与 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/replacements.htm 一字不差，但同一段在 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations.htm 上前面多一句 'Before then,'，引用时请标 replacements.htm 为出处。

**核验依据**:从 Wayback 下载 https://www.bls.gov/emp/projections-archive/2014-24.zip（快照 20240926224409，9,823,242 字节）与 2016-26.zip（快照 20240927022127，9,989,732 字节），解压其中 occupation.xlsx 逐格核对。2014-24 版 Table 1.2 第 2 行末列表头逐字为 'Job openings due to growth and replacements, 2014-24'（无 annual average 副标题，为十年合计），'Total, all occupations' 行 = 150539.9 / 160328.8 / 100 / 100 / 9788.9 / 6.5 / 46506.9。2016-26 版对应表头逐字为 'Occupational openings, 2016-26 annual average'，Total 行 = 156063.8 / 167582.3 / 100 / 100 / 11518.6 / 7.4 / 18981.5。年化与倍数复算：46,506.9/10 = 4,650.69 千/年（≈465 万/年，论断正确）；18,981.5/4,650.69 = 4.081（论断的「约 4.1 倍」在水平口径上正确）；占就业比口径 3.089% vs 12.163% → 3.94 倍。BLS 自我判决逐字命中 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/replacements.htm 正文首段：'BLS used a cohort-component method for estimating job openings due to replacement needs from the 1991 through the 2014–24 projections. This method is no longer in use because BLS identified statistical and conceptual issues with the implementation of this method that compromised the accuracy and validity of the resulting estimates.' 关于「第三方是否质疑新 separations 法」：我检索到的第三方（Chmura Economics 2018 博客、Minnesota DEED《New Separations Methodology》2018、Arkansas/Montana LMI）均只作中性描述，并独立佐证「这类比率平均涨了四倍以上」，未见对新方法的统计学质疑；亦未检索到任何针对 2016-26 之后 separations/openings 方法的独立量化评估。


#### [G-extra] REFUTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(a) 原表述：'公开文献中基本不存在非 BLS 作者对 BLS 职业预测准确度的系统性量化评估' → 事实错误，至少有两项反例。正确表述：'独立量化评估的文献很薄且陈旧，但确实存在：Bishop & Carter, Educational Evaluation and Policy Analysis 13(3), 1991, pp.221–246（康奈尔 ILR，非 BLS 作者，量化指出 BLS 系统性低估技能型职业增长）；以及 H.O. Stekler & Rupin Thomas, Monthly Labor Review, July 2005, pp.46–56（乔治华盛顿大学经济系，两位作者均非 BLS 雇员），该文对 BLS 1988–2000 职业预测计算了 MAPE（9 大类未加权 5.86%、加权 5.29%，naïve 模型分别为 13.8%/11.8%；338 个细分职业未加权 45.2%、加权 15.0%）、dissimilarity index 与 Spearman 秩相关，并以 naïve 外推模型作基准，还明确批评 BLS 自评「缺少比较基准」。可安全承重的说法是：**未检索到任何 2005 年之后的独立系统性量化评估，也未检索到对 2016-26 起新 separations/openings 方法的任何独立评估**。' (b) 原表述：'该 WP 有已发表版（ILR Review 68(2), 2015），优先引用过审版' → 正确表述：'过审版为 Peter Cappelli, "Skill Gaps, Skill Shortages, and Skill Mismatches: Evidence and Arguments for the United States", ILR Review 68(2), March 2015, pp.251–290, DOI 10.1177/0019793914564961，但该文封闭获取、OpenAlex 显示无任何开放仓储全文，我无法核实该句是否保留于过审版、更无法给出页码。因此该句只能引 NBER WP 20382（2014 年 8 月），出处为工作论文正文第 49 页（PDF 第 51 页），并应注明「工作论文版」。'

**核验依据**:(a) 反例一手核对：Stekler & Thomas 全文 PDF https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2005/07/art5full.pdf（Wayback 20260613045502，11 页）抬头逐字：'H.O. Stekler is a research professor in—and Rupin Thomas, a graduate of—the Department of Economics, George Washington University'，摘要逐字：'in most cases, the accuracy of BLS projections were comparable to estimates from naïve extrapolated models'；正文含上述 MAPE 表与「338 disaggregated occupations 未加权 MAPE 45.2、加权 15.0」。反例二 Bishop & Carter 元数据经 OpenAlex 核实（DOI 10.3102/01623737013003221）：Educational Evaluation and Policy Analysis, 1991, 13(3), 221–246。(c) BLS 自陈逐字命中 https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2012-2022-occupational.htm（Wayback 20260613002016）：'An important way to evaluate any projection is to compare it against other, similar projections. This is not possible for occupational projections because there are no comparable projections which are not in some way derived from BLS projections.'——该句本身成立，且与反例不冲突（它说的是「没有可比的独立预测」，不是「没有独立的准确度评估」）。(b) Cappelli 引文逐字核对 NBER 原始 PDF https://www.nber.org/system/files/working_papers/w20382/w20382.pdf（1,097,634 字节，67 页，抬头 'NBER WORKING PAPER SERIES / Working Paper 20382 / NATIONAL BUREAU OF ECONOMIC RESEARCH / August 2014'），第 49 页（PDF 第 51 页）原文：'Governments do not have a particularly good record of forecasting where jobs will be years in advance, however, and students and their families would bear the costs when those forecasts are wrong.'——一字不差。过审版核实路径已穷尽：OpenAlex works API 返回 is_oa=false、oa_status='closed'、any_repository_has_fulltext=false、无 pdf_url；SemanticScholar 的候选 PDF 实为同名演讲幻灯片（26 页）而非期刊正文；CRS R47059 只引书目不引该句。故过审版逐字核实判为不可得。


### 第 3 席


#### [G6] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:回到一手文件复核。www.bls.gov 对自动抓取返 403,改走两条独立路径:(a) Wayback 原始快照 http://web.archive.org/web/20260612175342id_/https://www.bls.gov/emp/ind-occ-matrix/occupation.xlsx(407,365 bytes,真 xlsx);(b) 官方交互数据库 https://data.bls.gov/projections/occupationProj(未被拦截,服务端渲染)。两路结果一致。Table 1.10 第 3 行 'Total, all occupations' 逐格:Employment 2024=169956.1;2034=175167.9;change=5211.8(3.1%);Labor force exits=8155.0;Occupational transfers=10187.1;Total separations=18342.1;Occupational openings=18863.3。恒等式复算:18342.1+5211.8/10=18863.28,四舍五入 18863.3 ✓。占比复算:18342.1/18863.3=97.236%→97.2% ✓;521.18/18863.3=2.764%→2.8% ✓。单位换算 18863.3 千=1886.33万、18342.1 千=1834.21万、521.18 千=52.118万 全部正确。定义句在 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations.htm 逐字命中:'This estimate of openings does not count workers who change jobs but remain in the same occupation.' JOLTS 与 EP openings 确为两个构念,https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/separations-faqs.htm 逐字:'The JOLTS job openings metric is a stock measure of the number of job openings, or positions that are open, not filled, on the last business day of a month. In contrast, occupational openings are projections of the number of positions that are created and filled on an annual basis.'——论断称后者为'某时点空缺存量'准确。一处值得写进文章的限定(不改判):同一 FAQ 另说 'For declining occupations, not all workers who separate need to be replaced',所以严格讲 18342.1 是'分离量等于 openings 的 97.2%',而非'97.2% 的 openings 是替补岗位';衰退职业的负增长项已在总量里相抵。BLS 自己在 Career Outlook 亦作同样口径表述('Most openings come from the need to replace workers who leave permanently')。


#### [G7] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:两处需改。(1) 萎缩职业清单漏了一个。原表述:'sep 占比 >100% ... (retail salespersons、cashiers、customer service reps、office clerks、secretaries 均净减少)'——前 15 名中 sep 占比 >100% 的实为 6 个,清单漏掉 Waiters and waitresses(100.4%,2024–34 净变化 −16.3 千,年均 −1.63 千)。修正后:'retail salespersons、cashiers、waiters and waitresses、customer service reps、office clerks、secretaries 六个均净减少'。(2) BLS Career Outlook 引语的适用范围被放大。原表述把 'Each of the occupations in chart 1, like most at this education level, had wages below the median for all occupations.' 用作 BLS 对 openings 榜整体低薪的自我承认;但该文(Elka Torpey,2025 年 9 月)的 chart 1 只是 'No formal educational credential' 这一学历档内 openings 最多的职业,'this education level' 指的就是该档,不是全职业 openings 前 15 名。修正后应写:'BLS 自己在讲 no-formal-credential 这一档时承认:该档 openings 最多的职业几乎都低于全职业中位薪。'——不能当成对整个 openings 榜的承认。

**核验依据**:一手核对(occupation.xlsx Table 1.2/1.10,Wayback 原始快照;并与 data.bls.gov/projections/occupationProj 交叉验证)。基准值:Table 1.2 第 3 行 'Median annual wage, dollars, 2024' Total, all occupations = 49,500(脚注[1] 注明来自 OEWS,只覆盖 wage and salary workers,不含自雇——与 openings 的全岗位口径略有错配,属标准做法)。15 行逐格全部对上、排序也对:Fast food 904.3/30,480/97.4%/净+23.32千每年;Home health 765.8/34,900/90.3%;Retail sales 555.8/34,580/100.4%;Cashiers 542.6/31,190/105.8%;Stockers 472.3/37,090/95.0%;Waiters 456.7/33,760/100.4%;Laborers 384.3/38,940/98.9%;Janitors 351.3/35,930/98.6%;Customer service 341.7/42,830/104.5%;General & operations managers 308.7/102,950/94.7%;Office clerks 282.4/43,630/106.3%;Cooks restaurant 250.7/36,830/91.3%;Heavy truck drivers 237.6/57,440/96.3%;Nursing assistants 204.1/39,530/98.4%;Secretaries 202.8/46,290/101.5%。'13 个低于中位数'成立:15 个中仅 General & operations managers(102,950)与 Heavy truck drivers(57,440)高于 49,500。'>100% 即萎缩'这个推论我特意去证伪,结果它是严格恒等而非近似:现行方法下 openings = separations + 年均净变化,无下限截断(实证:Cashiers 574.0−31.36=542.64→542.6;Retail 557.8−1.96=555.84→555.8),故 sep/openings>1 ⟺ 净变化<0,充要。引语出处与逐字:http://web.archive.org/web/20251121173416id_/https://www.bls.gov/careeroutlook/2025/article/education-level-and-openings-2024-34.htm,该文明确列出五个学历档、chart 1 对应 'No formal educational credential'。


#### [G8] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:三组自算数字全部独立复算通过。方法:取 occupation.xlsx Table 1.2 的 832 条 'Line item'(排除 Summary 行以免重复计数),按第 13 列 'Typical education needed for entry' 分组加总。年均 openings 占比复算:High school 6753.6=35.81%;No formal credential 6287.8=33.34%;Bachelor's 3333.5=17.67%;Postsecondary nondegree 1161.1=6.16%;Some college 440.2=2.33%;Associate's 332.3=1.76%;Master's 316.8=1.68%;Doctoral/professional 236.3=1.25%——与论断的 35.8/33.3/17.7/6.2/2.3/1.8/1.7/1.3 及各绝对值全部一致。BA+MA+Doc openings=3886.6→20.61%(用已公布总量 18863.3 作分母则 20.60%),均入 20.6% ✓。2024 就业:42469.6+3828.8+4511.3=50809.7→29.90% ✓。十年净增:Bachelor's 2378.5(45.65%)+Master's 391.7(7.52%)+Doctoral 255.0(4.89%)=3025.2→58.06%(用 5211.8 作分母 58.04%),均入 58.0% ✓。'Some college 唯一净减少档' ✓:−157.0,占比 −3.0%,八档中唯一为负。机制论断我用 Table 1.10 与 1.2 连接后按学历档实算分离率验证,成立且差距很大:Total separations rate(年均)Bachelor's 7.29%、Master's 7.25%、Doctoral 4.67%,对比 No formal credential 15.47%、High school 10.96%、Some college 10.83%——学位档 openings 相对少确实源于低分离率。口径限定逐字核对通过,https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/nem-definitions.htm:'The Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) education and training classification system consists of three categories of information that BLS analysts have assigned to each detailed occupation.' 及 'Typical education needed for entry. This category best describes the typical level of education that most workers need to enter the occupation.'——确为 BLS 指派字段,非在岗者实际学历分布。给写作方的一句提醒(不构成事实错误):'openings 少=稳定'只是一半;同一组数字也意味着学位类职业每年可进入的口子相对其存量更窄,对求职者是双刃的,别只讲一面。


#### [G9] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:两个 vintage 的 Excel 我都拿到原件并逐格核。www.bls.gov 直连 403,blsmon1 镜像无归档 zip,最终经 Wayback 原始快照取得:2014-24.zip(http://web.archive.org/web/20260613002005id_/https://www.bls.gov/emp/projections-archive/2014-24.zip,9,823,242 bytes)与 2016-26.zip(.../20260613002042id_/...,9,989,732 bytes),解包内含各自 occupation.xlsx。2014-24 Table 1.2 第 2 行第 10 列表头逐字 'Job openings due to growth and replacements, 2014-24',第 5 行 Total, all occupations = 46506.9(同表 Employment 2014=150539.9、2024=160328.8、change=9788.9)✓。2016-26 Table 1.2 表头逐字 'Occupational openings, 2016-26 annual average',第 5 行 Total = 18981.5 ✓(注:某 NSF 补充表流传的 18,742.0 与一手不符,以 BLS 归档件为准)。年化比复算:46506.9/10=4650.69;18981.5/4650.69=4.082→约 4.1 倍 ✓。若直接并列 46506.9 与 18981.5 会读成 −59%,即论断所说的'腰斩'错觉 ✓。BLS 自我判决逐字命中,出处是 https://www.bls.gov/emp/documentation/replacements.htm(不是 separations FAQ——后者措辞不同,写成 'BLS used a cohort–component method for estimating openings due to replacement needs from 1991 through the 2014–24 projections. BLS identified ... and therefore developed the current separations method');replacements.htm 上一字不差:'BLS used a cohort-component method for estimating job openings due to replacement needs from the 1991 through the 2014–24 projections. This method is no longer in use because BLS identified statistical and conceptual issues with the implementation of this method that compromised the accuracy and validity of the resulting estimates.' 建议引用时标这个 URL。另有更硬的一句可用,来自 separations-faqs.htm:'Published estimates from the 2014–24 projections and prior years should not be compared with the 2016–26 projections to estimate how the number of openings has changed over time.' 我另外挖到论断没提的第三重不可比、且是量化验证过的:旧法对衰退职业把增长截断为零(openings=replacements+max(growth,0)),新法不截断(openings=separations+growth,可为负)。实证:2014-24 表中 Chief executives chg=−4.1、repl=58.4、openings=58.4(未减);全表 Σ(repl+max(chg,0))=46506.4≈已公布 46506.9,而 Σrepl+Σchg 只有 45114.9。这恰恰说明 G7 的'>100% 即萎缩'只在新法下成立。两点含糊需在文中说清但不改判决:(1) 4.1 倍是两个不同期次(2014-24 vs 2016-26)、不同就业基数(150539.9 vs 156063.8)的比值,按分离率归一后方法效应约 3.9 倍,不宜说成纯方法效应;(2) 旧法 23.5% 是十年累计替换率,新法 11.0% 是年均分离率,周期性也不同。


#### [G-extra] REFUTED · confidence=high

**修正**:原表述:'公开文献中基本不存在非 BLS 作者对 BLS 职业预测准确度的系统性量化评估;唯一找到的独立学者批评是 Peter Cappelli(NBER WP 20382, 2014)。' → 修正后:此说不成立,至少存在两项非 BLS 作者的系统性量化评估,且都不是泛泛批评而是带误差指标的正式评估。(a) H.O. Stekler 与 Rupin Thomas(均为 George Washington University 经济系,非 BLS),《Evaluating BLS labor force, employment, and occupation projections for 2000》,Monthly Labor Review,2005 年 7 月,第 46–56 页。(b) John H. Bishop 与 Shani Carter(Cornell ILR,非 BLS),《How accurate are recent BLS occupational projections?》,Monthly Labor Review,1991 年 10 月;同二人另有《The Worsening Shortage of College-Graduate Workers》,Educational Evaluation and Policy Analysis 13(3), 1991。两项均刊于 BLS 自家的 MLR,但作者是外部学者,满足'非 BLS 作者'。此外论断把两件事混为一谈了:BLS 那句自陈说的是'不存在可与之对比的独立预测(projections)',不是'不存在独立评估(evaluations)'——前者成立,后者不成立,写作时必须拆开。

**核验依据**:(a) 我下载了 Stekler & Thomas 全文 PDF(Wayback 原始快照 http://web.archive.org/web/20260613045502id_/https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2005/07/art5full.pdf,11 页)并读了正文。作者栏逐字:'H.O. Stekler is a research professor in—and Rupin Thomas, a graduate of—the Department of Economics, George Washington University.' 其占用了整节做职业预测的量化评估:对 9 个 major occupational groups 和 338 个细类分别算 MAPE(细类未加权 MAPE=45.2%,加权 MAPE=15.0%)、算 dissimilarity indexes 做结构误差分解、用两个 naïve share 模型做基准、并给出预测/实际增长率的五分位转移表(Table 7)。这就是论断说'基本不存在'的那种东西。(b) Bishop & Carter 的 MLR 1991-10 篇经 Gale Academic OneFile 与 Cornell eCommons(https://ecommons.cornell.edu/items/3afb9f5d-9a38-47be-8454-be64c770565f)确认作者与出处;其量化结论为 BLS 1980 年代方法变更后系统性低估高技能职业增长(BLS projected 专业/技术/管理岗占 1988–2000 就业增长的 44.5%,该二人估 70%,1988-03 至 1991-03 实际为 87%)。(c) BLS 自陈句逐字命中,在多个 vintage 的职业预测评估页上都有,例:https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2002-2012-occupational.htm 与 https://www.bls.gov/emp/evaluations/2006-2016-occupational.htm——'An important way to evaluate any projection is to compare it against other, similar projections. This is not possible for occupational projections because there are no comparable projections which are not in some way derived from BLS projections.' 有意思的是 Stekler 独立佐证了这一点:'no other organization made projections of these variables. Consequently, there is no benchmark for judging the BLS forecasts.' 但这恰恰是他仍然完成了评估(改用 naïve 基准)的那篇文章里说的。(d) Cappelli 那句我在 NBER WP 20382 原件核对通过(https://www.nber.org/system/files/working_papers/w20382/w20382.pdf,手稿第 49 页/PDF 第 51 页,逐字:'Governments do not have a particularly good record of forecasting where jobs will be years in advance, however, and students and their families would bear the costs when those forecasts are wrong.')。已发表版(ILR Review 68(2):251–290, 2015,题名改为 'Skill Gaps, Skill Shortages, and Skill Mismatches: Evidence and Arguments for the United States')有付费墙:SAGE 全文与 PDF 均返 403,Wharton 站点该 PDF 404,semanticscholar 上那份是同名演讲幻灯不是正文,故'该句是否保留、页码为何'这一子项我取不到、判为不可核实——但它不影响本条总判决。


## 批次 B1 — verify:NYFed分专业


### 第 1 席


#### [G10] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:数字与排名部分逐格核对全部正确,无需改动;需要修正的是三处口径来源与限定语。
(1)underemployment 那句 50% 判定原文的**位置**:原表述「underemployment 判定(逐字)…判定依据来自 O*NET」被挂在「NY Fed 分专业表」名下 → 正确表述:该句逐字出现在交互页 **Underemployment 标签页**的 notes(其 source 行为「Current Population Survey (IPUMS); U.S. Department of Labor, O*NET」),而 **Outcomes by Major 标签页的 notes 根本没有定义 underemployment,其 source 行只写「U.S. Census Bureau, American Community Survey (IPUMS)」,不提 O*NET**。O*NET 适用于分专业表这一点,只能靠页面 FAQ(「This analysis uses survey data from the U.S. Department of Labor's Occupational Information Network (O*NET) Education and Training Questionnaire…」)来搭桥,不是分专业表自己的注释。写文章时不能说「该表注明依据 O*NET」。
(2)阈值措辞不一致:网站写「50 percent or more」,而 NY Fed 自己指定的方法论原文(Abel-Deitz-Su, NBER WP 22654 / NY Fed Staff Report 749,第 6 页)写的是「**more than 50 percent** of the respondents working in that occupation indicated that at least a bachelor's degree was necessary」。边界口径(等于 50% 算不算)两处相反,引用时应注明以网站版为准。
(3)缺一条关键限定语:原表述的「定义(逐字)」清单里没有说明中位工资是否折算不变价 → 正确表述:Outcomes by Major 标签页的 notes **完全没有 constant-dollar 声明**,而同一交互的 Wages 标签页明写「Annual wages are expressed in constant 2025 dollars」。可判定分专业表的 $ 数是**当年名义美元**,不可跨年份直接比较(证据:2022 数据版 Overall 早期中位薪 $50,000,若折成 2025 年美元应约 $55,000;而 CPS 口径的 Wages 表 2022 年不变价中位数是 $57,216)。
其余全部 HOLDS:发布日 2026-02-04、基于 2024 ACS(非 CPS)、73 个专业(CSV 共 74 行数据,其中 1 行为 Overall,排名计算不含 Overall)、22–27 岁 bachelor's or higher、中位工资 bachelor's degree only、Mid-career 35–45、exclude those currently enrolled——四句定义均与 college-labor-chart-meta.json 逐字一致。9 个专业 + Overall 的 4 个数字共 40 格全部与 CSV 完全吻合(Elementary Education CSV 记作 1.18/Biology 51.13/Fine Arts 58.87,与论断的 1.180/51.130/58.870 同值)。分母为**在业**毕业生已由 NBER WP 22654 第 6 页原文确证:「We measure the underemployment rate as the share of **employed** college graduates working in jobs that do not require a college degree.」排名复算:CS 失业率 6.992 在 73 专业中升序第 70 位 / 降序第 4 位(高于它的只有 Anthropology 7.922、Computer Engineering 7.783、Fine Arts 7.655),低就业率升序第 9 位,早期中位薪降序第 2 位(仅次于 CE $90,000);Nursing 低就业率 12.781 为 73 个专业最低(1/73)。

**核验依据**:一手 CSV(2026-07-24 直连下载,HTTP 200,3894 字节,75 行):https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-outcomes-by-major-data.csv —— 关键行原文:Computer Engineering,7.783,15.835,90000,131000,39.432 / Computer Science,6.992,19.127,87000,120000,32.691 / Nursing,2.147,12.781,70000,87000,30.292 / Elementary Education,1.18,16.213,45000,55000,50.596 / Biology,4.292,51.13,45000,83000,63.983 / Psychology,4.985,48.291,45000,72000,51.864 / Fine Arts,7.655,58.87,45000,72000,22.475 / Economics,3.524,33.094,72000,115000,41.595 / Finance,2.758,27.758,70000,112000,31.483 / Overall,4.211,39.35,58000,87000,39.347。
定义 JSON:https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-chart-meta.json —— "--#explore#outcomes-by-major": {"releaseDateInfo": "February 4, 2026, based on data from 2024", "source": "Source: U.S. Census Bureau, American Community Survey (IPUMS).", "notes": "Notes: Figures are for 2024. Unemployment and underemployment rates are for recent college graduates (that is, those aged 22 to 27 with a bachelor's degree or higher), and median wages are for full-time workers with a bachelor's degree only. Early career graduates are those aged 22 to 27, and mid-career graduates are those aged 35 to 45. … All figures exclude those currently enrolled in school."}(此段无 O*NET、无 constant dollars)。50% 判定句实出于同文件 "--#explore#underemployment" 节点。
FAQ / O*NET 说明与「分母为在业」的原始出处:https://www.newyorkfed.org/research/college-labor-market(FAQ 全文)与 https://www.nber.org/system/files/working_papers/w22654/w22654.pdf(pdftotext 第 350 行原句)。Wages 标签页不变价声明与 https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-wages-data.csv(2022 年 57216、2024 年 61584,均为 constant 2025 dollars)构成名义/实际对照。排名由本地 Python 对 73 行(剔除 Overall)复算。


#### [G11] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:四个版本 35 个数字逐格核对全部正确,发布日与数据年份也全部由各版 meta.json 的 releaseDateInfo 确证。需要修正的是三处解读口径。
(1)起薪涨幅口径:原表述「起薪同期从 $78k 涨到 $87k(+11.5%)」→ 正确表述:「**名义**起薪从 $78,000(2022 ACS)涨到 $87,000(2024 ACS),名义 +11.5%;分专业表不做不变价折算(与同一交互的 Wages 标签页不同,其 notes 无 constant-dollar 声明),扣除同期 CPI-U 年均值 292.655→313.689(+7.19%)后**实际涨幅约 +4.0%**」。对准大学生做专业选择,+11.5% 与 +4.0% 是两个不同量级的信号,必须标注是名义值。
(2)「低就业率 2022→2023 还在下降」→ 正确表述:「2022→2023 低就业率为 16.654%→16.456%,**下降 0.20pp,实质上是持平**,远小于该表单年跨版本变动的中位绝对值(1.25pp),不足以支撑「还在下降」这种带方向性的措辞」。
(3)漏了一条会改变结论的对照:CS 低就业率 2021 版为 19.1%,2024 版为 19.127% —— 即 **2024 年的 19.1% 不是新高,而是回到 2021 年的水平**,中间的 16.7%/16.5% 才是低谷。原表述「三个指标不同向」成立,但若只呈现 2022→2024 的上升,会让读者误以为低就业率在恶化到史无前例;实际是「先降后回」。
其余 HOLDS:2023-02-10 版(2021 ACS)CS 4.8/19.1/$73,000、CE 3.7、Overall 5.1;2024-02-22 版(2022 ACS)CS 4.267/16.654/$78,000、CE 2.311、Overall 3.557;2025-02-20 版(2023 ACS)CS 6.056/16.456/$80,000、CE 7.538、Overall 3.640;2026-02-04 版(2024 ACS)CS 6.992/19.127/$87,000、CE 7.783、Overall 4.211 —— 全部逐格吻合(2023 版原始文件确实只给 1 位小数)。「CS 失业率 2 年内 +2.7pp」= 6.992−4.267 = 2.725pp,正确。四版口径均为 ACS、22–27 岁、bachelor's or higher、排除在校生,已由四份 meta.json 的 outcomes-by-major notes 逐字确认一致,时序可比性成立。

#### [G12] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:传播链、数字对应、以及三级回溯修订全部核实无误,但报道本身的描述需要四处修正。
(1)标题:原表述「CNBC 2025-05-16《College majors with the best and worst job prospects — art history beats finance》」→ 正确表述:「该文 2025-05-16 首发、**2025-06-05 更新**;更新后 CNBC 现行标题为《College majors with the best and worst **employment** prospects — **philosophy now outranks finance**》,『art history beats finance』是**首发/授权转载版**保留的旧标题(NBC4 页面 <title> 与 h1 仍是旧标题,正文内嵌的 [CNBC] 署名行已是新标题)」。引用时必须说清用的是哪一版标题,否则读者按新标题搜不到。
(2)必须补上的关键事实:**该文文末带更正声明**——「Correction: This story has been updated to correct the data in a chart.」即首发版图表数据是错的、事后被订正。论断说「CNBC 引用的数字逐个回对 2025-02-20 版 CSV 完全吻合」——这对**现行订正后版本的正文数字**成立(0.4/0.441、3/3.047、3.2/3.180、3.7/3.704、6.1/6.056、7.5/7.538、1.4/1.422、$80,000 逐个吻合),但**不能推及首发版的图表**。「病毒式传播」传的恰恰主要是那张图。
(3)「口径事故」的指控要收窄:原表述暗示 CNBC 未交代分专业数据来源 → 正确表述:「CNBC **明确写了**『The New York Fed's report was based on Census data from 2023』;真正未标注口径的是那句 5.8%——文中只写『according to the Federal Reserve Bank of New York』,未说明它来自 CPS 月度、季调+三月移动平均、且是**全专业合计**。两套口径在版面上相邻但只有一套被标注,这才是事故的准确描述。」
(4)修订链要补一条:原表述只追了 5.8%,但**同句的对照值 4.6% 同样被修订过**——CNBC 引用时(2025-05 版数据)2024-03 恰为 4.6(整数级巧合),现行版已改为 4.521。即那句话的两个数字如今都不再成立,「5.8% vs 4.6%」当前应为「5.672% vs 4.521%」,同比升幅从 +1.2pp 缩到 +1.15pp。
其余 HOLDS:6.056(2023 ACS,2025-02-20 发布)已被 6.992(2024 ACS,2026-02-04 发布)取代,截至 2026-07 「CS 应届失业率 6.1%」确已过期两年;CE 7.5%→7.783% 同理。5.8%/5.710%/5.672% 三个值全部命中,序列确实做季调+三月移动平均并回溯修订。
另需给文章加一条数据质量警示(论断未提):NY Fed 该页 2025-10-31 曾挂出停更公告「Due to the suspension of necessary data…」,且现行 Unemployment 注释写明「**October 2025 results are estimated due to missing data**」——CPS 月度序列 2025-10 之后的值含估算成分。

**核验依据**:报道原文(CNBC 直连 403,用 NBC Washington 授权转载版,HTTP 200,81341 字节):https://www.nbcwashington.com/news/business/money-report/college-majors-with-the-best-and-worst-employment-prospects-philosophy-now-outranks-finance/3915293/ —— 抓取到的原句:「For computer science and computer engineering, the unemployment rate in those fields was 6.1% and 7.5%, respectively」「the unemployment rate for art history majors was 3%, and for nutritional sciences, the unemployment rate was just 0.4%… The New York Fed's report was based on Census data from 2023」「Philosophy majors have an unemployment rate of 3.2%, for example, and for finance, it's 3.7%」「median wages of $80,000」「The unemployment rate among nursing majors is just 1.4%」「…for recent college grads rose to 5.8% in March, up from 4.6% the same time a year ago, according to the [Federal Reserve Bank of New York]」「**Correction: This story has been updated to correct the data in a chart.**」;页面同时含 h1「…job prospects — art history beats finance」与内嵌「[CNBC] College majors with the best and worst employment prospects — philosophy now outranks finance」;Published May 16, 2025 • Updated on June 5, 2025。

#### [G13] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:论断 B 全部复算命中,论断 A 引语与数字命中,论断 C 在我能穷尽的一手渠道内成立。需要修正四处。
(1)【A,最重要】引语被去掉了限定词。原表述:「逐字:『the confidence intervals around these estimates of recent grad unemployment are so large that it makes no sense to use them to make firm conclusions about the returns to particular majors.』」→ 正确表述:原句为「**In most cases,** the confidence intervals around these estimates of recent grad unemployment are so large that it makes no sense to use them to make firm conclusions about the returns to particular majors, **let alone use them to make policy decisions.**」去掉句首「In most cases」会把作者的「多数情况下」升格为「一律」,是把不确定性论证反过来说死了——恰恰犯了作者批评的毛病。
(2)【A】EPOP 那个 90% 的口径不是 NY Fed 的 Computer Science 行。原表述「Computer and Information Sciences 22–27 岁 2023 年 EPOP 为 90%」→ 正确表述:「EIG 是**为了扩大样本而改用 ACS 更粗的『computer and information sciences』大类(作者原文说该类下辖六个专业)**才算出 90%,与 NY Fed 表中单列的 Computer Science 不同口径,两者不能并排。作者同时写明该值『**down more than one percent from 2022**』。」
(3)【A】必须标注 EIG 文章的时效边界:发表于 **2025-08-13**,当时可用的最新数据只到 2023 ACS(作者原文:『the American Community Survey, which only extends through 2023』),并且作者自己写下『it is likely that the job prospects for young graduates of computer and information science programs have worsened since the last ACS』。2026-02-04 发布的 2024 ACS 已把 CS 抬到 6.992%、CE 抬到 7.783%。因此那句「greatly exaggerated, at least for now」的『for now』**已经到期**,不能当作 2026 年的现成反驳。另 EIG 是政策倡导型智库(Economic Innovation Group),作者 Connor O'Brien 为其研究人员——论断已提示,确认属实。
(4)【B 的推论】「CS/CE 的高失业率稳定复现」措辞过强。原表述 → 正确表述:「在 2023 ACS 与 2024 ACS **两个连续版本**之间,CS/CE 的高位是复现的(CS 6.056→6.992,+0.94pp;CE 7.538→7.783,+0.25pp);但把窗口拉到四版,**CE 在 2022 ACS 版本上只有 2.311%**(2021 版 3.7%),即 CE 的『高失业率』本身也是一次跨版本大跳变的产物,只稳了两年。用『稳定复现』替准大学生下结论,与本条自己论证的『单年 ACS 噪声极大』互相矛盾。」
(5)【C】边界说明:「NY Fed 从未公布该表的样本量或标准误」——我把能查的一手渠道走遍了(交互页正文与全部 FAQ 条目、四份数据 CSV 的表头、college-labor-chart-meta.json 的全部 notes、页面提供的历史数据下载 College-labor-data_historical.xlsx 的全部 sharedStrings),**均无任何 N、SE、MOE、置信区间字样**,分专业 CSV 只有 6 列;方法论指定的两篇论文(NBER WP 22654 / Staff Report 749、Current Issues 20-1)讲的是 CPS/ACS 的判定方法,不提供该表的分专业样本量。故本条在我可核范围内成立,但「从未」是无界否定命题,建议文章改写为「NY Fed 在该交互页、数据文件、FAQ 及其指定方法论文献中均未提供该表的样本量或标准误」。
以下 HOLDS 且逐格复算命中:CE 点估计 7.5% → 「between four percent and 11 percent」;Physics 「between two percent and 12 percent」;Public Policy 「from zero percent to 13 percent」。论断 B 的 12 组跳变全部与两版 CSV 精确吻合,且恰好就是按绝对变化排序的前 12 名;73 专业(剔除 Overall)失业率变化**中位绝对值 = 1.2520pp**(论断称 1.25),**绝对变化 >2pp 者恰为 20 个**(无恰好等于 2 的边界案例)。

**核验依据**:EIG 原文(直连 HTTP 200,39937 字节,逐行提取):https://agglomerations.eig.org/p/a-viral-chart-on-recent-graduate —— datePublished 元数据 "2025-08-13T10:30:58+00:00",署名 Connor O'Brien;原句「We find that we can say with 95 percent confidence that the unemployment rate for new computer engineering graduates is somewhere between four percent and 11 percent.」「For recent physics majors, we are 95 percent certain that the unemployment rate is somewhere between two percent and 12 percent. For public policy majors, from zero percent to 13 percent.」「In most cases, the confidence intervals around these estimates of recent grad unemployment are so large that it makes no sense to use them to make firm conclusions about the returns to particular majors, let alone use them to make policy decisions.」「Using broader categories of degree fields (thus a larger sample)… The ACS buckets six majors under the category…」「A full 90 percent of 22- to 27-year-olds who majored in those fields were employed in 2023. That is down more than one percent from 2022…」「Unlike the American Community Survey, which only extends through 2023…」「Still, news of the much-anticipated implosion of the computer science graduate job market is greatly exaggerated, at least for now.」
跨版本复算:2023 ACS 版 https://web.archive.org/web/20250416141515id_/https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-outcomes-by-major-data.csv 对 2024 ACS 版(直连线上,同路径)。专业名集合两版完全相同(对称差为空),73 项。本地 Python 复算:median |Δ| = 1.2520pp;|Δ|>2pp 计 20 个。按 |Δ| 降序前 12:Early Childhood Education 1.298→6.593(+5.295)、Performing Arts 2.718→6.950(+4.232)、Nutrition Sciences 0.441→4.536(+4.095)、Environmental Studies 2.582→6.307(+3.725)、Art History 3.047→6.688(+3.641)、Medical Technicians 2.823→6.236(+3.413)、Public Policy and Law 5.513→2.237(−3.276)、Mechanical Engineering 1.529→4.364(+2.835)、Chemical Engineering 2.024→4.711(+2.687)、Business Analytics 2.392→5.034(+2.642)、Architecture 4.284→6.844(+2.560)、Foreign Language 4.040→1.579(−2.461);CS +0.936、CE +0.245。第 13–20 名(论断未列,不影响结论):Pharmacy +2.433、Earth Sciences +2.379、Ethnic Studies +2.291、Sociology −2.140、Advertising and PR +2.134、General Engineering +2.127、Journalism −2.037、Mathematics +2.019。
CE 四版时序(证明「只稳两年」):2021 ACS 3.7 → 2022 ACS 2.311 → 2023 ACS 7.538 → 2024 ACS 7.783,取自上述四份 id_ 快照/线上文件。
