**核验依据**:下载当期 CSV(https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-outcomes-by-major-data.csv,本地 /private/tmp/.../scratchpad/cur.csv)。文件 75 行 = 1 表头 + 73 个专业 + 1 行 Overall,'覆盖 73 个专业'正确。逐格核对:Computer Engineering 7.783/15.835/90000/131000;Computer Science 6.992/19.127/87000/120000;Nursing 2.147/12.781/70000/87000;Elementary Education 1.18/16.213/45000/55000;Biology 4.292/51.13/45000/83000;Psychology 4.985/48.291/45000/72000;Fine Arts 7.655/58.87/45000/72000;Economics 3.524/33.094/72000/115000;Finance 2.758/27.758/70000/112000;Overall 4.211/39.35/58000/87000 —— 全部逐字吻合。排名(73 个专业、剔除 Overall)复算:CS 失业率升序第 70 名 = 倒数第 4(Anthropology 7.922 > CE 7.783 > Fine Arts 7.655 > CS 6.992),低就业率升序第 9(前八为 Nursing 12.781、Aerospace 14.709、Civil 15.567、CE 15.835、Special Ed 16.042、Elementary Ed 16.213、ChemE 17.862、Construction Services 17.933),早期中位薪降序第 2(CE 90000 第一),Nursing 低就业率 12.781 确为 1/73 —— 全部成立。定义:college-labor-chart-meta.json 的 '--#explore#outcomes-by-major' 段 releaseDateInfo = 'February 4, 2026, based on data from 2024',source = 'U.S. Census Bureau, American Community Survey (IPUMS)'(确为 ACS 非 CPS),notes 逐字含 'those aged 22 to 27 with a bachelor's degree or higher'、'median wages are for full-time workers with a bachelor's degree only'、'Early career graduates are those aged 22 to 27, and mid-career graduates are those aged 35 to 45'、'All figures exclude those currently enrolled in school'。underemployment 那句 'A job is classified as a college job if 50 percent or more...' 逐字确在站上,但出处是 Underemployment 页(CPS 序列)的 notes,by-major 页 notes 未重述、其 source 行也未列 O*NET;O*NET 依据由页面 FAQ 补上('This analysis uses survey data from the U.S. Department of Labor's Occupational Information Network (O*NET) Education and Training Questionnaire')。'分母是在业毕业生'一句站上并未写明,但 FAQ 指向的方法论原文 Abel/Deitz/Su, 'Are Recent College Graduates Finding Good Jobs?'(https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/media/research/current_issues/ci20-1.pdf)明写:'We calculate the underemployment rate as the number of graduates underemployed divided by total graduates employed.' —— 成立。两处需读者知道的补充(不影响判决):(1) meta.json 顶层 releaseDateInfo 是 'May 5, 2026, with 2026:Q1 data',那是 CPS 两个页签的更新日,by-major 表仍是 2026-02-04;(2) by-major 的薪资列 notes 没有 'constant dollars' 字样(Wages 页签才写 'constant 2025 dollars'),即该表薪资是当年名义美元,跨年份版本比较不可直接相减。


#### [G11] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 「起薪同期从 $78k 涨到 $87k(+11.5%)」→ 「同期」指向错位。紧邻的前半句说的是「低就业率 2022→2023 还在下降」,而 $78k→$87k 跨的是 2022→2024 两个数据年;2022→2023 起薪只从 $78,000 涨到 $80,000(+2.6%)。正确写法:CS 早期中位薪 2022 年 $78,000 → 2023 年 $80,000 → 2024 年 $87,000,两年累计名义 +11.5%。(2) 「+11.5%」是名义值,须加限定语。该表薪资为当年名义美元(by-major 页 notes 无 constant-dollar 字样,与 Wages 页签的 'constant 2025 dollars' 不同);BLS CPI-U 年均 2022=292.655、2024=313.689,期间物价 +7.19%,故 $78,000(2022 元)折 2024 元为 $83,608,实际购买力涨幅仅约 +4.1%。面向选专业的学生,「起薪两年涨 11.5%」必须改成「名义 +11.5%、实际约 +4%」。(3) 「低就业率 2022→2023 还在下降」字面为真但幅度须交代:16.654→16.456,只降 0.198pp,远小于该表跨版本变动的中位数 1.25pp,属噪声级,不宜作为「三个指标不同向」的承重证据;真正的转折在 2023→2024(16.456→19.127,+2.67pp)。

**核验依据**:用 Wayback id_ 原始快照取回四版 CSV(响应为 gzip,须解压后读):web.archive.org/web/20231122172655id_/、20240507092624id_/、20250416141515id_/、20260303160457id_/ + /medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-outcomes-by-major-data.csv。四版均 75 行。CS 行:2023 版 4.8/19.1/73000;2024 版 4.267/16.654/78000;2025 版 6.056/16.456/80000;2026 版 6.992/19.127/87000。CE 失业率:3.7 / 2.311 / 7.538 / 7.783。Overall 失业率:5.1 / 3.557 / 3.640 / 4.211 —— 表中 20 个数字全部逐格吻合。同一批快照的 college-labor-chart-meta.json 的 '--#explore#outcomes-by-major' 段确认发布日与数据年一一对应:February 10, 2023 / 'Figures are for 2021';February 22, 2024 / 'Figures are for 2022';February 20, 2025 / 'Figures are for 2023';February 4, 2026 / 'Figures are for 2024'。四版 notes 中的人群定义(22–27、bachelor's degree or higher、排除在校生)逐字一致,「同口径」成立。失业率 4.267→6.992 = +2.725pp,四舍五入 +2.7pp 成立。CPI 取自 BLS 公开 API 序列 CUUR0000SA0 年均值。


#### [G12] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 「已过期两年」→ 夸大了一个发布周期。精确表述:6.1%(原值 6.056)出自 2026-02-04 之前的上一版,即 NY Fed 2025-02-20 发布、测量 2023 日历年的版本;它只被替换过一次,当期版本是 2026-02-04 发布、测量 2024 日历年的 6.992%。即「落后一个数据年 / 一个发布周期,所描述的是 2023 年的劳动力市场」,而不是「过期两年」。(2) 报道标题需补一句。CNBC 正文现行标题是《College majors with the best and worst employment prospects — philosophy now outranks finance》,且文末带更正声明「Correction: This story has been updated to correct the data in a chart.」,更新时间 2025-06-05;「art history beats finance」是 NBC 转载页/更新前的标题。引用该报道时应注明所核对的是 2025-06-05 更正后的版本。(3) 「该 5.8% 本身被回溯修订过」应写成双向修订:同一句里的同比基数 4.6%(2024 年 3 月)也被修订了,当前值为 4.521%。完整链条:发文时版本 2025 年 3 月 = 5.802、2024 年 3 月 = 4.600;2025-08 存档版 5.710 / 4.542;当前(2026-05-05 发布)版 5.672 / 4.521。

**核验依据**:CNBC 直连 403,改用 NBC Washington 授权转载版(https://www.nbcwashington.com/news/business/money-report/college-majors-with-the-best-and-worst-employment-prospects-philosophy-now-outranks-finance/3915293/),Jessica Dickler / CNBC,Published May 16, 2025,Updated on June 5, 2025。逐字核对文中数字与 2025-02-20 版 CSV(Wayback 20250416141515id_):CS 6.1%↔6.056、CE 7.5%↔7.538、art history 3%↔3.047、nutritional sciences 0.4%↔0.441(NY Fed 标签为 'Nutrition Sciences')、philosophy 3.2%↔3.180、finance 3.7%↔3.704、nursing 1.4%↔1.422、'median wages of $80,000'↔CS/CE 均 80000(该版并列最高，与 Chemical Engineering 同为 80000);文中「based on Census data from 2023」亦与该版 notes 'Figures are for 2023' 吻合。口径事故属实:同文写「the unemployment rate for recent college grads rose to 5.8% in March, up from 4.6% the same time a year ago, according to the Federal Reserve Bank of New York」,该值来自 CPS 月度序列(college-labor-unemployment-data.csv,notes 明写 'Rates are seasonally adjusted and smoothed with a three-month moving average'),与 ACS 分专业表不同源不同频。两套口径确实不可并排:同为 2023 日历年,CPS 应届生失业率 12 个月均值 4.377%,而 2023 ACS 的 Overall 只有 3.640%,相差 0.74pp。三个修订值逐一核对自 Wayback 存档的 CPS CSV:20250501074651id_ → 3/1/2025 = 5.802、3/1/2024 = 4.6;20250808044602id_ → 3/1/2025 = 5.710、3/1/2024 = 4.542;当期直连文件 → 3/1/2025 = 5.672、3/1/2024 = 4.521。当期 by-major CSV 的 CS = 6.992、CE = 7.783,均已核。


#### [G13] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 论断 A 的「逐字」引语不完整,砍掉了作者自己的限定语。原文:「In most cases, the confidence intervals around these estimates of recent grad unemployment are so large that it makes no sense to use them to make firm conclusions about the returns to particular majors, let alone use them to make policy decisions.」→ 引用时必须保留开头的 'In most cases,'(作者说的是「多数情况下」,不是全称判断),尾部截断可用省略号标出。(2) 论断 A 应补时点与覆盖限定:该文发于 2025-08-13,分析对象是 2023 ACS;其 EPOP 90% 与「implosion is greatly exaggerated」的结论都止于 2023 年数据,并未覆盖 2026-02-04 发布的 2024 ACS(CS 失业率已进一步升至 6.992%,CE 7.783%)。作者本人在文中也写明「it is likely that the job prospects for young graduates of computer and information science programs have worsened since the last ACS」。(3) 利益相关须写得比「政策倡导型智库」更具体:Economic Innovation Group 是华盛顿的倡导型机构,由科技投资人(Sean Parker、Ron Conway)出资创办,长期主张扩大高技能移民/H-1B;该文开篇即点名这波报道被用于「high-skilled immigration」争论,「CS 就业市场没有崩」正是其政策立场的支撑面。(4) 论断 C 建议加一条更硬的正面证据(不改变结论):NY Fed 自己的方法论原文做分专业分析时用的是 13 个宽口径专业 + 2009–11 三年合并 ACS(「We classify recent college graduates as belonging to one of thirteen different undergraduate majors... for the 2009-11 period」);网页版扩到 73 个细分专业 + 单年 ACS,这一扩展从未配套发布过任何样本量、标准误或方法论说明。

**核验依据**:论断 A:直取 https://agglomerations.eig.org/p/a-viral-chart-on-recent-graduate(Connor O'Brien,Aug 13, 2025,标题《A viral chart on recent graduate unemployment is misleading》)。原文逐字:computer engineering「somewhere between four percent and 11 percent」;physics「somewhere between two percent and 12 percent」;public policy「from zero percent to 13 percent」;方法为「Using special weights provided by the ACS」(重复权重);EPOP「A full 90 percent of 22- to 27-year-olds who majored in those fields were employed in 2023」(ACS 把 6 个专业归入 computer and information sciences 大类);结尾「Still, news of the much-anticipated implosion of the computer science graduate job market is greatly exaggerated, at least for now.」——CI 三组数、EPOP 90%、结尾引语全部吻合,唯 'In most cases,' 被漏掉。论断 B:用我自己下载的 2023 ACS 版(Wayback 20250416141515id_)与 2024 ACS 版(当期直连)逐专业复算 73 个专业失业率差值:变化绝对值中位数 = 1.2520pp(claim 的 1.25pp 精确成立);|Δ|>2pp 的恰为 20 个(>=2pp 也是 20 个,无边界歧义)。清单逐条核实无一错:Early Childhood Education 1.298→6.593(+5.295)、Performing Arts 2.718→6.950(+4.232)、Nutrition Sciences 0.441→4.536(+4.095)、Environmental Studies 2.582→6.307(+3.725)、Art History 3.047→6.688(+3.641)、Medical Technicians 2.823→6.236(+3.413)、Public Policy and Law 5.513→2.237(−3.276)、Mechanical Engineering 1.529→4.364(+2.835)、Chemical Engineering 2.024→4.711(+2.687)、Business Analytics 2.392→5.034(+2.642)、Architecture 4.284→6.844(+2.560)、Foreign Language 4.040→1.579(−2.461);另有 8 个未列入的同级跳变(Pharmacy +2.433、Earth Sciences +2.379、Ethnic Studies +2.291、Sociology −2.140、Advertising and PR +2.134、General Engineering +2.127、Journalism −2.037、Mathematics +2.019)。对照组 CS +0.936pp、CE +0.245pp,与 claim 的 +0.94/+0.25 一致。论断 C:检索了当期 CSV(仅 6 列,无 N/SE)、college-labor-chart-meta.json 全部 notes、页面 FAQ 全文(https://www.newyorkfed.org/research/college-labor-market,含「Does the Fed keep historical records on outcomes by major?」等 14 问,无一处提样本量或标准误)、可下载的 College-labor-data_historical.xlsx(sharedStrings 全扫,只有 underemployed job types 的两条注释)、FAQ 指向的两篇方法论原文(ci20-1.pdf 与 NBER w22654),以及 2016 年网页发布公告 —— 均无分专业样本量、标准误或置信区间。ci20-1.pdf 第 657–672 行确认原方法为 13 个专业 + 2009–11 三年合并。「从未公布」属全称否定,以上为已穷尽的公开渠道,故给 high 而非绝对。


### 第 3 席


#### [G10] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:Downloaded the live CSV (https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-outcomes-by-major-data.csv) and the driver metadata (https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/research/interactives/data/college-labor-market/college-labor-chart-meta.json). Meta block '--#explore#outcomes-by-major' reads verbatim: releaseDateInfo 'February 4, 2026, based on data from 2024'; source 'Source: U.S. Census Bureau, American Community Survey (IPUMS).'; notes 'Notes: Figures are for 2024. Unemployment and underemployment rates are for recent college graduates (that is, those aged 22 to 27 with a bachelor's degree or higher), and median wages are for full-time workers with a bachelor's degree only. Early career graduates are those aged 22 to 27, and mid-career graduates are those aged 35 to 45. ... All figures exclude those currently enrolled in school.' All ten checked. CSV has 74 data rows = 73 majors + one 'Overall' row. Every cell in the claim's table matches to the third decimal: Computer Engineering 7.783/15.835/90000/131000; Computer Science 6.992/19.127/87000/120000; Nursing 2.147/12.781/70000/87000; Elementary Education 1.18/16.213/45000/55000; Biology 4.292/51.13/45000/83000; Psychology 4.985/48.291/45000/72000; Fine Arts 7.655/58.87/45000/72000; Economics 3.524/33.094/72000/115000; Finance 2.758/27.758/70000/112000; Overall 4.211/39.35/58000/87000. Rankings recomputed over the 73 majors excluding Overall: CS unemployment rank 70/73 ascending (4th worst; worst five = Architecture 6.844, Performing Arts 6.950, CS 6.992, Fine Arts 7.655, Computer Engineering 7.783, Anthropology 7.922) — CONFIRMED; CS underemployment rank 9/73 best — CONFIRMED; CS early wage rank 2/73 ($87k, behind CE $90k) — CONFIRMED; Nursing underemployment rank 1/73 (12.781) — CONFIRMED. Rankings are robust to whether the Overall row is included (Overall's 39.35 underemployment and $58k wage do not sit between any of the cited ranks; CS would be 71/74 on unemployment, still 4th worst). Underemployment classification sentence verified verbatim in the site metadata; denominator verified in the primary methodology paper: Abel & Deitz, NY Fed Staff Report 749 (https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/media/research/staff_reports/sr749.pdf), p.6: 'We measure the underemployment rate as the share of employed college graduates working in jobs that do not require a college degree' — so the unemployed are indeed outside the denominator. CONFIRMED. Two textual caveats that do not change any number: (1) the verbatim '50 percent or more' sentence and the O*NET source attribution appear in the CPS-based Underemployment tab's notes and in the site FAQ ('This analysis uses survey data from the U.S. Department of Labor's Occupational Information Network (O*NET) Education and Training Questionnaire'), NOT in the Outcomes-by-Major tab's own source line, which cites only ACS/IPUMS; (2) SR749's own wording is 'more than 50 percent of the respondents,' one word looser than the website's '50 percent or more.' The population-mismatch the claim flags is real and important: for Biology the Share-with-Graduate-Degree is 63.98%, so the $45,000 early-career median describes only the ~36% who stopped at a bachelor's, while the 4.292% unemployment covers all degree levels.

Each version's college-labor-chart-meta.json '--#explore#outcomes-by-major' block confirms release date and data year: Nov-2023 snapshot -> 'February 10, 2023' / 'Figures are for 2021'; May-2024 snapshot -> 'February 22, 2024' / 'Figures are for 2022'; Apr-2025 snapshot -> 'February 20, 2025' / 'Figures are for 2023'; Mar-2026 snapshot -> 'February 4, 2026, based on data from 2024' / 'Figures are for 2024'. All four vintages carry the identical notes sentence separating the two populations. Every cell of the claim's time series matches exactly: CS unemployment 4.8 / 4.267 / 6.056 / 6.992; CS underemployment 19.1 / 16.654 / 16.456 / 19.127; CS early wage 73000 / 78000 / 80000 / 87000; CE unemployment 3.7 / 2.311 / 7.538 / 7.783; Overall unemployment 5.1 / 3.557 / 3.64 / 4.211. Arithmetic checked: 6.992 − 4.267 = +2.725pp (claim's +2.7pp correct); 87000/78000 = +11.538%. The three corrections above come from cells in the same files the claim relies on: Overall early wage 50000 (2022) -> 58000 (2024) = +16.0%; CS underemployment 16.654 (2022) -> 19.127 (2024) = +2.473pp; the wage-column population is stated as 'bachelor's degree only' in every vintage's notes.

Source: ... Current Population Survey (IPUMS).' CS/CE 当期值 6.992/7.783 见 G10 证据。


#### [G13] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:B 全对，A 与 C 需修，另有一处对称性漏洞必须补上。(A) 引语被删掉了限定词：原表述引 'the confidence intervals around these estimates of recent grad unemployment are so large that it makes no sense to use them to make firm conclusions about the returns to particular majors.' → 原文实为 'In most cases, the confidence intervals ... about the returns to particular majors, let alone use them to make policy decisions.' 少了句首 'In most cases'(作者自己没有说全体适用)，尾部也截断了。CI 数值(CE 4%–11%、Physics 2%–12%、Public Policy 0%–13%)、EPOP 90%、结尾引语均逐字无误。但论断漏掉了 EIG 自己写下的两条自限：'Given the surge in layoffs from Big Tech firms in 2023 and 2024, it is likely that the job prospects for young graduates of computer and information science programs have worsened since the last ACS'，以及结论限定 'at least for now'——该文发于 2025-08-13，用的是 2023 ACS；2026-02-04 发布的 2024 ACS 已把 CS 推到 6.992%。引用它时必须标注「其结论只覆盖到 2023 数据，作者本人已预告会变差」。(C) 『NY Fed 从未公布』是全称否定，应改为可证的表述：→『NY Fed 在该表的公开发布渠道中均未提供样本量、标准误或置信区间——四份 CSV 无 N/SE 列，页面 notes、FAQ、以及历史数据 xlsx(College-labor-data_historical.xlsx)的 Cover Sheet 注释中均无 sample size / standard error / margin of error 字样；方法论底稿 SR749 亦未公布该网页表的专业级样本量』。(最重要)论断把噪声论证只用在一边：原表述『CS/CE 的高失业率稳定复现，而媒体拿来做对照的艺术史/营养学一年就翻车』→ 正确表述『CS/CE 的当前高位只由两次连续读数支撑，且 CE 这个高位本身就诞生于一次单年跳变：2022 ACS 2.311% → 2023 ACS 7.538%，+5.23pp，是 2022→2023 全表第二大跳变(仅次于 Aerospace Engineering 的 7.771→1.397，−6.37pp)，正是论断用来否定艺术史的同一种波动。CS 同期也跳了 4.267→6.056(+1.79pp)，是那一年中位绝对变化 0.853pp 的两倍多。且 EIG 给 CE 的 95% CI 为 4%–11%，7.538 与 7.783 两次读数同处一个区间内，「复现」在统计上不提供额外信息。因此可承重的表述只能是「CS/CE 连续两年读数偏高，但样本噪声量级下尚不足以称为稳定趋势」。』给准大学生看的文章尤其不能只对一方使用噪声论证。

**核验依据**:EIG 原文 https://agglomerations.eig.org/p/a-viral-chart-on-recent-graduate 全文抓取逐字核对(Connor O'Brien, Aug 13, 2025)：'we can say with 95 percent confidence that the unemployment rate for new computer engineering graduates is somewhere between four percent and 11 percent'；'For recent physics majors, we are 95 percent certain that the unemployment rate is somewhere between two percent and 12 percent. For public policy majors, from zero percent to 13 percent.'；'A full 90 percent of 22- to 27-year-olds who majored in those fields were employed in 2023.'(注意：这是 ACS 归并的 'computer and information sciences' 六个专业的大类，非 NY Fed 表里的 Computer Science 单项)；'Still, news of the much-anticipated implosion of the computer science graduate job market is greatly exaggerated, at least for now.' 论断 B 用两版 CSV(Wayback 20250416141515id_ = 2023 ACS，20260303160457id_ = 2024 ACS)全量复算：73 个专业同名可配对，无一缺失；失业率变化的中位绝对值 = 1.252pp(论断 1.25，命中)；|Δ|>2pp 者恰为 20 个(命中)；论断列出的 12 个跳变最大者与我算出的按 |Δ| 排序前 12 完全一致且数值逐格吻合(Early Childhood Education 1.298→6.593 +5.295、Performing Arts 2.718→6.950、Nutrition Sciences 0.441→4.536、Environmental Studies 2.582→6.307、Art History 3.047→6.688、Medical Technicians 2.823→6.236、Public Policy and Law 5.513→2.237、Mechanical Engineering 1.529→4.364、Chemical Engineering 2.024→4.711、Business Analytics 2.392→5.034、Architecture 4.284→6.844、Foreign Language 4.040→1.579)；CS +0.936pp、CE +0.245pp(论断 +0.94/+0.25，命中)。B = 完全成立。对称性反证由我另算 2022→2023 一档得到：该年中位绝对变化仅 0.853pp、19 个 >2pp，前两名为 Aerospace Engineering −6.374 与 Computer Engineering +5.227。C 的核查路径：https://www.newyorkfed.org/research/college-labor-market 全文(含 FAQ)grep 'margin of error / standard error / sample size / confidence interval / observations' 零命中；四份数据 CSV 无 N 或 SE 列；下载 College-labor-data_historical.xlsx 并解出 sharedStrings，注释只有 source/notes 三条，无样本量；SR749 (https://www.newyorkfed.org/medialibrary/media/research/staff_reports/sr749.pdf) 仅在脚注提到 'small sample sizes within many local labor markets'，未公布网页表的专业级 N。利益相关：EIG 由 Sean Parker 与 John Lettieri、Steve Glickman 于 2015 年创办，启动资金约 1500 万美元，自述使命为 'empower entrepreneurs and investors to forge a more dynamic U.S. economy'，并长期倡导高技能移民扩容——该文开篇即把争议框定为涉及 'the future of STEM education, the effects of AI on labor markets, and even high-skilled immigration'，与其倡导议程直接相关。这不否定其统计工作(CI 复算方法本身正当，且有 GitHub 仓库可查)，但『CS 就业市场没有崩塌』这一结论方向与其资助方与政策立场同向，引用时应披露。


## 批次 B2 — verify:CS饱和对账


### 第 1 席


#### [G14] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:取得报告正文 PDF(https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/cew-the_major_payoff.pdf)与官方数据工具后,发现两条数字系伪、多处口径需修。

【严重错误 1 — CS 对比基准】原表述「CS 应届失业率 7.2%(2021–23),对比 2013–15 的 4.3%」→ 正确表述:报告正文原文为「unemployment among recent graduates with a bachelor's degree in computer science is 7.2 percent, **up from a low of 5.3 percent in 2014**」(脚注 66 = ACS 2013–15 pooled 与 2021–23 pooled)。基准是 **5.3%,不是 4.3%**。4.3% 实为 2022 年 multi/interdisciplinary studies 的应届失业率(Figure 13),被张冠李戴。此错误把 CS 失业率涨幅从 +1.9pp 夸大为 +2.9pp,近乎放大 50%。

【严重错误 2 — 应届收入区间】原表述「应届收入区间 $34,000(communication disorders)至 $86,000(computer science)」→ 全部四个要素皆错。报告 Figure 6:最低为 **$37,000(zoology)**;communication disorders sciences and services 为 **$39,000**。Figure 5:最高为 **$98,000(petroleum engineering)**;**computer science 应届中位为 $83,000**($86,000 是 chemical engineering 与 computer engineering)。全文检索「$34,000」零命中。且此区间口径为 ACS **2009–23 十五年合并**、全职全年、仅学士(不含研究生学位者)、2024 年美元 —— 与失业率的 2021–23 合并不是同一口径。

【严重错误 3 — 应届失业率区间】原表述「应届失业率区间 1.3%(operations logistics and e-commerce)至 11%(film, video, and photographic arts)」→ REFUTED,CEW 根本不发布专业层级失业率。正文脚注 8 原文:「Our analysis of the unemployment rate among bachelor's degree holders is aggregated at the detailed field level due to small sample sizes within some majors.」我下载了官方失业率数据工具底层数据(https://cewgeorgetown.github.io/wiw-25-ur/ur_earn_ur.js),字段只有 field / detailed_field,**无 major 字段**。2022 年应届失业率的真实全域区间是 **2.8%(Education)至 8.9%(Arts)**,共 16 个 detailed field。请整条删除。

【口径修正 4 — 「大类」措辞】原表述「computers/statistics/mathematics 大类」→ 应为 CEW 的 **detailed field(16 个之一)**,隶属 STEM broad field,下辖 **8 个专业**:applied mathematics、computer and information systems、**computer engineering**、computer science、information sciences、mathematics、statistics and decision science、及 other。**Computer Engineering 单列为专业,且就在这个 6.8% 的 detailed field 之内**(数据工具 dfield 字段确证),不在 architecture and engineering 之下 —— 即 6.8% 已包含计算机工程。此「大类」与 NCES CIP 11 不是同一个东西。

【口径修正 5 — prime-age 定义】原表述「prime-age 学士 vs 仅高中:中位收入高 70%($81,000)」→ prime-age = **ages 25–54**;$81,000 口径为 **全职全年(full-time, full-year)、仅持学士(排除同时持研究生学位者)**、ACS 2009–23 合并、2024 年美元。若含研究生学位者则为 $88,000(脚注 5)。失业率 2.9% vs 6.2% 口径不同,为 ACS **2021–23 合并**、25–54 岁,不限全职。

【口径修正 6 — 79,000/107,000】数字正确,但脚注 64 显示其为 ACS **2021–23 合并**,而报告绝大多数收入数字用 2009–23 合并 —— 同一篇报告内收入口径不统一,引用时须逐条标注。

【口径修正 7 — 152 vs 142】152 个专业仅对 prime-age 成立;方法论附录原文「a total of 152 majors for prime-age workers and **142 majors for recent college graduates**」。

【补充 — 年份标签陷阱】方法论原文:失业率「for each year represents a pooled three-year average... '2022' represents the pooled average of 2021–23」。故报告里的「2014」= 2013–15 合并,「2022」= 2021–23 合并。图 13 标题写「2022」但注释写 2021–23 合并,引用时不要当作单年。

【利益相关 — 须标注】CEW 是乔治城 McCourt 公共政策学院下属政策研究所,自述使命为「promoting postsecondary access, completion, and career success」,即先验立场是支持读大学;本报告资助方为 **Lumina Foundation 与 Gates Foundation**(两家均以提高学位获得率为纲领)。报告主结论「学士学位仍值得」与资助方纲领同向。

【最重要 — 削弱「关键作用」】原论断称此为「不同机构、不同口径的独立复算,削弱纯样本噪声解释」。但 CEW 与 NY Fed 使用的是**同一套底层微观数据:Census Bureau American Community Survey**。因此这不是独立测量,而是同一份调查的不同切法(不同年龄窗、不同合并策略)。它能排除的是「某一种切法的偶然」,**不能**排除 ACS 本身的抽样/口径问题。文章若把它写成「两个独立来源互证」属于高估确定性,应改写为「同一数据源的稳健性检验」。

**核验依据**:一手正文 PDF https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/cew-the_major_payoff.pdf(6.3MB,pdftotext 全文提取):p.37 原文「unemployment among recent graduates with a bachelor's degree in computer science is 7.2 percent, up from a low of 5.3 percent in 2014」+ 脚注 66「ACS 2013–15 (pooled) and 2021–23 (pooled)」;脚注 8「aggregated at the detailed field level due to small sample sizes」;Figure 13 注释「respondents ages 22–26... pooled three-year average from 2021–23. Workers with both a bachelor's degree and a graduate degree are not included」;Figure 5(petroleum $98,000 / chem eng $86,000 / computer eng $86,000 / computer science $83,000);Figure 6(zoology $37,000 / counseling psych $38,000 / communication disorders $39,000);Table C1(computers, statistics, and mathematics 53,499→138,755 = 159%);方法论附录「152 majors for prime-age workers and 142 majors for recent college graduates」「'2022' represents the pooled average of 2021–23」;脚注 5(含研究生学位者 $88,000)。新闻稿 https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/GeorgetownCEW_PressRelease_Major-Payoff_10.16.25.pdf 逐字含 Ban Cheah 引语,与论断所引完全一致(159%/6.8%/highest within STEM/$79,000/$107,000/'high unemployment does not always go hand-in-hand with low earnings'),并含 $81,000、70%、2.9%、6.2%、152 majors。官方数据工具底层 JSON:https://cewgeorgetown.github.io/wiw-25-ur/ur_earn_ur.js(仅 field/detailed_field,无 major;2022 应届 UR 最低 Education 2.8%、最高 Arts 8.9%;computers/statistics/mathematics 应届 ur=0.068、rp50=78,800)与 https://cewgeorgetown.github.io/wiw-25-earn/wiw-earn.js(该 detailed field 下 8 个专业含 Computer engineering;All majors p50r_ba=81,000)。资助方与使命见正文 ACKNOWLEDGMENTS 页。


#### [G15] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:逐格核对 https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d23/tables/dt23_325.35.asp 原表:**全部 10 个年份数字、两个跌幅、两个涨幅、provisional 状态全部无误**。但两处结构性表述必须改。

【错误 1 — 「谷」年份认定错】原表述「1993–94 年 24,527(谷,−42.1%)」→ 1993-94 **不是谷底**。原表相邻年份:1991-92 = 24,821;1992-93 = **24,519**;1993-94 = 24,527;1994-95 = 24,737;1995-96 = **24,506**。真正的最低点是 **1995–96 年的 24,506**,次低是 1992-93 的 24,519,1993-94 只排第三。正确表述应为:「1985–86 峰值 42,337 → 1991-92 至 1995-96 形成约 24,500 的**五年谷底平台**,最低点 1995–96 年 24,506(较峰值 −42.1%)」。跌幅百分比两种算法都是 −42.1%(42,337→24,527 为 −42.07%;→24,506 为 −42.11%),数字可保留,但「1993-94 是谷」的说法错误。**这直接影响滞后期叙事**:第一轮周期的底部不是一个点而是持续五年的平台,复苏起点应记为 1996-97,而非 1994-95。

(第二轮谷底 2008-09 = 37,992 认定正确:2007-08 = 38,523,2009-10 = 39,593,确为局部最小;59,488→37,992 = −36.13%,「−36.1%」无误。)

【错误 2 — CIP 归属推测错】原口径警告称「第三轮上升可能部分反映 CIP 11 口径扩张(**数据科学等新专业被归入**)」→ 事实相反:CIP 2020 中 **Data Science = 30.70 / 30.7001,归在 CIP 30「Multi/Interdisciplinary Studies」之下,不在 CIP 11**。故数据科学学位**不**计入表 325.35。请改为:「CIP 11(Computer and Information Sciences and Support Services)范围远大于 computer science 本身,含信息技术(11.0103)、信息学(11.0104)、人工智能(11.0102)、信息系统安全(11.10 系列)等;口径宽度问题成立,但与数据科学无关 —— 数据科学被归入 CIP 30。」

【口径补充 3 — 「provisional」与「已修订」】表格 SOURCE 行原文「IPEDS... Fall 2000 through Fall 2021 (final data) and **Fall 2022 (provisional data)**」,确认 2021-22 为 provisional ✓。但 NOTE 行还有一句必须一并标注:「**Some data have been revised from previously published figures.**」以及「Data in this table are based on the **2020** Classification of Instructional Programs」—— 即整条 1964 年以来的长序列是用 2020 版 CIP **回溯重编**的,跨版本 Digest 的同年数字会不一致。用它做「两轮完整蛛网」的跨四十年比较时,这是比 provisional 更大的不确定性来源。

【核验结论 4 — 无更新版】d24 与 d25 的 dt24_325.35 / dt25_325.35 **均返回 HTTP 404**;d23 仍是该表最新版,最晚年份止于 2021-22。故无更晚年份可补(IPEDS Completions 原始数据本身已有 2022-23、2023-24,但未进入 Digest 该表)。

【口径补充 5 — 滞后期】「泡沫破裂 2000-03 → 峰值 2003-04 学年,约 4 年」算术无误,但 2003-04 学年授予日在 2004 年春,故为约 4 年;更需注明:学位数峰值反映的是**约 2000 年入学**的那一届,即峰值人群恰恰是在泡沫顶点做出专业选择的学生,「滞后 4 年」本质是四年学制本身,而非市场信号传导延迟。

**核验依据**:直取 https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d23/tables/dt23_325.35.asp 原始 HTML 并逐行解析全部 58 个学年行。核到的学士学位数(both sexes):1985-86 = 42,337;1986-87 = 39,767;1991-92 = 24,821;1992-93 = 24,519;1993-94 = 24,527;1994-95 = 24,737;**1995-96 = 24,506**;2003-04 = 59,488;2007-08 = 38,523;2008-09 = 37,992;2009-10 = 39,593;2013-14 = 55,271;2017-18 = 79,597;2019-20 = 97,054;2020-21 = 104,883;2021-22 = 108,503。验算:108,503/39,593 = +174.0%;108,503/37,992 = +185.6%(≈+186%);42,337→24,527 = −42.07%;59,488→37,992 = −36.13%。表 NOTE/SOURCE 原文:「Data in this table are based on the 2020 Classification of Instructional Programs. Some data have been revised from previously published figures.」「Completions component, IPEDS Fall 2000 through Fall 2021 (final data) and Fall 2022 (provisional data).」表题:「Degrees in computer and information sciences conferred by postsecondary institutions... Academic years 1964-65 through 2021-22」。d24/d25 该表 curl 实测 HTTP 404(d23 为 200)。Data Science CIP 归属见 NCES CIP 2020 明细 https://nces.ed.gov/ipeds/cipcode/cipdetail.aspx?y=56&cipid=92951(30.70)与 cipid=92953(30.7001),均在 CIP 30 Multi/Interdisciplinary Studies 之下。


#### [G16] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:bls.gov 直连 403,改用 Wayback 原始快照(id_ 后缀,--compressed 解 gzip)逐格核对,三处全部逐字命中,无一处需修。

论断 A — OOH「Software Developers, Quality Assurance Analysts, and Testers」,快照 http://web.archive.org/web/20260601110258id_/https://www.bls.gov/ooh/computer-and-information-technology/software-developers.htm:Quick Facts 原文「2024 Median Pay $131,450 per year / $63.20 per hour」「Number of Jobs, 2024: 1,895,500」「Job Outlook, 2024–34: 15% (Much faster than average)」「Employment Change, 2024–34: 287,900」。逐字句「Overall employment of software developers, quality assurance analysts, and testers is projected to grow 15 percent from 2024 to 2034, much faster than the average for all occupations.」与论断所引完全一致。「About 129,200 openings... are projected each year, on average, over the decade.」✓ 全职业平均 3% ✓(Quick Facts 定义行「The average growth rate for all occupations is 3 percent」)。

论断 B — OOH「Computer Programmers」,快照 http://web.archive.org/web/20260710181800id_/https://www.bls.gov/ooh/computer-and-information-technology/computer-programmers.htm:「2024 Median Pay $98,670 per year」「Number of Jobs, 2024: 121,200」「Job Outlook, 2024–34: -6% (Decline)」「Employment Change, 2024–34: -7,200」。逐字句「Despite declining employment, about 5,500 openings for computer programmers are projected each year, on average, over the decade. All of those openings are expected to result from the need to replace workers who transfer to other occupations or exit the labor force, such as to retire.」与论断所引(含省略号处)完全一致 ✓。

论断 C — MLR 2026-01,快照 http://web.archive.org/web/2026id_/https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2026/article/industry-and-occupational-employment-projections-overview.htm:原文「One occupation that stands to benefit from these trends is software developers. Not only are they projected to experience employment growth that is over 5 times faster than the all-occupation average (15.8 percent), but they are also projected to have the second-largest increase in employment of all occupations with 267,700 new jobs added through 2034.」✓ AI 利好句原文「The growing adoption of AI technologies, including generative AI tools, is another factor that will fuel strong job growth among computer and mathematical occupations.」✓ AI 利空句原文(位于 Highlights 段)「The growing adoption of AI technologies, including generative AI tools, and resulting productivity gains are expected to dampen labor demand in a variety of fields, such as sales, design, and administrative support.」✓ 两句同篇并存,论断的「同时写成利好与利空」描述准确。

冲突待解 — 论断的区分完全正确,且我在 OOH 页内找到了官方对照证据:该页 Job Outlook 图表原文列出「Software developers 16% / Software developers, quality assurance analysts, and testers 15% / Software quality assurance analysts and testers 10% / Computer occupations 9% / Total, all occupations 3%」。即 287,900 与 15% 属**三职业合并组**;267,700 与 15.8% 属 **software developers 单列(SOC 15-1252)**,OOH 图表将其四舍五入为 16%。两者口径确实不同,文章必须区分 —— 论断这一点成立。建议行文时用「合并组 +15%/+287,900」与「软件开发者单列 +15.8%(OOH 图表作 16%)/+267,700」并列,并注明 MLR 全职业平均为 3.1%、OOH 作 3%。

另需向读者标注(不影响判决):这是 BLS 就业projections 模型的**十年期预测值**(2024 基年,2026 年初发布),不是实测就业数据;且 BLS 在同一文件内对 AI 给出双向表述,本身即表明该模型对 AI 冲击未作单向假设。另一处细微但真实的差别:合并组的年均空缺是「**Many** of those openings」来自替换需求,程序员是「**All** of those openings」—— 论断已正确保留了这一 all/many 区别。


#### [G17] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:逐字引语与四个结构数字全部核实无误,但两处表述放大了幅度、一处遗漏关键限定,须修。

【逐字引语 — 完全一致 ✓】原文「Even after the recent rise, software development job postings remain about 27.5% below their pre-pandemic level, while overall job postings are essentially the same as in February 2020.」一字不差。

【修正 1 — 「低约 30%」夸大】原表述「software development ≈ 73(较 2020-02 **低约 30%**)」→ 应为**低约 27%**。文章正文说 27.5%,而我取到 Hiring Lab 官方底层数据后实测:Software Development(total postings)指数 2026-06-30 = **73.47**,2026 年 6 月区间 72.33–73.62,即较基期低 26.4%–27.7%。论断自己写的「≈73」是对的,但「低约 30%」与之自相矛盾且偏悲观 2–3 个百分点。请统一写「约低 27%」。

【修正 2 — 「+15%」应为「接近 15%」且须注明会随数据版本变动】原表述「自 2025-02-24 起算美国软件开发岗位广告 +15%」→ 文章原文两处均为「risen **almost** 15%」「grown by **almost** 15%」,是「接近 15%」而非「达到 15%」。更重要的是:我下载的当前版本官方数据(截至 2026-07-17)算出的同一区间涨幅已是 **+19.5%**(63.26 → 75.62),而全部岗位为 **−6.8%**。Indeed 会持续修订季节调整因子与回溯数据,**同一个「自 Claude Code 发布以来 +15%」的说法在不同抓取时点会给出不同数字**。文章若引用须写明数据截取日期。

【修正 3 — 71% 与 37% 不可相加,须注明重叠】原表述并列「71% 来自资深岗、37% 来自标题含 AI 的岗位」但未注明二者关系。文章图注原文明确:「senior roles account for 71% of the **net** increase... and AI-related titles for 37%, **with the two categories overlapping**.」必须补上「两类互相重叠,不能相加,合计不等于 108%」,否则读者会误推出「仅 −8% 为其他」。另须注明是**净增量(net increase)**的构成,不是存量结构。

【修正 4 — 三个基期混在同一条里】该论断把三个不同基期的数字并列:27.5% 是对 **2020-02-01 = 100**;+15%/−7% 是对 **2025-02-24 = 100**;71%/37% 是 **2025-05 至 2026-05** 的增量分解。文章中必须分开陈述,否则读者会把它们当成同一坐标系下的数字。

【核实通过 — JPI = 101.0 ✓】原表述「全部岗位 JPI = 101.0(2026-06-30)」经底层数据确证:aggregate total postings **季调(SA)** 指数 2026-06-30 = **100.96**,四舍五入即 101.0 ✓。但须注明是**季调**值 —— 同日**未季调(NSA)**为 **104.02**,差 3 个点,不标 SA/NSA 会被人拿反。

【FRED 序列补取】FRED 直连与 WebFetch 均被拦(HTTP 403/000),改由 Indeed Hiring Lab 官方 GitHub 仓库取到同一底层序列(FRED 的 IHLIDXUSTPSOFTDEVE 即由此数据供稿):关键值 2025-02-24 = 63.26;2026-05-31 = 73.55;2026-06-01 = 73.44;2026-06-15 = 72.51;2026-06-30 = 73.47;2026-07-08 = 74.38;最新 2026-07-17 = 75.62。全部岗位 SA:2025-02-24 = 108.97;2026-06-30 = 100.96;2026-07-08 = 101.18。

【方法论一处未获证实】原表述称指数为「7 日滚动平均」→ 该文只写了「seasonally adjusted」与「indexed to 100 on February 1, 2020」,**未**提 7 日滚动平均。这是 Hiring Lab JPI 的通用方法论(见其 JPI 方法说明页),但不出自本文,引用时不要挂在本文名下。

【利益相关 — 论断已列,再补两条更尖锐的】(a) 论断已正确指出 Indeed 是招聘平台、指数只覆盖 Indeed 岗位、平台份额变化会污染指数、广告数下降也可能是招聘方式变化。(b) **更需注意的编辑框架问题**:本文把指数基期直接定在「2025-02-24, the launch of Claude Code」,并以 Claude Code 发布为叙事轴心 —— 这是作者选定的框架,不是自然实验;文章自己也承认「correlation does not imply causation」「there were, of course, many other factors influencing the market both then and now」。(c) 文章核心结论明确指向**招聘集中于资深岗**,并自述「This suggests demand is growing for experienced professionals who can work with AI, **not necessarily a broad-based recovery across all software roles**」,还引用了「seniority-biased technological change」。面向准大学生时,这条恰恰是**对入门口最不利**的证据,不能被「软件岗位在回升」的标题效应盖过。

**核验依据**:文章一手 HTML(curl 直取,非摘要):https://www.hiringlab.org/2026/07/08/ai-and-job-postings-from-destruction-to-creation/ —— 逐字段落含「Even after the recent rise, software development job postings remain about 27.5% below their pre-pandemic level, while overall job postings are essentially the same as in February 2020.」;「Claude Code was introduced in late February, 2025. Since that date, the number of job postings for software developers published on Indeed in the US has risen almost 15%, while job postings overall have declined by 7%.」;图注「indexed to 100 on February 24, 2025, the launch of Claude Code」与「indexed to 100 on February 1, 2020」;「71% of the increase in Software Development job postings between May 2025 and May 2026 came from senior roles, and 37% came from jobs that mention AI in their title.」;图注「senior roles account for 71% of the net increase... and AI-related titles for 37%, with the two categories overlapping.」;「This suggests demand is growing for experienced professionals who can work with AI, not necessarily a broad-based recovery across all software roles.」作者 Guillermo Gallacher,发布日 2026-07-08。底层数据取自 Indeed Hiring Lab 官方公开仓库 https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hiring-lab/job_postings_tracker/master/US/job_postings_by_sector_US.csv(Software Development / total postings)与 .../aggregate_job_postings_US.csv(indeed_job_postings_index_SA 与 _NSA),两文件基期行均为 2020-02-01 = 100,数据截至 2026-07-17。FRED 侧 https://fred.stlouisfed.org/series/IHLIDXUSTPSOFTDEVE 经 curl(HTTP 000)与 WebFetch(HTTP 403)两路均不可达,故以上游同源数据替代,数值可直接用于该 FRED 序列的逐月值。


### 第 2 席


#### [G14] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:Retrieved the full 74-page report PDF (collector had only the press release). Core direction survives; four specifics are wrong.

(1) BASELINE NUMBER IS WRONG — this is the most serious error. 原表述:'CS 应届失业率 7.2%(2021–23),对比 2013–15 的 4.3%' → 正确表述:'CS 应届失业率 7.2%,对比 2014 年的 5.3%'。The report (p.37) reads verbatim: 'unemployment among recent graduates with a bachelor's degree in computer science is 7.2 percent, up from a low of 5.3 percent in 2014.' Footnote 66 identifies the two windows as ACS 2013–15 (pooled) and 2021–23 (pooled) — so '2014' is the midpoint label of the 2013–15 pooled window. The figure 4.3% appears nowhere in the report in this context. Using 4.3% inflates the deterioration from 1.9pp to 2.9pp — a ~50% overstatement of the very quantity the article is built on.

(2) UNEMPLOYMENT-BY-MAJOR RANGE DOES NOT EXIST IN THIS REPORT — 原表述:'应届失业率区间 1.3%(operations logistics and e-commerce)至 11%(film, video, and photographic arts)' → 应删除或改归他源。Footnote 8 states verbatim: 'Our analysis of the unemployment rate among bachelor's degree holders is aggregated at the detailed field level due to small sample sizes within some majors.' CEW publishes unemployment for only the 16 detailed fields, never for individual majors. 'Operations logistics and e-commerce' appears in the report only as an EARNINGS entry (Figure 5: $70,000 recent / $92,000 prime-age), and 'Film, video, and photographic arts' only in the Appendix major-code list. These two by-major unemployment figures are almost certainly cross-contaminated from the NY Fed by-major table and must not be attributed to CEW.

(3) EARNINGS RANGE IS WRONG ON BOTH ENDS — 原表述:'应届收入区间 $34,000(communication disorders)至 $86,000(computer science)' → 正确表述:'应届收入区间 $37,000(zoology)至 $98,000(petroleum engineering)'。Figure 6 (lowest entry-level earnings): zoology $37,000, counseling psychology $38,000, communication disorders sciences and services $39,000 (third-lowest, and $39k not $34k). Figure 5 (most lucrative for recent grads): petroleum engineering $98,000, chemical engineering $86,000, computer engineering $86,000, actuarial science $85,000, electrical engineering $84,000, computer science $83,000. So $86,000 belongs to computer ENGINEERING, not computer science; computer science recent-grad median is $83,000 and ranks 6th, not 1st.

(4) TIME WINDOW APPLIES TO UNEMPLOYMENT ONLY, NOT EARNINGS — 原表述:'失业率为 2021–2023 三年合并估计' is correct, but the article must not extend that window to earnings. I counted the source lines: 8 figures/tables cite ACS 2021–23 (pooled) — all unemployment; 19 cite ACS 2009–23 (pooled) — all earnings, expressed in 2024$. So $79,000 / $107,000 / $81,000 / $83,000 / $86,000 are 15-year pooled figures, NOT 2021–23. (One exception: the specific p.37 sentence carrying $79,000/$107,000 cites footnote 64 = 2021–23 pooled, so the report is internally inconsistent here; the article should not present the general earnings table as recent-window data.)

(5) 限定语缺失 — all earnings are restricted to FULL-TIME, FULL-YEAR workers holding a bachelor's degree ONLY (people who also hold a graduate degree are excluded). Ban Cheah's own quote concedes this: 'among those who do manage to land a job.' Unemployment rates, by contrast, cover all respondents in the age band who are employed or unemployed, bachelor's-only. Omitting 'full-time, full-year' materially overstates typical outcomes.

(6) 专业数 — 原表述:'152 个专业' → 报告将 152 个专业分入 16 个 detailed fields 和 8 个 broad fields,但应届(recent college graduate)口径只覆盖 142 个专业(正文 p.50 明示)。Recent-grad claims should cite 142.

(7) '大类' 措辞 — 'computers, statistics, and mathematics' is a DETAILED FIELD (1 of 16), not a top-level field; it bundles 8 majors of which computer science is 42% of recent grads. Its 6.8% is therefore not a computer-science rate.

VERIFIED AS STATED: 7.2% CS recent-grad unemployment ✓; 6.8% for computers/statistics/mathematics and 'highest within STEM' ✓ (arts 8.9% is higher but is not STEM); $79,000 median / $107,000 75th pct ✓; 159% degree growth 2009–2023 ✓ and it is the fastest-growing of all 16 detailed fields (next: health 109%) ✓; prime-age bachelor's $81,000 median, 70% premium over high-school-only, 2.9% vs 6.2% unemployment ✓; recent grads = ages 22–26 ✓ (vs NY Fed 22–27); prime-age = ages 25–54 ✓; both Ban Cheah quoted sentences ✓ verbatim.

(c) COMPUTER ENGINEERING IS SINGLE-LISTED — yes. Table 3 breaks the detailed field into 7 majors: computer engineering $86,000/$121,000 (13% of recent grads), computer science $83,000/$116,000 (42%), statistics and decision science $73,000/$102,000 (2%), applied mathematics $70,000/$105,000 (2%), information sciences $68,000/$103,000 (4%), computer and information systems $63,000/$96,000 (12%), mathematics $61,000/$93,000 (14%).

(e) 利益相关须标注 — CEW is 'a research and policy institute within Georgetown's McCourt School of Public Policy' and is openly advocacy-oriented: the report and press release argue for aligning degree production with labor-market needs and for broadening access to 'lucrative or in-demand majors.' Its institutional interest is in defending the value of the bachelor's degree, and the headline finding ($81,000, +70% premium) serves that interest. Note also the 159% supply figure comes from IPEDS Completions matched to CEW's own major taxonomy (Appendix C, Table C1: 53,499 in 2009 → 138,755 in 2023, bachelor's level), a BROADER grouping than NCES CIP 11 — the two supply series in G14 and G15 are not interchangeable.

**核验依据**:Primary source retrieved directly: full report PDF https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/cew-the_major_payoff.pdf (74 pp., 6.27 MB, downloaded and text-extracted). Landing page https://cew.georgetown.edu/cew-reports/major-payoff/ (dated Oct 15, 2025). Press release https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/GeorgetownCEW_PressRelease_Major-Payoff_10.16.25.pdf (dated Washington DC, Oct. 16, 2025), which contains the Ban Cheah quote verbatim as claimed. Authors: Catherine Morris, Ban Cheah, Jeff Strohl. Key pages: p.37 (7.2% / 'up from a low of 5.3 percent in 2014', fn.66 = ACS 2013–15 pooled and 2021–23 pooled); fn.8 (unemployment aggregated only to detailed-field level due to small samples); fn.7 (earnings = ACS 2009–23 pooled, full-time full-year, bachelor's alone); Figure 5 (recent-grad top earners, petroleum engineering $98,000); Figure 6 (recent-grad bottom earners, zoology $37,000, communication disorders $39,000); Figure 13 (unemployment by detailed field, 'Unemployment rates among prime-age workers and recent college graduates, 2022', note = 2021–23 pooled); Table 3 (7 majors within computers/statistics/mathematics); Table C1 (IPEDS completions 53,499→138,755, +159%); Figure 1 / p.50 (152 majors total, 142 for recent college graduates).


#### [G15] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:Fetched the primary table directly: https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d23/tables/dt23_325.35.asp — 'Table 325.35. Degrees in computer and information sciences conferred by postsecondary institutions, by level of degree and sex of student: Academic years 1964-65 through 2021-22'. Every bachelor's-total figure in the claim matches the table cell-for-cell: 1985-86 = 42,337; 1993-94 = 24,527; 2003-04 = 59,488; 2008-09 = 37,992; 2009-10 = 39,593; 2013-14 = 55,271; 2017-18 = 79,597; 2019-20 = 97,054; 2020-21 = 104,883; 2021-22 = 108,503. I recomputed all four percentages: 42,337→24,527 = -42.07% (claim -42.1% ✓); 59,488→37,992 = -36.13% (claim -36.1% ✓); 39,593→108,503 = +174.05% (claim +174% ✓); 37,992→108,503 = +185.6% (claim +186% ✓). I also confirmed 1985-86 is the first local peak (the series rises monotonically from 87 in 1964-65) and 1993-94 is the first trough (1992-93 = 24,519 is marginally lower, 24,519 vs 24,527 — a trivial 8-degree difference that does not affect the argument; if the article wants the exact minimum of that trough it is 1992-93, but 1993-94 is the last year before the recovery begins and is a defensible trough marker).

Provisional-data flag CONFIRMED verbatim in SOURCE note: 'Completions component, IPEDS Fall 2000 through Fall 2021 (final data) and Fall 2022 (provisional data). (This table was prepared November 2023.)' — so 2021-22 is provisional as the claim states.

NEWER EDITION: none available for this table. Both https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d24/tables/dt24_325.35.asp and https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d25/tables/dt25_325.35.asp return HTTP 404, as does dt24_325.10. The d23 edition (prepared Nov 2023) remains the latest release of table 325.35, so no post-2021-22 year can be appended from this source. Independent partial cross-check for a later year: CEW's Appendix C Table C1 (IPEDS Completions, bachelor's level) gives 138,755 in 2023 for 'computers, statistics, and mathematics' — a broader grouping than CIP 11, so it is directionally consistent (continued growth) but NOT a substitute value for this series.

CIP SCOPE — the claim's caliber warning is correct and, if anything, understated. The table's NOTE reads: 'Data in this table are based on the 2020 Classification of Instructional Programs. Some data have been revised from previously published figures.' The field label is 'computer and information sciences' (NCES's CIP 11, 'Computer and Information Sciences and Support Services'), which subsumes information systems, IT, network administration, data processing and, since the 2020 CIP revision, newer data-science codes. It is materially broader than 'computer science' proper. The claim's own warning that the third-cycle +186% may partly reflect CIP-11 scope expansion is sound; note additionally that the 2020 CIP revision itself is a definitional break inside the series, and NCES explicitly warns that data have been revised — so cross-decade comparisons carry a classification discontinuity the article should disclose.

LAG CLAIM — arithmetically correct (dot-com peak March 2000 → conferral peak in academic year 2003-04, ≈4 years) but the causal reading deserves a caveat the claim does not state: because a bachelor's degree takes ~4 years, the 2003-04 conferral peak is a lagged echo of students who ENROLLED around 1999-2000, i.e. at or before the crash — it is not a response to the crash. The behavioural response (enrollment flight) appears in conferrals only from 2004-05 onward, which is where the -36.1% slide actually begins. For a cobweb argument this strengthens rather than weakens the point, but the '4-year lag' should be described as the degree-pipeline delay, not as a 4-year decision lag.


#### [G16] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:bls.gov returns HTTP 403 to both WebFetch and direct curl, so I used Wayback raw (id_) captures and verified cell-by-cell.

CLAIM A — OOH Software Developers, QA Analysts, and Testers, snapshot 2026-07-05 (https://web.archive.org/web/20260705083303id_/https://www.bls.gov/ooh/computer-and-information-technology/software-developers.htm). Quick Facts read exactly: 2024 Median Pay $131,450/yr ($63.20/hr); Number of Jobs, 2024 = 1,895,500; Job Outlook 2024–34 = 15% (Much faster than average); Employment Change 2024–34 = 287,900. Quoted sentence matches verbatim: 'Overall employment of software developers, quality assurance analysts, and testers is projected to grow 15 percent from 2024 to 2034, much faster than the average for all occupations.' Openings sentence: 'About 129,200 openings for software developers, quality assurance analysts, and testers are projected each year, on average, over the decade.' All five figures ✓. One nuance worth carrying into the article: the $131,450 Quick-Facts median is the COMBINED three-occupation median; the same page's Pay section gives software developers alone a May 2024 median of $133,080. If the article pairs a wage with the software-developer-only projection, it should use $133,080, not $131,450.

CLAIM B — OOH Computer Programmers, snapshot 2026-07-10 (https://web.archive.org/web/20260710181800id_/https://www.bls.gov/ooh/computer-and-information-technology/computer-programmers.htm). Quick Facts: 2024 Median Pay $98,670/yr ($47.44/hr); Number of Jobs, 2024 = 121,200; Job Outlook 2024–34 = -6% (Decline); Employment Change 2024–34 = -7,200. Quoted text matches verbatim: 'Despite declining employment, about 5,500 openings for computer programmers are projected each year, on average, over the decade. All of those openings are expected to result from the need to replace workers who transfer to other occupations or exit the labor force, such as to retire.' (The claim's ellipsis correctly elides 'on average, over the decade' and the trailing 'such as to retire.') All five figures ✓.

CLAIM C — MLR January 2026 EP overview, snapshot 2026-04-03 (https://web.archive.org/web/20260403222046id_/https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2026/article/industry-and-occupational-employment-projections-overview.htm), title 'Industry and occupational employment projections overview and highlights, 2024–34'. All three quoted passages verified verbatim: (i) 'One occupation that stands to benefit from these trends is software developers. Not only are they projected to experience employment growth that is over 5 times faster than the all-occupation average (15.8 percent), but they are also projected to have the second-largest increase in employment of all occupations with 267,700 new jobs added through 2034.' (ii) tailwind: 'The growing adoption of AI technologies, including generative AI tools, is another factor that will fuel strong job growth among computer and mathematical occupations.' (iii) headwind, from the Highlights list: 'The growing adoption of AI technologies, including generative AI tools, and resulting productivity gains are expected to dampen labor demand in a variety of fields, such as sales, design, and administrative support.' ✓

THE CONFLICT THE CLAIM FLAGS IS REAL AND ITS RESOLUTION IS CONFIRMED. The two numbers are different units of analysis and the article is right to insist on separating them: OOH's +15% / +287,900 / 1,895,500 covers the THREE-occupation bundle (software developers + QA analysts + testers, SOC 15-1252 + 15-1253); MLR's +15.8% / +267,700 covers SOFTWARE DEVELOPERS ALONE (SOC 15-1252). The two are arithmetically consistent: 267,700 / 0.158 implies a 2024 software-developer base of ≈1,694,000, leaving ≈201,000 QA analysts and testers to reach the bundle's 1,895,500. So the bundle grows slightly slower (15%) than developers alone (15.8%) because the QA/testers component — the part most exposed to automation — grows more slowly. That is a substantively important point for a piece about AI and entry-level work, and it survives scrutiny.

ALL-OCCUPATION AVERAGE — the claim's '3%' is right as BLS's own rounded figure; the precise value in the MLR occupational matrix is 'Total, all occupations ... 3.1' percent (+5,211.8 thousand jobs, 2024 base 169,956 thousand). Both internal consistency checks pass: 15.8 / 3.1 = 5.1 ('over 5 times faster' ✓) and the computer-and-mathematical group's 10.1% is 3.26× the average ('over 3 times as fast' ✓). If the article states the average to one decimal, use 3.1%.

CAVEAT NOT IN THE CLAIM: BLS EP projections are a deterministic 10-year model whose occupational staffing patterns are largely extrapolated from historical industry-occupation matrices; the 2024–34 round was published before most agentic-coding adoption data existed, and BLS itself hedges by writing AI as both tailwind and headwind in the same document. The +15% should be presented as BLS's modelled baseline, not as a forecast with error bars.


#### [G17] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:All four structural numbers and the verbatim quote check out, but two statements need fixing and one framing issue must be disclosed.

(1) THE SHORTFALL IS OVERSTATED — 原表述:'software development ≈ 73(较 2020-02 低约 30%)' → 正确表述:'≈73.5,较 2020-02 低约 26.5%(文章自称 27.5%)'。An index of 73 IS 27 percent below 100, so '≈73' and '低约30%' contradict each other inside the same bullet. Indeed's own published daily series (which I retrieved) gives Software Development total postings = 73.47 on 2026-06-30, i.e. 26.5% below the Feb-1-2020 baseline; the article's own text says 27.5% below. Neither is 'about 30%'. The series last stood ~30% below pre-pandemic in January 2026 (69.40), six months earlier. Overstating the shortfall by 3.5pp cuts against the article's own thesis and should be corrected downward.

(2) '7 日滚动平均' IS NOT SUPPORTED BY THIS SOURCE — 原表述:'季节调整,7 日滚动平均,2020-02-01 = 100' → 正确表述:'季节调整,2020-02-01 = 100(该文未提及 7 日滚动平均)'。The article describes its charts only as 'seasonally adjusted US job postings, indexed to 100 on February 1, 2020' and 'indexed to 100 on February 24, 2025'. A 7-day trailing average is part of Indeed's general JPI methodology but is not stated in this piece; the published data file is daily. Drop the claim or cite Indeed's methodology page separately.

(3) BASELINE SELECTION MUST BE DISCLOSED — this is the most important correction. 原表述 neutrally says '自 2025-02-24 起算美国软件开发岗位广告 +15%'. The article does not choose that date neutrally: its chart is captioned 'indexed to 100 on February 24, 2025, the launch of Claude Code', and the piece states 'Claude Code was introduced in late February, 2025' and 'the introduction of Claude Code and other widely available agentic AI tools at roughly the same time as software roles started to bounce back is a coincidence that cannot be ignored.' Two problems follow. First, the baseline is a single AI vendor's product-launch date, which builds the causal story into the measurement. Second, and decisively, THAT DATE IS ESSENTIALLY THE BOTTOM OF THE SERIES: in Indeed's own data the Software Development index reads 63.26 on 2025-02-24, and the monthly trough of the entire 2025–26 window is 62.79 (Feb 2025) / 62.57 (Mar 2025). Measuring the rebound from within one point of the all-time low mechanically maximizes it. Any article reusing '+15%' must say the baseline is the trough, or the reader will mistake a bounce off the floor for a return to health — the level is still ~26.5% below 2020.

(4) 71% 与 37% 不可相加 — the article states the two categories overlap ('senior roles account for 71% of the net increase ... and AI-related titles for 37%, with the two categories overlapping'). Also note both are shares of the NET INCREASE between May 2025 and May 2026, not shares of postings. As written the claim is accurate; the article must not let readers sum them to 108%.

VERIFIED AS STATED: the verbatim quote is exact — 'Even after the recent rise, software development job postings remain about 27.5% below their pre-pandemic level, while overall job postings are essentially the same as in February 2020.' ✓ 71% senior ✓ 37% AI-titled ✓ 'risen almost 15%' ✓ overall 'declined by 7%' ✓. JPI total postings = 101.0 on 2026-06-30 ✓ (exact value 100.96 SA; NSA 104.02).

利益相关 — the claim's flags are correct and I would add one. Indeed is a job-advertising platform whose commercial interest lies in job postings being a meaningful labor-market signal; the index covers only Indeed postings, so platform-share drift contaminates it, and duplicate/stale/never-filled postings are counted. Beyond that: this specific post (author Guillermo Gallacher, Hiring Lab, 2026-07-08) is structured as an AI-impact narrative anchored to a competitor-neutral but vendor-specific event, and its headline framing 'from destruction to creation' is a directional claim the underlying level data (still 26.5% below 2020) does not by itself establish.

**核验依据**:Article fetched directly: https://www.hiringlab.org/2026/07/08/ai-and-job-postings-from-destruction-to-creation/ ('AI and Job Postings: From Destruction to Creation', Guillermo Gallacher, July 8, 2026). All quoted sentences extracted from the raw HTML and matched character-for-character, including the key-points summary, the body text, and the three chart alt-text descriptions (which is where the indexing dates and the overlap disclosure appear).

I DID obtain the underlying series the collector could not — not via FRED (fredgraph.csv returned empty and the series page was unreachable) but from Indeed Hiring Lab's own public data repository, which is the upstream source FRED republishes: https://github.com/hiring-lab/job_postings_tracker — files US/job_postings_by_sector_US.csv (10.4 MB) and US/aggregate_job_postings_US.csv.

Software Development, 'total postings', index Feb-1-2020 = 100, last observation of each month:
2025-01 66.90 | 2025-02 62.79 | 2025-03 62.57 | 2025-04 63.27 | 2025-05 63.98 | 2025-06 65.56 | 2025-07 66.04 | 2025-08 65.25 | 2025-09 64.29 | 2025-10 65.90 | 2025-11 66.62 | 2025-12 67.31 | 2026-01 69.40 | 2026-02 70.87 | 2026-03 72.89 | 2026-04 72.61 | 2026-05 73.55 | 2026-06 73.47 | 2026-07-17 75.62 (partial month).
Spot values: 2025-02-24 = 63.26; 2026-06-30 = 73.47 → +16.1% over the article's stated window (the article says 'almost 15%', so its own figure is conservative relative to the published data — likely a slightly different endpoint or SA variant; either way the claim reproduces the article faithfully and the article understates rather than overstates).

Aggregate total postings (seasonally adjusted): 2025-02-24 = 108.97; 2026-06-30 = 100.96 → -7.4%, matching the article's '7%' decline, and confirming the claim's 'JPI = 101.0 (2026-06-30)'. NSA on that date = 104.02.

The monthly table above is the series the collector was asked to supply and could not; it is reproducible from the CSV above.


### 第 3 席


#### [G14] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:取得了报告正文 PDF(https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/cew-the_major_payoff.pdf)。多处口径需修正:

(1) 最严重 —— 「对比 2013–15 的 4.3%」→ 报告正文第36页原文是「unemployment among recent graduates with a bachelor's degree in computer science is 7.2 percent, up from a low of **5.3 percent in 2014**」(脚注66: ACS 2013–15 pooled 与 2021–23 pooled)。而 CEW 博客(https://cew.georgetown.edu/resource/cew-blog-major-payoff/)写的是「7.2 percent in 2021–23, up from **4.3 percent** in 2013–15」。**同一机构两份出版物对同一统计量给出 5.3% 与 4.3% 两个互相矛盾的数字**。该起点值不可承重;若使用必须同时标注冲突,或干脆只用 7.2% 而不用涨幅叙事。

(2)「应届收入区间 $34,000(communication disorders)至 $86,000(computer science)」→ 这是博客/在线工具口径,报告正文不支持。正文 Figure 5/6(ACS **2009–23** pooled、2024年美元)给出:最低 $37,000(zoology),communication disorders sciences and services 为 **$39,000**;最高 **$98,000(petroleum engineering)**;computer science 是 **$83,000**;**$86,000 属于 computer engineering 与 chemical engineering**,不是 computer science。把 $86,000 说成「computer science 且为区间上限」双重错误。

(3)「应届失业率区间 1.3%–11%」→ 同样只见于博客/数据工具。报告正文 Appendix A 明确「We report a total of **16 detailed fields**」,脚注8:「Our analysis of the unemployment rate among bachelor's degree holders is aggregated at the **detailed field level** due to small sample sizes within some majors」。正文 Figure 13 的应届 field 级区间是 **2.8%(education)–8.9%(arts)**。引用 1.3%/11% 必须注明来源是在线数据工具而非报告,且与报告自陈的「专业层级样本量不足」方法论相抵触。

(4)「152 个专业」→ 152 是 prime-age 口径;**应届(recent college graduates)只有 142 个专业**(Appendix A 原文)。

(5)「computers/statistics/mathematics 大类」→ 报告术语是 **detailed field**(16 个之一),不是最高层 field(8 个)。「6.8% 为 STEM 内最高」成立,但全部 detailed field 中最高的是 arts 的 **8.9%**,需避免误读成「最高」。

(6) 收入时间窗必须分开:$79,000/$107,000 是 ACS **2021–23 pooled**、**已就业且全职全年**者(脚注64);而 Figure 5/6/Table 3 的收入是 ACS **2009–23 pooled**。同一报告内收入用了两套窗口。

(7) prime-age 定义 = **25–54 岁**。$81,000/70% 溢价 = ACS **2009–23 pooled**、**限全职全年、仅学士学位**(脚注7);失业率 2.9% vs 6.2% = ACS **2021–23 pooled**、劳动力口径(「excludes those who are not in the labor force」)、**不限全职**(脚注6)。收入与失业率**不是同一时间窗**,论断把两者并列陈述会误导。

(8)「削弱了纯粹是样本噪声的解释」这一关键作用需大幅弱化 → NY Fed 的 by-major 结果同样取自 **American Community Survey**,与 CEW 是**同一套微观数据**的不同切片(不同年龄窗、不同池化),**不构成独立测量**。三年池化确实降低抽样方差,但 ACS 专业自报编码、ACS 失业定义等共同误差不会因换机构而抵消。应改为「同一数据源的不同切法给出同向结果,降低了单年抽样噪声的可能,但不是独立复算」。

(9) 立场标注:CEW 是 Georgetown McCourt 公共政策学院下属的研究/政策机构,报告基调明确亲学位(正文:「the evidence overwhelmingly indicates that a bachelor's degree is a worthwhile investment in the long run」),需标注。

已核实无误的部分:recent college graduates = **22–26 岁** ✓;失业率确为 **2021–23 三年合并** ✓(Appendix A:「The unemployment rate for each year represents a pooled three-year average to address sample size concerns」,且 CEW 把 2021–23 池化值标注为「2022」);CS 应届 7.2% ✓;computers/statistics/mathematics 6.8% 且为 STEM 内最高 ✓;$79,000/$107,000 ✓;**159%** ✓(Appendix C1: IPEDS Completions,2009 = 53,499 → 2023 = 138,755,为 16 个 detailed field 中最快,healthcare 109% 次之);Ban Cheah 两段逐字引语与新闻稿完全一致 ✓;(c) **Computer engineering 确有单列**(Table 3,应届中位 $86,000/prime-age $121,000)—— 但须警告 Table 3 中 computer engineering 旁的「13%」是它占该 detailed field 应届人数的**份额**,不是失业率(computer science 42%、mathematics 14%)。

**核验依据**:一手件:报告正文 PDF https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/cew-the_major_payoff.pdf (已下载全文提取)。第36页原文:「unemployment among recent graduates with a bachelor's degree in computer science is 7.2 percent, up from a low of 5.3 percent in 2014.」脚注66:「ACS, 2013–15 (pooled) and 2021–23 (pooled)」。Appendix A(第56页):「We report a total of 152 majors for prime-age workers and 142 majors for recent college graduates」「We report a total of 16 detailed fields」「The unemployment rate for each year represents a pooled three-year average」。脚注8:失业率仅按 detailed field 汇总。Figure 5:petroleum engineering $98,000 / chemical engineering $86,000 / computer engineering $86,000 / computer science $83,000。Figure 6:zoology $37,000 最低,communication disorders sciences and services $39,000。Figure 13:应届 arts 8.9% > humanities 6.7% = social sciences 6.7% > computers/statistics/mathematics 6.8%(STEM 内最高),education 2.8% 最低。Table 3 第13%列标题为「Distribution by detailed major」。Appendix C1:computers/statistics/mathematics 2009 = 53,499 → 2023 = 138,755 = 159%。新闻稿 https://cew.georgetown.edu/wp-content/uploads/GeorgetownCEW_PressRelease_Major-Payoff_10.16.25.pdf 逐字核对 Cheah 引语一致。博客 https://cew.georgetown.edu/resource/cew-blog-major-payoff/ 原文:「up from 4.3 percent in 2013–15」「ranging from a low of 1.3 percent ... operations logistics and e-commerce to a high of 11 percent ... film, video, and photographic arts」「a low of $34,000 ... communication disorders sciences and services to a high of $86,000 ... computer science」—— 与正文冲突。NY Fed 数据源(ACS)见 https://www.newyorkfed.org/research/college-labor-market。


#### [G15] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:逐格核对 https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d23/tables/dt23_325.35.asp 全表(1964-65 至 2021-22 逐年),数字**全部正确**,但两处需修正、一处需补充:

(1)「1993-94 年 24,527(谷)」→ **1993-94 不是该轮最低点**。同表中 1992-93 = **24,519**、1995-96 = **24,506**,均低于 1993-94 的 24,527。该轮低谷是 1992-93 至 1995-96 的**平台期**,最低值出现在 **1995-96(24,506)**。应改为「谷底平台 1992-93～1995-96,最低 24,506(1995-96)」。跌幅百分比不受影响(以 24,527 算 −42.07%,以 24,506 算 −42.11%,均为 −42.1%)。

(2)「可能部分反映 CIP 11 口径扩张(**数据科学等新专业被归入**)」→ 括号里的例子**是错的**。CIP 2020 把 **Data Science 编在 30.70、Data Analytics 编在 30.71**,均属 **CIP 30 Multi/Interdisciplinary Studies**,因此数据科学学位**不计入**表 325.35。口径警告的前半(CIP 11「Computer and Information Sciences and Support Services」范围大于 computer science 本身,含 11.01 一般计算机与信息科学、11.0103 信息技术、11.04 信息科学、11.09 网络、11.10 信息技术管理等)**成立且重要**,但「数据科学被归入」这个具体佐证必须删除。另需补:该表**全部年份统一按 2020 CIP 编制**,表注并声明「Some data have been revised from previously published figures」,这部分(非全部)缓解了口径扩张的担忧 —— HEGIS 时代(1970-71～1985-86)与 IPEDS 时代的映射仍不完美。

(3) 更新版:**没有更晚年份可补**。dt24_325.35 与 dt25_325.35 均返回 **404**,2024/2025 menu_tables 中不含 325.x 章节表(第3章尚未发布)。**d23 仍是该表最新版**,表注「This table was prepared November 2023」,覆盖至 2021-22。

(4) 「2021-22 为 provisional data」**成立**,表注原文:「Completions component, IPEDS Fall 2000 through Fall 2021 (final data) and **Fall 2022 (provisional data)**」。

已逐格核实无误:1985-86 = 42,337(确为该轮峰值,1984-85 = 39,121、1986-87 = 39,767);2003-04 = 59,488(确为该轮峰值,2002-03 = 57,433、2004-05 = 54,111);2008-09 = 37,992(确为谷,2007-08 = 38,523、2009-10 = 39,593);2009-10 = 39,593;2013-14 = 55,271;2017-18 = 79,597;2019-20 = 97,054;2020-21 = 104,883;2021-22 = 108,503。计算全部正确:39,593→108,503 = +174.0%;42,337→24,527 = −42.1%;59,488→37,992 = −36.1%;37,992→108,503 = +185.6%(≈+186%)。滞后期论断成立:互联网泡沫破裂 2000 年 3 月,学位峰值 2003-04 学年(2004 年春毕业),约 4 年,与「入学后四年毕业」的机制一致。

**核验依据**:一手件:https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/d23/tables/dt23_325.35.asp 「Table 325.35. Degrees in computer and information sciences conferred by postsecondary institutions, by level of degree and sex of student: Academic years 1964-65 through 2021-22」。已抓取原始 HTML 并解析全部 58 行逐年数据(见上列数字)。关键相邻年份:1992-93 = 24,519、1993-94 = 24,527、1994-95 = 24,737、1995-96 = 24,506。表注原文:「Data in this table are based on the 2020 Classification of Instructional Programs. Some data have been revised from previously published figures.」;来源行:「... IPEDS Fall 2000 through Fall 2021 (final data) and Fall 2022 (provisional data). (This table was prepared November 2023.)」。更新版探测:dt24_325.35.asp = HTTP 404、dt25_325.35.asp = HTTP 404、dt25_325.10.asp = HTTP 404;https://nces.ed.gov/programs/digest/2025menu_tables.asp 返回 200 但不含 325.x。CIP 归属:https://nces.ed.gov/ipeds/cipcode/cipdetail.aspx?y=56&cipid=92951 (30.70 Data Science)、cipid=92953 (30.7001 Data Science, General) —— 属 CIP 30 而非 CIP 11。


#### [G16] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:三处一手件全部逐格/逐字核对通过(bls.gov 直连 403,改用 Wayback 快照)。

论断A —— OOH「Software Developers, Quality Assurance Analysts, and Testers」,快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20260717110649/https://www.bls.gov/ooh/computer-and-information-technology/software-developers.htm 的 Quick Facts 逐格:2024 Median Pay $131,450/年($63.20/小时);Number of Jobs, 2024 = 1,895,500;Job Outlook, 2024–34 = 15% (Much faster than average);Employment Change, 2024–34 = 287,900。逐字引语完全一致:「Overall employment of software developers, quality assurance analysts, and testers is projected to grow 15 percent from 2024 to 2034, much faster than the average for all occupations.」年均空缺:「About 129,200 openings ... are projected each year, on average, over the decade.」全部命中。补一条可用的对照:该页另给 software developers 单独 median $133,080、QA analysts and testers 单独 $102,610(May 2024),$131,450 是合并组数。

论断B —— OOH「Computer Programmers」,快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20260710181800/https://www.bls.gov/ooh/computer-and-information-technology/computer-programmers.htm:2024 Median Pay $98,670;Number of Jobs, 2024 = 121,200;Job Outlook, 2024–34 = -6% (Decline);Employment Change, 2024–34 = -7,200。逐字引语完全一致:「Despite declining employment, about 5,500 openings for computer programmers are projected each year, on average, over the decade.」及「All of those openings are expected to result from the need to replace workers who transfer to other occupations or exit the labor force, such as to retire.」全部命中。

论断C —— MLR 2026年1月,快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20260714170856/https://www.bls.gov/opub/mlr/2026/article/industry-and-occupational-employment-projections-overview.htm。三处引语逐字核对全部一致:「Not only are they projected to experience employment growth that is over 5 times faster than the all-occupation average (15.8 percent), but they are also projected to have the second-largest increase in employment of all occupations with 267,700 new jobs added through 2034.」;「The growing adoption of AI technologies, including generative AI tools, is another factor that will fuel strong job growth among computer and mathematical occupations.」;「The growing adoption of AI technologies, including generative AI tools, and resulting productivity gains are expected to dampen labor demand in a variety of fields, such as sales, design, and administrative support.」

冲突待解已核实为真且论断的处理正确:OOH 的 15%/+287,900 是 **三个 SOC 合并组**(software developers 15-1252 + QA analysts and testers 15-1253);MLR 的 15.8%/+267,700 是 **software developers 单一 SOC**。两者差额约 20,200 与 QA/testers 规模相符,内部自洽。论断要求「必须在文章中区分」是正确的。

唯一可加精度的细节(不影响判决):论断写「全职业平均增速 3%」,MLR 原文给的是 **3.1 percent**(「Total employment is projected to grow 3.1 percent between 2024 and 2034, increasing from 170.0 million to 175.2 million, for a total of 5.2 million additional new jobs.」),四舍五入无误,建议直接写 3.1% 更严谨。


#### [G17] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:逐字引语与四个结构数字全部核实通过,但三处表述需收紧;并已补齐采集者未取得的 FRED 序列。

(1)「自 2025-02-24 起算美国软件开发岗位广告 **+15%**」→ 原文两处均为「**almost** 15%」(Key points:「US software development job postings have grown by almost 15% since the launch of Claude Code in late February, 2025」;图注:「software development postings have risen almost 15%」),即**略低于** 15%。写成「+15%」是向上取整。另需注意:FRED 序列实测 2025-02-24 = 63.26 → 2026-06-30 = 73.47 为 **+16.1%**,与文中「almost 15%」不符 —— 因为 Indeed 的季调因子会回溯修订,发文时的数据切片与当前 FRED 值不同。引用时应写「接近 15%(Hiring Lab 2026-07-08 口径)」并注明该指数会被回溯修订。

(2)「software development ≈ 73(较 2020-02 **低约 30%**)」→ 应为**低约 27.5%**。原文明确「about 27.5% below their pre-pandemic level」;FRED 2026-06-30 = **73.47**,2026 年 6 月区间 72.33–73.62,对应低 26.4%–27.7%。「约 30%」高估约 2.5 个百分点,在「CS 已饱和」这类判断上属于不该放过的含糊。指数值「≈73」本身正确。

(3)「增量中 71% 来自资深岗,37% 来自标题含 AI 的岗位」→ 必须补两个限定:两者都是 **2025-05 至 2026-05 净增量(net increase)的份额**,且**两类互相重叠**。原文图注:「senior roles account for 71% of the net increase ... and AI-related titles for 37%, **with the two categories overlapping**」。不可相加,也不可推出「其余 -8% 来自初级岗」。

(4) 建议补一条口径警告(论断的利益相关段还不够):Indeed 的「software development」是 **Indeed 自有的、基于标准化职位标题(normalized job titles)的 sector 分类**,不是 SOC/官方职业分类,因此**不能与 BLS 的 software developers 直接对齐** —— 这正是 G16 与 G17 能同时为真的技术原因之一,值得写明。

已核实无误的部分:逐字引语与原文**完全一致**:「Even after the recent rise, software development job postings remain about 27.5% below their pre-pandemic level, while overall job postings are essentially the same as in February 2020.」;全部岗位 **−7%** ✓(FRED IHLIDXUS 2025-02-24 = 108.97 → 2026-06-30 = 100.96,即 −7.35%);**JPI 2026-06-30 = 100.96**(论断写 101.0,四舍五入正确);指数口径 **2020-02-01 = 100、季节调整、7 日 trailing average** 三项全部属实(Indeed 官方方法说明);71%/37% 数字本身正确 ✓;文章日期 2026-07-08、作者 Guillermo Gallacher ✓。

**核验依据**:一手件:https://www.hiringlab.org/2026/07/08/ai-and-job-postings-from-destruction-to-creation/ (直接抓取 HTML 并提取正文,逐字比对上述引语与图注)。原文 Key points 三条:「US software development job postings have grown by almost 15% since the launch of Claude Code in late February, 2025, while overall job postings fell by 7% over the same period.」/「71% of the increase in software development job postings between May 2025 and May 2026 is from senior roles, and 37% is due to jobs that mention AI in their title.」正文图注:「... senior roles account for 71% of the net increase in US software development postings between May 2025 and May 2026, and AI-related titles for 37%, with the two categories overlapping.」

FRED 序列(采集者未取得,现补齐):IHLIDXUSTPSOFTDEVE via https://fred.stlouisfed.org/graph/fredgraph.csv?id=IHLIDXUSTPSOFTDEVE —— 2020-02-01 = 100.00;2025-02-24 = 63.26;2025-05-31 = 63.98;2026-05-31 = 73.55;2026-06-01 = 73.44;2026-06-30 = 73.47(6月最低 72.33、最高 73.62);2026-07-17 = 75.62(最新)。全部岗位 IHLIDXUS —— 2025-02-24 = 108.97;2026-06-30 = 100.96;2026-07-17 = 101.54。

方法论一手件:https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hiring-lab/job_postings_tracker/master/README.md —— 确认 February 1, 2020 = 100 基准、seven-day trailing average、按地区与 sector 分别季调(2024年11月起改用 Deutsche Bundesbank 日频季调法并回溯修订历史数据)、且 sector 为「an Indeed categorization based on normalized job titles」。


## 批次 C1 — verify:AI暴露度构念


### 第 1 席


#### [G18] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 归属错误(最重要):3% 与 49% 两个数字**不在任何一版 arXiv 摘要里**。我逐版拉取了 v1/v3/v4/v5 摘要:v1、v3 摘要只有 80%/19%;v4、v5 摘要是 80%/19% + 「about 15% of all worker tasks」/「between 47 and 56% of all tasks」。3%/49% 出现在正文第 1 节 Introduction。原表述「arXiv v5 摘要:…人工标注下『仅 3%』『最多 49%』」→ 正确表述「arXiv v5 **摘要**给出 80%/19% 与 15%/47–56%(后者是**任务**占比);3%/49% 出自 v5 **正文 Introduction**,原文为 'Human assessments suggest that only 3% of U.S. workers have over half of their tasks exposed to LLMs when considering existing language and code capabilities without additional software or modalities... our human estimates indicate that up to 49% of workers could have half or more of their tasks exposed'」。(2) 分母单位不一致:arXiv 说 workers(劳动者,就业加权);Science 说 **jobs**。同一篇论文里还有第三套口径——未加权的**职业**层面:'human annotators determined that 2.3% of occupations are α50-exposed, 21.6% are β50-exposed, and 47.3% are ζ50-exposed'。写给准大学生时必须点明 3%/2.3%/1.8% 是三个不同分母。(3) Science 版并非「同一篇的正式版」:它是 3 页的 **Policy Forum**(页面标注 'No access Policy Forum Artificial Intelligence'),不是完整研究论文;其摘要**完全没有** 80%/19%,唯一的两个数字就是 1.8% 和 46%。(4) 日期:Science 网页署 20 Jun 2024,Crossref published-print 为 2024-06-21。(5) 论断 A 暗示的「两个版本两套数字」需精确化:1.8%/46% 对应的是 arXiv **正文**的 3%/49%(即 α50 与 ζ50),而非摘要的 80%/19%。(6) 且这不是数据版本差异——两版都用 O*NET 27.2(arXiv 正文 'We use the O*NET 27.2 database (O*NET, 2023)';Science 参考文献 9 'O*NET, O*NET 27.2 Database'),所以 3%→1.8%、49%→46% 是**真实下修**,不是口径漂移。(7) 论断 C 的时间线免责声明需加限定:'We do not make predictions about the development or adoption timeline of such LLMs.' 是 **v4 才加入摘要**的,v1/v3 摘要没有这句。

**核验依据**:一手 PDF:https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5(已下载,pdftotext 逐字核)。论断 B 全部逐字命中:§3.3 'we define exposure as a measure of whether access to an LLM or LLM-powered system would reduce the time required for a human to perform a specific DWA or complete a task by at least 50 percent';E0/E1/E2 三档 rubric 完整(E0 含 'while maintaining equivalent quality');三指标原文 '(i) α, corresponding to E1... (ii) β, which is the sum of E1 and 0.5*E2... (iii) ζ, the sum of E1 and E2, an upper bound';**β 是摘要 80%/19% 的基础已逐字坐实**——§4.1 'Based on the β values, we estimate that 80% of workers belong to an occupation with at least 10% of its tasks exposed to LLMs, while 19% of workers are in an occupation where over half of its tasks are labeled as exposed.';阈值自陈 'Although this threshold is somewhat arbitrary, it was selected for ease of interpretation by annotators.'。论断 C 四处免责全部逐字命中:①§1 'We define exposure as a proxy for potential economic impact without distinguishing between labor-augmenting or labor-displacing effects.' ②§1 'This exposure measure reflects an estimate of the technical capacity to make human labor more efficient; however, social, economic, regulatory, and other determinants imply that technical feasibility does not guarantee labor productivity or automation outcomes.' ③Table 4 脚注 '...but it does not necessarily suggest that their tasks can be fully automated by these technologies.' ④摘要 'We do not make predictions about the development or adoption timeline of such LLMs.'。Science 版**已取得一手原文**(非二手):经 Wayback id_ 原始快照 http://web.archive.org/web/20250916160519id_/https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adj0998 取得摘要全文,逐字为 'By applying this framework (with both humans and using an LLM), we estimate that roughly 1.8% of jobs could have over half their tasks affected by LLMs with simple interfaces and general training. When accounting for current and likely future software developments that complement LLM capabilities, this share jumps to just over 46% of jobs.',页面标注 'Science 20 Jun 2024 Vol 384, Issue 6702 pp. 1306 - 1308'、'No access Policy Forum'。书目经 Crossref API 独立复核(https://api.crossref.org/works/10.1126/science.adj0998):issue 6702、page 1306-1308、published-print 2024-06-21。故**不判 UNVERIFIABLE**。RePEc 复核:https://ideas.repec.org/p/arx/papers/2303.10130.html 至今仍著录为 'Papers 2303.10130, arXiv.org, revised Aug 2023',**无 Published in / 期刊卷期字段指向 Science 2024**,论断属实。


#### [G19] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:一手 PDF arXiv 2303.10130v5 逐格核对。Table 2 六个数字**全部精确命中**,原表为:GPT-4 Rubric 1 vs Human — α/E1 80.8%/0.223、β/E1+.5*E2 65.6%/0.591、ζ/E1+E2 82.1%/0.654;GPT-4 Rubric 2 vs Human — α 81.8%/0.221、β 65.6%/0.538、ζ 79.5%/0.589。故「一致率 65.6%–82.1%」覆盖全部人机比较行,属实;「r 最低 0.221」属实(出自 Rubric 2 的 α 行,而论文自身分析采用 Rubric 1,其 α 的 r=0.223——两者实质等同,不构成错误,仅提示两个数来自不同行)。表注原文 'In the paper we use GPT-4, Rubric 1.'。标注者身份逐字命中:§3.3 'enlisted experienced human annotators who have reviewed GPT-3, GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 outputs as part of OpenAI's alignment work (Ouyang et al., 2022)',脚注 5 另称 'the contracted annotators'——「OpenAI 自己的对齐标注承包商」成立。四处自陈全部逐字命中:①§3.4.1 'A fundamental limitation of our approach lies in the subjectivity of the labeling... However, this group is not occupationally diverse, potentially leading to biased judgments regarding LLMs' reliability and effectiveness in performing tasks within unfamiliar occupations.' ②§3.4.2 'Consequently, there are slight differences between the rubric presented to humans and the one used for GPT-4... As a result, we use multiple annotation sources, but none should be considered the definitive ground truth relative to the others.' ③§3.4.3 'Human annotators were mostly unaware of the specific occupations mapped to each DWA during the labeling process.' ④§3.4.3 'If indeed, the task-based breakdown is not a valid representation of how most work in an occupation is performed, our exposure analysis would largely be invalidated.'。86 vs 15 逐字命中:Table 4 内两行分别为 'Humans labeled 15 occupations as "fully exposed."' 与 'The model labeled 86 occupations as "fully exposed."',86/15=5.73,「5.7 倍」属实。Barbers 逐字命中:§4.1 '...yields some curious results (e.g. ranking Barbers as having reasonably high exposure)'。名单重叠核对结果比论断更强:Table 4 同一指标下人机 top-5 **零重叠**——Human α(Interpreters and Translators / Survey Researchers / Poets, Lyricists and Creative Writers / Animal Scientists / Public Relations Specialists)vs Model α(Mathematicians / Correspondence Clerks / Blockchain Engineers / Court Reporters and Simultaneous Captioners / Proofreaders and Copy Markers);β、ζ 同样零重叠。故「几乎不重叠」成立(实为完全不重叠)。**须记录的反向平衡证据(论断未提,不改判但影响承重)**:同一 §3.4.2 结尾作者写 'Still, we observe a high degree of agreement between human ratings and GPT-4 ratings at the occupation level concerning overall exposure to LLM systems (see Table 2, Figure 2).',且 §3.4.3 称最大匹配法下 'the agreement remained relatively consistent'——引用此条时若只取缺陷不取作者的抗辩,会高估该缺陷的破坏力。


#### [G20] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:逐字核回 anthropic.com 各期原页后,多数数字属实,但有 5 处口径需修正,其中 1 处是实质性错误:(1)**实质错误**:「2025-09 … automation 首次越过 50%」→ 应为「automation 首次**超过 augmentation**,但**并未越过 50%**」。九月报告原文是 'This is the first report where automation usage exceeds augmentation usage.',而其脚注 4 明确给出绝对值 '45% automation versus 49% with Sonnet 4';正文另处称 Claude.ai 'about 50%'、'the split between automation and augmentation is nearly even'。因存在「以上皆非」类别,49% 可以在不过半的情况下超过 augmentation。「越过 50%」是无中生有的阈值。(2)「跨期不可比」→ 应为「作者自陈 'complicates direct comparison',但已做稳健性补救」。论断只截取了半句,原文完整为:'We note that V3 uses Claude Sonnet 4 for classification, while V2 used Sonnet 3.7, which complicates direct comparison. **To address this, we reran V3 data with Sonnet 3.7 and still found directive interactions rising significantly (though to a lower absolute level of 45% automation versus 49% with Sonnet 4). We also verified this trend is not driven by changes in task mix**...'。「complicates」≠「不可比」。(3)论断 C 犹他州:引语逐字无误,但同一脚注紧接着写 'However, we ran robustness checks and believe that this activity is not driving the results.' — 称其为「自曝数据污染」而不带这句,属选择性引用。(4)论断 D「22–25 岁 job-finding rate 下降 14%」:数字逐字无误,但原文限定语被删——'The averaged estimate in the post-ChatGPT era is a 14% drop in the job finding rate compared to that in 2022 in the exposed occupations, **although this is just barely statistically significant**.'(且该报告 2026-03-08 发过更正:'Corrected Figure 7, which incorrectly reversed the labels between top quartile and zero exposure group inflow rates.')。面向准大学生时删掉「勉强显著」等于把一条边缘结果包装成事实。(5)「2025-02:automation 42.6% / augmentation 57.4%」→ **这两个小数在一手来源中查无实据**。Anthropic 2025-02-10 公告页与 arXiv:2503.04761 一致给出的都是整数 57%/43%(公告页 'AI use leans more toward augmentation (57%)... compared to automation (43%)';论文正文 'slightly more conversations being labeled as augmentative (57%) than automative (43%)')。42.6/57.4 疑似来自 HuggingFace 数据集聚合,引用时应改回 57%/43% 或标明出处。另需补两条论断漏掉的关键限制条款:①样本是 **7 天快照**——'We use snapshots of Claude.ai Free and Pro conversations over 7-day periods';②首期**排除全部商业客户**——'the results shared in this paper exclude activity from business customers (i.e., Team, Enterprise, and all API customers)',这一点直接决定了后来 Claude.ai 与 1P API 的对比不是同一总体。还有:「首期约 100 万条(配套论文 400 万+)」的对举略失准——100 万正是论文**自身**任务映射分析的样本('Using Clio on a dataset of one million Claude.ai Free and Pro conversations'),摘要的 'over four million' 是跨各项分析的合计(聚类生成另用约 280 万条)。

**核验依据**:逐页 curl 抓取原始 HTML 后自行剥标签核对(未依赖 WebFetch 摘要器)。**2025-02-10 公告**(https://www.anthropic.com/news/the-anthropic-economic-index):'approximately one million conversations with Claude (specifically, Free and Pro conversations on Claude.ai)'、'around 20,000 specific work-related tasks'、'roughly 36%'、'approximately 4% of occupations use it across three-quarters'——均属实。**arXiv:2503.04761**(已下载 PDF 逐字核)摘要四句引语全部命中:'we present a novel framework...to analyze over four million Claude.ai conversations'、'AI usage primarily concentrates in software development and writing tasks, which together account for nearly half of all total usage'、'with ∼36% of occupations using AI for at least a quarter of their associated tasks'、'finding 57% of usage suggests augmentation of human capabilities... while 43% suggests automation'、'While our data and methods face important limitations and only paint a picture of AI usage on a single platform...'。37.2% 命中且限定为 'comprising 37.2% of all queries';~4% 命中,并附阈值定义 'Task usage is defined as occurrence across five or more unique user accounts and fifteen or more conversations'。**2025-09 报告**(https://www.anthropic.com/research/anthropic-economic-index-september-2025-report):'jumped from 27% to 39%'、'The share of directive conversations sampled from Claude.ai conversations jumped from 27% in V1 in late 2024 to 39% in V3'、'77% of API transcripts show automation patterns... versus just 12% for augmentation'、'97% of tasks show automation-dominant patterns in API usage, compared to only 47% on Claude.ai'——全部逐字命中。犹他州原文 'we discovered a notable fraction of its usage appeared to be possibly associated with coordinated abuse' 逐字命中。**2026-03 Learning curves**(https://www.anthropic.com/research/economic-index-march-2026-report):'Figure 1.3 shows that augmentation in Claude.ai increased slightly...
