In Appendix Figure A.3, we show that automation decreased sharply in the 1P API data.' 逐字命中。**2026-06 Cadences**(https://www.anthropic.com/research/economic-index-june-2026-report):'Our final linked sample consists of about 9,700 survey respondents.'、'The Economic Index Survey is not representative of the general population.'、'Computer and Mathematical occupations are the most heavily over-represented, making up roughly 30% of survey respondents—comparable to their share of Claude usage, but far above their 4% share of US employment.' 三处逐字命中。**2026-03-05 Labor market impacts**(https://www.anthropic.com/research/labor-market-impacts,Massenkoff & McCrory):'Computer Programmers are at the top, with 75% coverage'、'30% of workers have zero coverage'、'For every 10 percentage point increase in coverage, the BLS's growth projection drops by 0.6 percentage points'、'The average change in the gap since the release of ChatGPT is small and insignificant'——均逐字命中。**同段还有一句对 G21 极关键、论断未收录的话**:'Interestingly, there is no such correlation using the Eloundou et al. measure alone.' —— 即该 0.6pp 关系只存在于 Anthropic 自建的 coverage 复合指标,用 Eloundou 口径反而不成立。**利益相关核实**:确为厂商自有数据 + 自有分类器(Claude 给 Claude 的对话打标)+ 自行发布,且样本仅覆盖自家平台(其摘要已自陈 'a single platform')。CEO 反向表态属实:Amodei 2025-05 对 Axios 称 AI 可能在 1–5 年内消灭半数入门级白领岗、失业率升至 10–20%(https://www.axios.com/2025/05/28/ai-jobs-white-collar-unemployment-anthropic;该页被反爬阻断,经多家二手报道一致复述,故此条我按二手记录、不逐字引用),而公司研究部门 2026-03 的结论是 'no systematic increase in unemployment for highly exposed workers since late 2022'。方向确实相反;并且就在今日(2026-07-24)Fortune 刊出 'Anthropic's head of economics just explained why Anthropic's CEO was wrong about a white-collar bloodbath'(https://fortune.com/2026/07/24/anthropic-peter-mccrory-dario-amodei-why-hasnt-it-killed-jobs/),该分歧已公开化。需要向读者点明的推论:此处的利益冲突方向是**反直觉的**——厂商研究结论与本厂商 CEO 的营销叙事相悖,因此不宜简单套用「厂商发布故而夸大」的怀疑框架;真正的偏差风险在样本(仅自家平台、7 天快照、早期排除企业客户)而非动机。


#### [G21] HOLDS · confidence=high

**核验依据**:两篇 PDF 均已下载并 pdftotext 逐字核对。**Canaries(2025-11-13 版,https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/app/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf)§3.2 Occupational AI Exposure 逐字**:'The first uses exposure measures from Eloundou et al. (2024), who estimate AI exposure by O*NET task using ChatGPT validated with human labeling. They construct occupational exposure measures by aggregating the task data to the 2018 SOC code level. **We focus on the GPT-4 based β exposure measures from their paper.**' 紧接着:'The second approach we take uses generative AI usage data from the Anthropic Economic Index (Handa et al., 2025)... We use this as an estimate of whether AI usage for an occupation is primarily complementary or substitutable with labor.' 以及 'We use a 2010 SOC code to 2018 SOC code crosswalk from the BLS to merge the exposure measures to the payroll data.' —— 论断三项全部逐字坐实。**EIG(2026-01,https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf,Iscenko & Curto Millet《Looking for the Ladder》)脚注逐字**:'We calculate AI exposure quintiles using the data and code provided in the replication package for Eloundou et al. (2024), using GPT-4 β scores and an equal weight scheme. **We understand this is the same basis for exposure calculations as was used in Brynjolfsson et al. (2025).**' —— 反驳方与被反驳方共用同一套构念,论断的「假独立」判断不仅成立,而且是 EIG 自己写明的。**安慰剂问题逐字命中**(Robustness 章 'Longer Sample' 段,配 Figure A16):'A concern is that for the Eloundou et al. (2024) measures the most exposed quintile had slower employment growth starting around 2020. This is not the case for the Anthropic exposure measures, shown in Figures A17, A18, and A19.'。**0.01%/0.09% 逐字命中**(脚注 17):'Occupations in the first two augmentation quintiles have very low Claude usage (0.01% and 0.09% of conversations for the average occupation, respectively), with a high share of conversations classified as neither automative nor augmentative.' —— 数字无误;须保留的限定语是「**for the average occupation**」(指该五分位内平均每个职业占全部对话的比重),论断转述时丢了这五个词。同一脚注还给出第三至第五五分位为 0.47%/0.39%/0.33%,以及作者的补救:'Figures A9 and A10 show that automation and augmentation results are similar when dropping occupations with low overall Claude usage.'。**两点必须随该论断一并记录的加强/修正**:(甲)**加强**——Canaries 用的是 **GPT-4 β**,而 Eloundou 自己声明 'In this analysis, we present results from human annotators as our primary results.';换言之 Canaries 继承的正是 G19 所批评的那一套机器标注(与人类 β 的 Pearson r 仅 0.591、把 86 个职业标为 fully exposed,是人工 15 个的 5.7 倍)。Canaries 将其描述为 'ChatGPT validated with human labeling',与原作者的主次关系是倒置的。(乙)**边界修正**——「假独立」链条覆盖 Eloundou ↔ Canaries ↔ EIG ↔ Anthropic Economic Index(用量数据),但**不覆盖** Anthropic 2026-03-05 劳动力市场报告的 coverage 指标:该指标是「理论可行性 × 实际用量 × 自动化加权」的复合量,且报告明言 'Interestingly, there is no such correlation using the Eloundou et al. measure alone.'。故若把 G20 D 也算进「共享同一构念」会过度延伸。(丙)版本提示:EIG 自陈其评论针对的是 2025-08-26 初版 Canaries('Our comments in this note were written about the original version of the "Canaries" paper published on 26 August 2025'),我核的是 2025-11-13 版;我另探测了 2026 年 1–6 月的 Stanford 上传路径,均 404,未见更新版。


### 第 2 席


#### [G18] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:三处口径必须改。

(1) 归属错误 —— 3%/49% 不在 v5 摘要里。原表述:「arXiv v5 摘要:80%…19%…;人工标注下『仅3%…』、『最多49%』」→ 正确表述:「arXiv v5 摘要只有 80%/19%,以及『about 15% of all worker tasks』和『between 47 and 56% of all tasks』(注意单位是 tasks,不是 workers)。3% 和 49% 出现在正文引言(PDF 第2页),原文为:『Human assessments suggest that only 3% of U.S. workers have over half of their tasks exposed to LLMs when considering existing language and code capabilities without additional software or modalities. Accounting for other generative models and complementary technologies, our human estimates indicate that up to 49% of workers could have half or more of their tasks exposed to LLMs.』」

(2) Science 版是修订+换单位,不是同一口径的另一种说法。原表述:「Science 正式版给出的是 1.8% 和 46%」→ 补足为:「Science 版把 arXiv 的 3%→1.8%、49%→46%,且单位从 workers 改为 jobs;原文『roughly 1.8% of jobs could have over half their tasks affected by LLMs with simple interfaces and general training… this share jumps to just over 46% of jobs』。更关键:Science 摘要里根本没有 80%/19%。因此任何引用『80% 劳动力/19%』的写法,引的都是被期刊版取代的工作论文,不是同行评审版本。面向准大学生的文章必须写清这一点。」日期:PubMed 记录为 Science 2024 Jun 21;384(6702):1306-1308,Epub 2024 Jun 20 —— 采集者写的 2024-06-20 是在线首发日,纸刊日为 06-21,两者都可用但需注明。

(3) 「三档 E0/E1/E2」不准确。原表述:「三档 E0/E1/E2」→ 正确表述:「标注时实为四档 E0/E1/E2/E3,E3(图像生成能力)在全部分析中被并入 E2。原文脚注:『we categorize access to image capabilities separately (E3) to facilitate annotation, though we combine E2 and E3 for all analyses.』」

以下逐字核对无误,可原样承重:exposure 定义(3.3 节:『we define exposure as a measure of whether access to an LLM or LLM-powered system would reduce the time required for a human to perform a specific DWA or complete a task by at least 50 percent』,配合 rubric 中『while maintaining equivalent quality』);α=E1、β=E1+0.5*E2、ζ=E1+E2;摘要的 80%/19% 确实用 β(4.1 节:『Based on the β values, we estimate that 80% of workers belong to an occupation with at least 10% of its tasks exposed to LLMs, while 19% of workers are in an occupation where over half of its tasks are labeled as exposed.』);「somewhat arbitrary」逐字命中;论断 C 四处免责逐字全中(含 Table 4 脚注『…but it does not necessarily suggest that their tasks can be fully automated by these technologies.』和摘要末句『We do not make predictions about the development or adoption timeline of such LLMs.』)。

RePEc 核实:截至 2026-07-24,ideas.repec.org/p/arx/papers/2303.10130.html 仍只显示 arXiv 工作论文(datePublished 2023-03、摘要为 v5 版),无任何「Published in」字段指向 Science。补充发现:arXiv abs 页面本身也没有 journal-ref,只有 arXiv 自签 DOI (10.48550/arXiv.2303.10130)。即两个主要索引都没把它标为已发表 —— 这正是「80%/19% 仍在被广泛转引」的传播机制。

**核验依据**:一手:arXiv:2303.10130v5 全文 PDF(https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5,本地 pdftotext 逐字检)—— 摘要、引言第2页、3.3节 rubric 摘要框、Table 2/Table 4、3.4 限制章节均亲自读原文。Science 版:science.org 与 doi.org 均被 Cloudflare 拦截(HTTP 403 / "Just a moment..."),web.archive.org 亦不可达;改由三条路径取得出版方摘要原文:(a) Semantic Scholar 出版方馈送记录 https://api.semanticscholar.org/graph/v1/paper/DOI:10.1126/science.adj0998 返回完整摘要含 "roughly 1.8% of jobs...just over 46% of jobs";(b) 作者所属机构 GovAI 论文页 https://www.governance.ai/research-paper/gpts-are-gpts-labor-market-impact-potential-of-llms 同文;(c) 书目由 Crossref(https://api.crossref.org/works/10.1126/science.adj0998,384(6702):1306-1308, published 2024-06-21)、PubMed PMID 38900883("2024 Jun 21;384(6702):1306-1308. Epub 2024 Jun 20")、OpenAlex W4399848789(publication_date 2024-06-20)三方交叉确认。RePEc 页面原始 HTML 抓取确认无 Published 字段;arXiv abs 页 HTML 确认无 journal-ref。


#### [G19] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:数字与引语全对,但标注者构成的口径需要补齐两处。

(1) 原表述:「人工标注者是 OpenAI 自己的对齐标注承包商(非各职业从业者)」→ 正确表述:「人工标注由两部分人完成:作者本人 + OpenAI 对齐工作的外包标注员。原文:『The authors personally labeled a large sample of tasks and DWAs and enlisted experienced human annotators who have reviewed GPT-3, GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 outputs as part of OpenAI's alignment work (Ouyang et al., 2022).』脚注5 进一步说明分工:作者亲自标注那些明显需要高度体力/手工灵巧度的 DWA,其余由 contracted annotators 标注。」—— 这不减弱质疑(作者自己就是 OpenAI 员工,4 位作者中 3 位署名 OpenAI/OpenResearch),但「全部由承包商标注」的说法不成立,应写成「由 OpenAI 内部人员与其对齐外包标注员共同完成,无一是对应职业的从业者」。

(2) 原表述:「GPT-4 用的 rubric 与人类用的不是同一份」→ 补上作者自己的限定语:原文是『there are slight differences between the rubric presented to humans and the one used for GPT-4』——作者称差异为「slight」,且说明是刻意为之(『This decision was made deliberately to guide the model towards reasonable labels without excessively influencing human annotators.』)。写「不是同一份」时应同时给出作者的「slight」自评,否则读者会以为是两套完全不同的标准。

(3) 建议补一条比原论断更有杀伤力的事实(逐字可查):作者明确说人类标注才是主结果 ——『In this analysis, we present results from human annotators as our primary results.』而 Brynjolfsson 的 Canaries 和 EIG 的反驳用的都是 GPT-4 β 分数,即论文作者自己不当作主结果的那一套。

(4) 「人工与 GPT-4 排出的最高暴露职业名单几乎不重叠」——实测比这更极端:Table 4 中 α、β、ζ 三组 top-5,人工与模型的名单**完全零重叠**(人工 α:Interpreters and Translators / Survey Researchers / Poets, Lyricists and Creative Writers / Animal Scientists / Public Relations Specialists;模型 α:Mathematicians / Correspondence Clerks / Blockchain Engineers / Court Reporters and Simultaneous Captioners / Proofreaders and Copy Markers)。「几乎不重叠」偏保守,可改为「完全不重叠」。

以下逐格核对无误:Table 2 六个数字全中(GPT-4 Rubric1 vs Human:α 80.8%/0.223、β 65.6%/0.591、ζ 82.1%/0.654;GPT-4 Rubric2 vs Human:α 81.8%/0.221、β 65.6%/0.538、ζ 79.5%/0.589),故「一致率 65.6%–82.1%」「Pearson r 最低 0.221」均正确(0.221 来自 Rubric2 的 α 行)。四处自陈引语逐字全中,包括最致命的『If indeed, the task-based breakdown is not a valid representation of how most work in an occupation is performed, our exposure analysis would largely be invalidated.』。86 vs 15 两个计数逐字命中(Table 4 内嵌注:『Humans labeled 15 occupations as "fully exposed."』/『The model labeled 86 occupations as "fully exposed."』,86/15=5.73,「5.7 倍」正确)。Barbers 反直觉自陈逐字命中:『(e.g. ranking Barbers as having reasonably high exposure)』。

**核验依据**:一手:arXiv:2303.10130v5 PDF 全文(https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5)。亲读段落:3.3 节末「Human Ratings / GPT-4 Ratings」两个 bullet 及脚注5;Table 2 及其表注("In the paper we use GPT-4, Rubric 1.");3.4.1 Subjective human judgments;3.4.2 Measuring LLMs with GPT-4;3.4.3 Additional Weaknesses 的两个 bullet;Table 4 全表含两条内嵌计数注;4.1 Summary Statistics 中 Barbers 一句。全部经本地 pdftotext -layout 抽取后逐字比对,非搜索摘要。


#### [G20] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:绝大部分逐字命中,但有一处必须改,并有一处应补。

【必须改】原表述:「2025-09:…automation 首次越过 50%」→ 正确表述:「9 月报告的原话是『This is the first report where automation usage exceeds augmentation usage.』(自动化首次超过增强),同一页对 Claude.ai 的描述是『the split between automation and augmentation is nearly even』,并在与 API 对比时写『compared to about 50% for Claude.ai users』。六分类框架下还有『两者皆非』的对话,所以『超过 augmentation』≠『越过 50%』。原报告从未说 automation 越过 50%。写成『越过 50%』是把一个相对比较升级成了绝对阈值,对准大学生而言这正是被高估的确定性。」

【应补】跨期不可比的原因,采集者只列了分类器换代一条,实际至少两条,且第二条更硬:2026-03「Learning curves」报告另行改用 2019 年版 O*NET 任务分类(此前各期为 2010 年版),这直接改变了分母本身。论断 B 应把这条一并写入,理由更强。

【逐字核对无误的部分】
- arXiv:2503.04761 摘要三句全部逐字命中:『over four million Claude.ai conversations』『approximately 36% of occupations using AI for at least a quarter of their associated tasks』『57% of usage suggests augmentation of human capabilities (e.g., learning or iterating on an output) while 43% suggests automation (e.g., fulfilling a request with minimal human involvement)』『While our data and methods face important limitations and only paint a picture of AI usage on a single platform, they provide an automated, granular approach for tracking AI's evolving role in the economy…』。注:arXiv v1 提交日为 2025-02-11,博客发布日 2025-02-10。
- 首期博客:『approximately one million conversations with Claude (specifically, Free and Pro conversations on Claude.ai)』;O*NET『around 20,000 specific work-related tasks』;『only approximately 4% of jobs used AI for at least 75% of their tasks』;『we don't argue that the uses in our dataset are a representative sample of AI use in general』。42.6%/57.4% 确实出现(图表标注 Augmentation 57.4% total / Automation 42.6% total),正文为四舍五入的 57%/43%。Computer & Mathematical 37.2% ✓(37.2%+10.3% 写作类≈47.5%,「近一半」成立)。
- 2025-09-15 报告:『The share of directive conversations sampled from Claude.ai conversations jumped from 27% in V1 in late 2024 to 39% in V3.』✓;『In our data, 77% of API transcripts show automation patterns (especially full task delegation) versus just 12% for augmentation (e.g., collaborative refinement and learning).』✓(77%/12% 均确认);『97% of tasks show automation-dominant patterns in API usage, compared to only 47% on Claude.ai.』✓;论断 C 犹他州逐字命中:『When further investigating Utah's activity, we discovered a notable fraction of its usage appeared to be possibly associated with coordinated abuse.』;『We note that V3 uses Claude Sonnet 4 for classification, while V2 used Sonnet 3.7, which complicates direct comparison.』✓。
- 2026-03-24「Learning curves」:『augmentation in Claude.ai increased slightly』✓、『automation decreased sharply in the 1P API data』✓。
- 2026-06-26「Cadences」:『about 9,700 survey respondents』✓;Computer and Mathematical『roughly 30% of survey respondents』vs『4% share of US employment』✓;『The Economic Index Survey is not representative of the general population.』✓ 逐字。
- 论断 D(2026-03-05《Labor market impacts of AI》)全中:『Computer Programmers are at the top, with 75% coverage』✓(部分媒体转引为 74.5%,以官方页 75% 为准);『30% of workers have zero coverage, as their tasks appeared too infrequently in our data to meet the minimum threshold.』✓(注意:零 coverage 的原因是数据里出现太少,不等于「不受影响」——这条限定语必须一起引);『For every 10 percentage point increase in coverage, the BLS's growth projection drops by 0.6 percentage points.』✓;『We find no impact on unemployment rates for workers in the most exposed occupations』✓;『The averaged estimate in the post-ChatGPT era is a 14% drop in the job finding rate compared to that in 2022 in the exposed occupations.』✓。

【利益相关,已核实】方向冲突属实:Anthropic 官方研究(2026-03-05)结论为「未检测到暴露组失业率上升」,而 CEO Dario Amodei 2025-05-28 对 Axios 公开表示 AI 可能在五年内消灭半数入门级白领岗位、推高失业率至 10–20%(Axios, 2025-05-28,"Behind the Curtain: A white-collar bloodbath")。同时全部数据、分类器、报告均出自 Anthropic 自身,无第三方复核。文章引用这批数据时必须同时标注「厂商自有平台数据 + 厂商自有分类器 + 厂商自行发布」。

**核验依据**:一手页面逐条 WebFetch 原页核对:https://www.anthropic.com/news/the-anthropic-economic-index(2025-02-10)、https://www.anthropic.com/research/anthropic-economic-index-september-2025-report(2025-09-15,两次独立提问交叉验证 77%/12%、97%/47%、27%→39%、Utah、Sonnet 4 vs 3.7)、https://www.anthropic.com/research/economic-index-march-2026-report(2026-03-24)、https://www.anthropic.com/research/economic-index-june-2026-report(2026-06-26)、https://www.anthropic.com/research/labor-market-impacts(2026-03-05)。arXiv:2503.04761 摘要取自 arXiv 官方 abs 页面 HTML(https://export.arxiv.org/abs/2503.04761)本地正则抽取,逐字比对,非二手摘要。Amodei 表态经检索确认源头为 Axios 2025-05-28 报道。


#### [G21] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:核心论断成立且已逐字坐实,但有一处方向性错误必须改,另有两处需要补足以免过度延伸。

【必须改 —— 方向反了】原表述:「Canaries 的 augmentation 最低两个五分位,Claude 使用量仅占对话 0.01% 和 0.09%,即最『增强型』分组样本极稀薄」→ 正确表述:「0.01% 和 0.09% 属于 augmentation **最低**的两个五分位,即**最不具增强性**的那两组,不是『最增强型』。原文脚注17:『Occupations in the first two augmentation quintiles have very low Claude usage (0.01% and 0.09% of conversations for the average occupation, respectively), with a high share of conversations classified as neither automative nor augmentative. In contrast, occupations in the third through fifth quintiles average 0.47%, 0.39%, and 0.33% of Claude conversations.』问题的性质是:被当作**对照/参照组**的那一端几乎没有数据支撑,所以整个 augmentation 梯度的低端是空的 —— 这仍是严重缺陷,但描述必须反过来写。」

【应补 1 —— 不要把非独立性说得过头】Canaries 的**结果变量**(ADP 工资单微观数据,数百万工人月度就业)与 Eloundou 和 Anthropic 完全无关,是真正独立的一手数据。共享的只是**自变量/分组构念**。准确说法应是:「三项研究共享同一套『AI 暴露度』构念,而非共享数据或结论;因此它们能互相印证的是『按这套暴露度分组后就业出现分化』,不能互相印证『这套暴露度衡量的确实是 AI 影响』。」

【应补 2 —— 一条更硬的传导证据】Canaries 与 EIG 用的都是 **GPT-4 β**,而 Eloundou 论文自己写明『we present results from human annotators as our primary results』——即两篇下游研究采用的是原作者不当作主结果的那一套标注(且 GPT-4 与人类标注的 β 一致率仅 65.6%)。这比「共享前提」更具体。

【逐字核对无误的部分】
- Canaries §3.2:『We use two approaches for measuring occupational exposure to AI. The first uses exposure measures from Eloundou et al. (2024)… They construct occupational exposure measures by aggregating the task data to the 2018 SOC code level. We focus on the GPT-4 based β exposure measures from their paper.』✓ 逐字。
- 第二套来自 Anthropic Economic Index (Handa et al., 2025) ✓;automation/augmentation 的映射规则见脚注14 逐字:『Handa et al. (2025) use Claude to classify conversations into six categories. Directive and Feedback Loop conversations are considered Automative; Task Iteration, Learning, and Validation are considered Augmentative.』
- crosswalk ✓ 逐字:『We use a 2010 SOC code to 2018 SOC code crosswalk from the BLS to merge the exposure measures to the payroll data.』注意合并对象原文是 **payroll data**(ADP 工资单),写「薪资数据」可以,但更准确是「工资单就业微观数据」。
- 安慰剂/前趋势问题 ✓ 逐字:『A concern is that for the Eloundou et al. (2024) measures the most exposed quintile had slower employment growth starting around 2020. This is not the case for the Anthropic exposure measures, shown in Figures A17, A18, and A19.』**但必须补上上下文**:这段出现在「Longer Sample」稳健性检验小节,作者紧接在『This reduces the sample size and makes the data somewhat noisier.』之后 —— 引用时不给这句,会让该证据显得比原文更硬。
- EIG 也用同一套分数 ✓ 逐字坐实:『We calculate AI exposure quintiles using the data and code provided in the replication package for Eloundou et al. (2024), using GPT-4 β scores and an equal weight scheme. We understand this is the same basis for exposure…』—— 论断中「EIG 的反驳也用同一套 β 分数」完全成立。

**核验依据**:一手 PDF 亲自下载并 pdftotext 逐字检索:(1) Brynjolfsson, Chandar & Chen《Canaries in the Coal Mine?》Nov 2025 版,https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/app/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf(2.29 MB,5909 行文本)—— 亲读 §3.2 Occupational AI Exposure、§3.3 Other Data、脚注13/14/17、Longer Sample 稳健性段落、式(4.1) 附近。(2) Iscenko & Curto Millet《Looking for the Ladder: Is AI Impacting Entry-Level Jobs?》EIG, Jan 2026,https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf(1.19 MB)—— 亲读方法附注段,确认其复现包与 GPT-4 β 用法。(3) 交叉参照 arXiv:2303.10130v5 中『we present results from human annotators as our primary results』与 Table 2 的 β 一致率 65.6%。


### 第 3 席


#### [G18] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:（1）3%/49% 的出处错了。原表述「arXiv v5 摘要:…人工标注下"仅 3%"、"最多 49%"」→ 正确表述：这两个数在**正文第 1 节 Introduction**（v5 PDF 第 2 页），不在摘要。v5 摘要里的第二组数是 task-level 的「about 15% of all worker tasks」和「between 47 and 56% of all tasks」，是**任务占比**不是劳动者占比。（2）「两个版本两套数字」的对应关系错了。原表述暗示 80%/19% 被改成 1.8%/46% → 正确表述：这是**两组不同口径**。1.8% = α50（仅 LLM 本体），46% = ζ50（含互补软件）；80%/19% 是 β 口径。真正被下修的是 arXiv 正文的 3%→1.8%、49%→46%（另 arXiv 还有等权任务口径的 2.3% α50 / 47.3% ζ50，见 4.2 节）。Science 摘要里**根本没有 80%/19%**。（3）Science 版的性质要标注：它是 **Policy Forum**（观点/政策栏目，3 页，付费墙 "No access"），不是完整研究论文；rubric、Table 2、四处免责全部只存在于 arXiv 工作论文里。凡引「Eloundou et al. (2024)」者，引的是一篇 3 页政策短文。（4）「Table 4 脚注」→ 应为 **Table 4 表题（caption）**。（5）日期：science.org 页面自己显示 "20 Jun 2024"（电子出版日），PubMed/Crossref 的期号日期是 21 Jun 2024，卷期页 384(6702):1306–1308 无误。（6）RePEc 核实：ideas.repec.org 该条目至今（2026-07-24）仍无 "Published in" 字段，仍标为 2023 年 8 月修订的工作论文。

**核验依据**:① arXiv 2303.10130v5 PDF 全文下载并转文本逐字核对（https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5）：摘要含 80%/19%/15%/47–56%；Intro 原文「Human assessments suggest that only 3% of U.S. workers have over half of their tasks exposed to LLMs when considering existing language and code capabilities without additional software or modalities. Accounting for other generative models and complementary technologies, our human estimates indicate that up to 49% of workers could have half or more of their tasks exposed to LLMs.」；4.1 节原文「Based on the β values, we estimate that 80% of workers belong to an occupation with at least 10% of its tasks exposed to LLMs, while 19% of workers are in an occupation where over half of its tasks are labeled as exposed.」；4.2 节「human annotators determined that 2.3% of occupations are α50-exposed, 21.6% are β50-exposed, and 47.3% are ζ50-exposed」。② 定义与阈值逐字：E0/E1/E2 rubric 摘要框（「can decrease the time required to complete the DWA or task by at least half (50%)」，E0 含 quality 条款），及「Although this threshold is somewhat arbitrary, it was selected for ease of interpretation by annotators.」③ 四处免责全部逐字命中：「We define exposure as a proxy for potential economic impact without distinguishing between labor-augmenting or labor-displacing effects.」（Intro）；「This exposure measure reflects an estimate of the technical capacity to make human labor more efficient; however, social, economic, regulatory, and other determinants imply that technical feasibility does not guarantee labor productivity or automation outcomes.」（Intro）；Table 4 caption「…but it does not necessarily suggest that their tasks can be fully automated by these technologies.」；「We do not make predictions about the development or adoption timeline of such LLMs.」（摘要）。④ **Science 版一手取得**：science.org 被 403，改用 Wayback 原始快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20240621093530id_/https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adj0998 ，页面自述「Policy Forum … Science 20 Jun 2024 Vol 384, Issue 6702 pp. 1306-1308 DOI: 10.1126/science.adj0998 … No access」，Abstract 逐字：「…we estimate that roughly 1.8% of jobs could have over half their tasks affected by LLMs with simple interfaces and general training. When accounting for current and likely future software developments that complement LLM capabilities, this share jumps to just over 46% of jobs.」与 Semantic Scholar（DOI:10.1126/science.adj0998）返回的出版商摘要完全一致。PubMed PMID 38900883：ArticleDate 2024-06-20，JournalIssue PubDate 2024-06-21。⑤ RePEc：https://ideas.repec.org/p/arx/papers/2303.10130.html 无 Published-in 字段。


#### [G19] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:数字全对（Table 2 六格、86 vs 15、四处引语逐字无误），但三处口径需修：（1）标注者身份。原表述「人工标注者是 OpenAI 自己的对齐标注承包商」→ 正确表述：是**作者本人 + OpenAI 对齐工作的合同标注员**两部分。原文：「The authors personally labeled a large sample of tasks and DWAs and enlisted experienced human annotators who have reviewed GPT-3, GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 outputs as part of OpenAI's alignment work」；且脚注 5 明确分工：**作者亲自标注了「明显需要高度体力或手部灵巧度」的 DWA**，合同标注员标其余——这个分工本身就是一处未被讨论的方向性偏差来源。（2）rubric 差异。原表述「不是同一份」→ 论文自己的措辞是 "slight differences" / "slight modifications"，且**修改目的是 "to enhance agreement with a set of human labels"**——即 GPT-4 的 rubric 是朝人类标签调过的，这会机械抬高 Table 2 的一致率，比「不是同一份」更值得写。另有一处更大的不可比被漏掉：**人类标 DWA + 部分 task，GPT-4 标全部 task/occupation pair**，单位与覆盖面都不同。（3）「最高暴露职业名单几乎不重叠」→ 事实上 Table 4 的 α、β、ζ 三对 top-5 是**零重叠**，比"几乎"更强；但必须加限定：ζ 那一对的 top-5 是从 15 个（人工）和 86 个（模型）并列 100% 的职业里任取五个，非重叠不构成分歧证据；只有 α/β 两对可承重。且同一篇论文同时声称「we observe a high degree of agreement between human ratings and GPT-4 ratings at the occupation level」（Table 2 中 β r=0.591、ζ r=0.654），两者要并列呈现。附：86/15 是 **ζ 口径下"fully exposed"（=100%）** 的职业计数；Barbers 那句的语境是**未按 O*NET "Importance" 加权**导致的怪异结果，不是人机分歧。

**核验依据**:arXiv 2303.10130v5 PDF 逐字核对。Table 2 六格完全吻合：GPT-4 Rubric 1 vs Human — α/E1 = 80.8%、r=0.223；β/E1+.5*E2 = 65.6%、r=0.591；ζ/E1+E2 = 82.1%、r=0.654；Rubric 2 vs Human — α 81.8%/r=0.221（全表人机 r 最低值）、β 65.6%/r=0.538、ζ 79.5%/r=0.589；模型自比 Rubric1 vs Rubric2 高达 91.1%。四处引语逐字命中：3.4.1「A fundamental limitation of our approach lies in the subjectivity of the labeling… this group is not occupationally diverse, potentially leading to biased judgments…」；3.4.2「Consequently, there are slight differences between the rubric presented to humans and the one used for GPT-4… we use multiple annotation sources, but none should be considered the definitive ground truth relative to the others.」；3.4.3「Human annotators were mostly unaware of the specific occupations mapped to each DWA during the labeling process.」；3.4.3「If indeed, the task-based breakdown is not a valid representation of how most work in an occupation is performed, our exposure analysis would largely be invalidated.」；4.1「…yields some curious results (e.g. ranking Barbers as having reasonably high exposure).」Table 4 内嵌行逐字：「Humans labeled 15 occupations as "fully exposed."」/「The model labeled 86 occupations as "fully exposed."」（86/15 = 5.73×）。Top-5 名单：Human α = Interpreters and Translators / Survey Researchers / Poets, Lyricists and Creative Writers / Animal Scientists / Public Relations Specialists；Model α = Mathematicians / Correspondence Clerks / Blockchain Engineers / Court Reporters and Simultaneous Captioners / Proofreaders and Copy Markers（零重叠）。GPT-4 rubric 调参原文：「We made slight modifications to the rubric (which was used as a "prompt" to the model in this case) to enhance agreement with a set of human labels.」脚注 5：「The authors annotated DWAs that clearly required a high degree of physicality or manual dexterity, and the contracted annotators labeled the remaining activities…」来源 https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5


#### [G20] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:绝大多数数字逐字核实无误（含 42.6/57.4，见证据），但有 **1 处错误** 和 **4 处被砍掉的限定语**：（A）**错误**：「2025-09…automation 首次越过 50%」→ 正确表述：**automation 份额首次超过 augmentation 份额，绝对水平是 49%（Sonnet 4 分类器）/ 45%（Sonnet 3.7 重跑）**。报告原话是 "This is the first report where automation usage exceeds augmentation usage"，并两次形容 Claude.ai 的分裂为 "nearly even" / "about 50%"。写成"越过 50%"是把"超过对方"误读为"超过半数"，对准大学生是实打实的误导。（B）Sonnet 引语被砍半。原文完整为：「…which complicates direct comparison. **To address this, we reran V3 data with Sonnet 3.7 and still found directive interactions rising significantly (though to a lower absolute level of 45% automation versus 49% with Sonnet 4). We also verified this trend is not driven by changes in task mix…**」只引前半句等于删掉了作者的稳健性检验。（C）犹他州引语同样被砍。footnote 7 原文以「**However, we ran robustness checks and believe that this activity is not driving the results.**」结尾。可以叫"自曝污染"，但必须并列作者的稳健性声明。（D）14% 必须带限定：原文「…a 14% drop in the job finding rate compared to that in 2022 in the exposed occupations, **although this is just barely statistically significant**」，且它是**月度求职成功率（约 2%/月 降约 0.5pp）**，不是就业率/失业率，且「There is no such decrease for workers older than 25.」（E）0.6pp 那句后面紧跟「**although the relationship is slight**」，且同页还有一句关键的反向证据：「**there is no such correlation using the Eloundou et al. measure alone**」。另补三点口径：（F）跨期不可比的**更根本**原因不是换分类器，而是**每期都是 7 天快照**——论文限制章节原文「We use snapshots of Claude.ai Free and Pro conversations over 7-day periods」，2026-03 报告的样本更只有 2026-02-05 至 02-12 一周。（G）2026-06 的 9,700 是「linked sample」（随机抽 Claude 用户、剔除 session < 5 者），与 2025-12 用 Anthropic Interviewer 访谈的 81,000 人**不是同一批**，别混为一谈。（H）CEO 矛盾要精确：Amodei 2025-05-28 Axios 的"白领血洗"（入门级白领岗减半、失业率 10–20%、1–5 年内）属实；但公司报告的结论不是"影响有限"，而是"**迄今检测不到**"，且自陈可检测门槛约为 1 个百分点的差异失业率。把它写成"公司结论与 CEO 相反"要降调为"一个是预测、一个是迄今未检出"。
