#### [G21] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:核心事实**全部逐字成立**（Canaries 的自变量确实直接是 Eloundou GPT-4 β 与 Anthropic Economic Index；EIG 确实也用同一套 β；0.01%/0.09% 无误），但推论范围要收窄、同时有一条更强的证据被漏掉：（1）**推论过宽**。原表述「三项研究…共享同一套构念前提」→ 正确表述：**共享 Eloundou GPT-4 β 这一个构念的是 Canaries 与 EIG 这一对**；Anthropic Economic Index 本身（Claude 使用量分布）在构造上与 Eloundou rubric 相互独立，Canaries 用了**两套**自变量，Anthropic 那一套不受 Eloundou rubric 波及——恰恰相反，安慰剂问题只出现在 Eloundou 口径。所以「EIG 反驳 Canaries」这一场辩论确实是同一构念内部之争，但不能说三项研究整体都是假独立。（2）**漏掉一条比原论断更强的证据**：G20 论断 D 依赖的 Anthropic 2026-03-05《Labor market impacts of AI》，其 observed exposure / coverage 测度**本身就架在 Eloundou β 之上**（原文：「Task-level exposure estimates from Eloundou et al. (2023)… Eloundou et al.'s metric, β, scores tasks on a simple scale: 1… 0.5… 0」）。引用链比论断说的更长：Eloundou β → {Canaries 分组, EIG 反驳, Anthropic 自家位移风险测度}。（3）**同一页还有一条对 Eloundou 口径不利的独立检验**，比"假独立"更能承重：「there is no such correlation using the Eloundou et al. measure alone」——Eloundou β 单独使用时与 BLS 2024–2034 就业增长预测**不相关**。（4）安慰剂那句的语境要标注：它出现在 "Longer Sample" 稳健性小节，指的是把样本延到 2018 的 **Figure A16**，作者同段自陈「This reduces the sample size and makes the data somewhat noisier」——不是主样本结果。（5）0.01%/0.09% 要补对照与稳健性：第三至五分位分别为 0.47%/0.39%/0.33%；automation 口径最低组 0.05%、最高组 0.73%；作者称 Figures A9/A10 显示剔除低使用量职业后结果相似。（6）EIG 的「same basis」是 **EIG 自己的推断**（原文 "We understand this is the same basis…"），非 Brynjolfsson 团队确认，且 EIG 明示用 "an equal weight scheme"，与 Canaries 的聚合口径未必逐格相同。（7）版本要标注：EIG 那份 note 明说是针对 Canaries **2025-08-26 原始版**所写（脚注 1），而现行 Canaries 是 **2025-11-13** 版。

**核验依据**:① Canaries PDF 下载并转文本逐字核对 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/app/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf （封面日期 November 13, 2025）：§3.2 原文「We focus on the GPT-4 based β exposure measures from their paper.」「The second approach we take uses generative AI usage data from the Anthropic Economic Index (Handa et al., 2025).」「We use a 2010 SOC code to 2018 SOC code crosswalk from the BLS to merge the exposure measures to the payroll data.」；脚注 14 确认 directive+feedback loop→Automative、task iteration+learning+validation→Augmentative，并称 Table A2 复制自 Handa et al. Table 1；脚注 17 原文「Occupations in the first two augmentation quintiles have very low Claude usage (0.01% and 0.09% of conversations for the average occupation, respectively), with a high share of conversations classified as neither automative nor augmentative. In contrast, occupations in the third through fifth quintiles average 0.47%, 0.39%, and 0.33%… the lowest exposure group averaging 0.05% and the highest group averaging 0.73%. Figures A9 and A10 show that automation and augmentation results are similar when dropping occupations with low overall Claude usage.」；"Longer Sample" 小节原文「Figure A16 shows results when extending the balanced sample of firms to 2018. This reduces the sample size and makes the data somewhat noisier… A concern is that for the Eloundou et al. (2024) measures the most exposed quintile had slower employment growth starting around 2020. This is not the case for the Anthropic exposure measures, shown in Figures A17, A18, and A19.」② EIG PDF 下载并逐字核对 https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf （Iscenko & Curto Millet, January 2026）方法附录：「We calculate AI exposure quintiles using the data and code provided in the replication package for Eloundou et al. (2024), using GPT-4 β scores and an equal weight scheme. We understand this is the same basis for exposure calculations as was used in Brynjolfsson et al. (2025).」；脚注 1：「Our comments in this note were written about the original version of the "Canaries" paper published on 26 August 2025.」③ Anthropic 2026-03-05 页面 https://www.anthropic.com/research/labor-market-impacts 逐字：「Our approach combines data from three sources. The O*NET database… Our own usage data (as measured in the Anthropic Economic Index). Task-level exposure estimates from Eloundou et al. (2023)… Eloundou et al.'s metric, β…」及「Interestingly, there is no such correlation using the Eloundou et al. measure alone.」


## 批次 C2 — verify:AI就业实测对垒


### 第 1 席


#### [G22] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 最重要的一处:「三个不同口径的数字」实际上只有两个口径,不是三个。摘要的 16% 和 Fact 4 的「15 log points」是同一个估计量的两种写法,不是两次独立测量。原文结论 bullet 逐字为「After conditioning on firm-time effects, young workers experienced a 16% relative employment decline in the most exposed occupations.」——即 16% 就是带 firm-time 固定效应的事件研究估计;exp(0.15)−1 = 16.18% ≈ 16%。真正独立的另一个口径只有 Fact 2 的 6%(原始、未加控制)。原表述「摘要(相对口径)16%」与「Fact 4(事件研究,带 firm-time 固定效应)15 log points」并列 → 应改为「16%(=15 log points,同一估计量,带 firm-time 固定效应,相对最低暴露五分位);Fact 2 的 6% 是未加任何控制的原始变动」。
(2) Fact 2 分母:原表述「22–25 岁在最暴露职业的就业下降 6%」→ 正文 Fact 2 逐字是「for the highest two exposure quintiles employment for 22-25 year olds declined by 6%」,是**最高两个**五分位,不是单一最暴露五分位。
(3) 年长组对照:「+6% 至 +9%」只出现在导言(「compared to a 6-9% increase for older workers」);Fact 2 正文的对照是「employment for workers aged 35-49 grew by over 8%」。两者都在,但应注明「+6%~+9%」是导言口径。
(4) Fact 4 引语:导言版逐字为「Estimates for other age groups are much smaller in magnitude and not statistically significant」,正文 4.4 节版多一个词:「generally much smaller」。引用时应标明取自导言。
(5) 「作者已让步的部分」归类错误:「more AI-exposed jobs are actually less exposed to interest rates on average」不是让步,是作者对 EIG 利率解释的**反驳**。2026-02-09 博文中作者只在**时点**上让步(「the employment decline is significant only after 2024」),在利率渠道上明确不让步:「we do not find strong support for the hypothesis that interest rate changes explain the disproportionate decline」。原表述「并称…」把反驳并入让步 → 应拆开写。
(6) 版本漂移的**机制**需补:13%→16% 不是换了方法,是同一 firm-time 固定效应设定加了三个月数据。作者自述逐字:「reaching about 16% by October 2025, vs 13% in our paper with data only through July」。另注:博文说「by October 2025」,而论文数据窗口截至 2025 年 9 月——博文自身有一处月份不一致。
(7) 「截至 2026 年 7 月 Fortune 等仍在引 13%」这一条我未独立核实(未回到 Fortune 原文),不应以已核实的语气写入。

**核验依据**:一手 PDF 逐字核对(pdftotext 全文提取,非摘要器):Nov 版 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Canaries_BrynjolfssonChandarChen.pdf(题名页日期 November 13, 2025)。摘要逐字:「Early-career workers (ages 22-25) in AI-exposed occupations experienced 16% relative employment declines, controlling for firm-level shocks」✓。Fact 2 逐字:「for the highest two exposure quintiles employment for 22-25 year olds declined by 6% between late 2022 and September 2025, while employment for workers aged 35-49 grew by over 8%」。Fact 4 逐字:「a 15 log point decline in relative employment for the most AI-exposed quintiles compared to the least exposed quintile」+「Estimates for other age groups are generally much smaller in magnitude and not statistically significant」。结论 bullet 逐字:「After conditioning on firm-time effects, young workers experienced a 16% relative employment decline」→ 证明 16% 与 15 log points 同源。软件开发者逐字:「By September 2025, employment for software developers aged 22-25 declined nearly 20% compared to its peak in late 2022」✓。数据口径逐字:「ADP observes job titles for about 70% of workers in its system. We exclude workers without a recorded job title」✓;「After these restrictions we have records on 3.5 and 5 million workers each month」✓;「the distribution of firms using ADP services does not exactly match the distribution of firms across the broader US economy」✓;免责逐字:「we caution that the facts we document may in part be influenced by factors other than generative AI. Taken as a whole, our results are consistent with the hypothesis that generative AI has begun to affect entry-level employment.」✓。
版本漂移经 Wayback id_ 原始快照取得初版:https://web.archive.org/web/20250901000000id_/https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Canaries_BrynjolfssonChandarChen.pdf,题名页 August 26, 2025,摘要逐字:「early-career workers (ages 22-25) in the most AI-exposed occupations have experienced a 13 percent relative decline in employment even after controlling for firm-level shocks」——13% 与 16% 为同一设定、不同数据窗口。
2026-02-09 博文原始 HTML 抓取(未经 WebFetch 摘要)https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/news/canaries-interest-rates-and-timinga-more-on-recent-drivers-of-employment-changes-for-young-workers,逐字:「When we explore a more stringent approach to control for other factors, the employment decline is significant only after 2024.」;「we find evidence that more AI-exposed jobs are actually less exposed to interest rates on average」;「With the fixed-effect approach, the employment changes start later, but they are growing (reaching about 16% by October 2025, vs 13% in our paper with data only through July), and they have not shown a reversal.」发表状态:落地页仍只列工作论文 + Feb 2026 后续说明,无期刊,未过审属实。


#### [G23] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:五条论点、两处核心引语、署名与致谢**全部逐字核对无误**,唯第 2 条的口径混用必须修:
(1) 原表述「普查局数据显示到 2023 Q4 大企业中计划未来六个月用 AI 生产商品/服务的不到 10%;到 2025 Q3 也只升到 12%」——把两个**不同指标**串成一条序列。EIG 原文逐字:前半是「fewer than 10 percent of large businesses surveyed were even planning to use AI in the next six months」(计划采用),后半是「adoption by large businesses has only climbed to 12 percent」(实际采用)。→ 应改为「2023 Q4:计划未来六个月用 AI 的大企业不到 10%;2025 Q3:实际采用率 12%(两者非同一指标)」。
(2) 「大企业」须定义。EIG 脚注 2 逐字:「The largest business category in the U.S. Census Bureau's Business Trends and Outlook Survey covers companies with 250+ employees.」→ 大企业 = 250 人以上。
(3) 同一脚注还给了一个比 10% 更有力的数字,建议补入:「Current business adoption at the time of the survey was even weaker than planned adoption: in the same time period, fewer than 6 percent of businesses with 250 employees or more reported using AI technologies in the past two weeks for the production of goods and services.」
(4) 「与 Canaries 同一基础」应保留 EIG 自己的限定词。原文逐字:「We understand this is the same basis for exposure calculations as was used in Brynjolfsson et al. (2025).」——是「我们理解为」,非双方确认。
(5) **必须补一条重大限定**:EIG 脚注 1 逐字:「Our comments in this note were written about the original version of the 'Canaries' paper published on 26 August 2025.」这既证实了 G22 中「EIG 批评针对 13% 那版」的说法,也意味着 EIG 正文里引的「16 percent」是他们发表时改引的最新版数字,而分析本身针对旧版。文章若不写这一句,会让读者误以为 EIG 逐条打的是 Nov25 版。
(6) 补充报告标识:题为《Looking for the Ladder: Is AI Impacting Entry-Level Jobs?》,January 2026,EIG American Worker Project。

**核验依据**:一手 PDF 全文 pdftotext 提取:https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf。数据附录逐字:「Our analysis utilizes more than 238 million US job postings (averaging ~3.3 million monthly) from September 2019 to August 2025... across 767 6-digit SOC occupational categories and 21 2-digit NAICS industry sectors.」✓ 2.38 亿/月均 330 万/767×21 全部命中。暴露度构造逐字:「We calculate AI exposure quintiles using the data and code provided in the replication package for Eloundou et al. (2024), using GPT-4 β scores and an equal weight scheme.」✓ 复现包/GPT-4 β/等权重全部命中。时点逐字:「vacancies for the highest AI exposure quintile of occupations peaked in March–April 2022 and declined sharply throughout the remainder of the year」✓。共线性逐字:「Approximately 38 percent of workers in the most AI-exposed quintile are employed in these sectors, compared to less than 2 percent in the least-exposed quintile」(基于 2023 Census data)✓。资历逐字:「There is no evidence that job postings for junior roles within occupations most exposed to AI have declined more than postings for senior positions.」✓ 一字不差。25% 机械性收缩段落与 G23 所引**完全逐字一致**(已逐词比对)✓。核心主张逐字:「The most plausible explanation is that the data patterns observed are not early warnings of large-scale technological displacement, but rather the predictable consequences of a classic macroeconomic shock: the sharpest monetary policy tightening cycle in four decades.」✓ 一字不差。自设边界逐字:「All this being said, absence of evidence is not the same as evidence of absence」✓。
署名逐字确认:「Zanna Iscenko is AI & Economy Lead, Chief Economist's Team, Google; and Fabien Curto Millet is Chief Economist, Google.」✓ 一字不差。致谢逐字:「The authors are grateful to Guillaume Aimard for excellent research assistance. The piece was greatly improved by comments from and exchanges with: David Autor, Diane Coyle, Mohamed El-Erian, James Manyika, Ruth Porat, Michael Pisa, Michael Spence, Scott Strand, Kent Walker and David Weller.」→ Ruth Porat(Alphabet 总裁兼 CIO)、Kent Walker(Alphabet 总法律顾问)、James Manyika(Google SVP)三人全部在列 ✓,且致谢名单里 Google 高管密度比 G23 描述的更高。旁证:Humlum & Vestergaard 2026 年 3 月版参考文献已正式引用 Iscenko and Curto Millet (2026)。


#### [G24] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:三份证据里 A 基本成立但有一条查无实据,B 引的是**已被取代的版本**,C 不但可核实、而且三个系数中有两个口径错了。

【A · Yale Budget Lab】四处引语与四个基准日期全部逐字命中,但:
(1) 「2026Q1 青年失业率上升迹象统计不显著」——**该报告里没有这句话,也没有任何青年失业率显著性检验**。2026-04-16《Tracking the Impact of AI on the Labor Market》全文只做职业/行业相异性指数与暴露度份额,失业相关分析只有「按失业时长看被暴露任务占比无差异」。→ 此条应删除,或改挂到另一份报告。
(2) 「唯一异动」→ 原文是「The most notable difference」(最显著的差异),不是「唯一」。
(3) 必须补该报告自设的样本警告:「our results should be interpreted with caution particularly given small sample sizes」——这正是应届生那条异动的限定,原表述漏掉了。
(4) 更重要的遗漏:Budget Lab 已另发一篇方法更强的《AI Is Probably Not (Yet) the Reason for Labor Market Weakening》,用 synthetic difference-in-differences 做正式因果识别,结论为估计值「close to zero and cannot be distinguished from it, statistically speaking」。文章若只引相异性指数(纯描述性),等于弃用了 Yale 手上更有承重力的那份证据。

【B · Humlum & Vestergaard】原表述引的是 2025-07-15 版《Large Language Models, Small Labor Market Effects》,该版**已被取代**。
(1) 现行版:2026-03-13,题名已改为《Still Waters, Rapid Currents: Early Labor Market Transformation under Generative AI》(NBER w33777 同步改题)。
(2) 「confidence intervals ruling out effects above 1%」→ 现行版逐字为「ruling out effects larger than 2% two years after the launch of ChatGPT」。**置信区间从 1% 放宽到 2%**,这是实质性削弱。
(3) 「Modest productivity gains (3% time savings)」→ 3% 这个数字**已不在现行版摘要中**,全文亦未见该表述。
(4) 「Our findings challenge narratives of imminent disruption」→ 该句**已从现行版摘要中删除**,现行版结句为「Technological change reshapes work well before it surfaces in earnings or hours.」
(5) early-career 章节的悬案现已解决:**在现行版的 §3.4 与 Appendix D.2**(2025-07 版确实没有,采集者未定位到是对的)。但 EIG 与 Canaries 转述为「对入门级为零效应」是**误述**。原文逐字:「While we replicate the pattern of declining early-career employment in exposed occupations in Denmark, our difference-in-differences analysis reveals that the declines are not driven by firms adopting AI chatbots.」→ 正确口径是:丹麦**同样出现**入门级就业下降(与 Canaries 同向),零效应指的是「采用 AI 的职场与未采用职场之间无差异」这一**渠道**,不是「入门级没有下降」。精度为「rule out effects greater than a third of a percentage point」(早期职业就业份额)。EIG 的转述(「they evidence a decline… the DiD shows AI is not the driver」)是准确的;「零效应」这个简写是不准确的。
(6) 结果变量应写明是 earnings 与 recorded hours(及职场层面的就业/工资总额),不是个人层面的雇佣状态。

【C · Johnston & Makridis】**不判 UNVERIFIABLE——已取得一手全文**(CESifo Working Paper 12579,2026-03-25),SSRN 403 可绕过。三个系数:
(1) 「生产率 +10%」→ 错。结果变量是**产出**(BEA 实际增加值),不是生产率。原文逐字:「A one standard deviation increase in AI exposure raises sector output by roughly 10 percent by the end of the sample」;而**摘要头条数字是 7%**:「raises output by 7%」(全样本平均;10% 是 2024 年末端点值)。且产出 +10% 搭配就业 +3.9%,隐含劳动生产率约 +6%,绝非 +10%。
(2) 「工资 +4.8%」→ 错,且是本批最危险的一处。4.8% 是**工资总额(wage bill)**,不是工资。原文逐字:「By 2024, a one standard deviation increase in exposure is associated with a 4.8 percent increase in the wage bill (p < 0.01), a 3.9 percent increase in employment (p < 0.01), and a 2.6 percent increase in establishments (p < 0.05).」作者自己算过人均:ACS 个人层面小时工资仅 **+1.0–1.1%**;且作者结论是「only about 29% of the AI-induced [output gains accrue to labor]」、劳动份额每标准差下降 5 个百分点。对准大学生而言,「工资 +4.8%」与「小时工资 +1%、劳动份额下降」是完全不同的两条建议。
(3) 「就业 +3.9%」数字对,但现行版摘要已按暴露类型拆分:「where AI likely requires human collaboration, employment rises 4%; where AI can perform tasks independently, we find no significant employment effect」。3.9% 是合并值,不能不加区分地用。
(4) 数据源不止 QCEW:产出用 BEA,就业/工资总额用 QCEW(covers over 95% of all U.S. wage and salary jobs),个人工资用 ACS。原表述「匹配 QCEW 官方全覆盖行政数据」漏了 BEA 与 ACS。
(5) 必须补一条对全文论证有影响的事实:该文效应**始于 2021 年、早于 ChatGPT**(「effects emerging in 2021 when enterprise AI tools entered the market」)。这既削弱它作为「生成式 AI 正面效应」的证据力,也与 EIG 的时点论证同向——不能只当作 Canaries 的反方。
(6) Canaries 脚注 9 逐字引用无误,可原样使用。

**核验依据**:A:一手 PDF https://budgetlab.yale.edu/sites/default/files/page_to_pdf/1419/publication_1419.pdf,题头「Tracking the Impact of AI on the Labor Market — Published: April 16, 2026 Updated: April 16, 2026」。逐字命中四处:「While the occupational mix is changing more quickly than it has in the past, it is not a large difference and predates the widespread introduction of AI in the workforce.」/「Currently, measures of exposure, automation, and augmentation show no sign of being related to changes in employment or unemployment.」/「The most notable difference is an uptick in the dissimilarity of occupational mix between older and younger college graduates, though this remains at the high end of the historical range.」/「our analysis is not predictive of the future.」基准日期图例逐字:「AI (baseline Nov 2022) / Computers (baseline Jan 1984) / Control (baseline Jan 2016) / Internet (baseline Jan 1996)」——四个基准全部命中。全文 grep「unemploy」仅得暴露度按失业时长的分析,无青年失业率显著性检验。另一篇:https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/ai-probably-not-yet-reason-labor-market-weakening(原始 HTML 抓取),逐字:「The estimate is close to zero and cannot be distinguished from it, statistically speaking.」
B:2025-07-15 版 https://www.andershumlum.com/s/chatbots_july25.pdf(题名《Large Language Models, Small Labor Market Effects》,摘要含「above 1%」「3% time savings」「challenge narratives of imminent disruption」;全文 grep early-career/entry-level 仅 2 处,均非分析章节,证实该版无入门级章节)。现行版经 RFBerlin 机构镜像取得:https://www.rfberlin.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/26078.pdf(Discussion Paper 078/26,题名页 March 13, 2026),摘要逐字:「using difference-in-differences, we estimate precise null effects on earnings and recorded hours at both the worker and workplace levels, ruling out effects larger than 2% two years after the launch of ChatGPT」;§3.4 逐字:「Crucially, Panel (b) shows that the decline in early-career jobs is not driven by firms adopting AI chatbots... estimates precise enough to rule out effects greater than a third of a percentage point」;导言逐字:「While we replicate the pattern of declining early-career employment in exposed occupations in Denmark, our difference-in-differences analysis reveals that the declines are not driven by firms adopting AI chatbots.」样本口径仍成立:「We received about 25,000 valid and complete responses to each survey」「25,000 workers across 7,000 workplaces」「11 occupations」「November–December of 2023 and 2024」✓。NBER w33777 现题名亦为《Still Waters, Rapid Currents》。
C:SSRN 5375017 与 6460358 均返回 HTTP 403,改走机构镜像成功:https://www.ifo.de/DocDL/cesifo1_wp12579.pdf(CESifo WP No. 12579,《AI, Output, and Employment》,Andrew C. Johnston & Christos A. Makridis,March 25, 2026,55 页)。摘要逐字:「A one standard deviation increase in exposure raises output by 7%, with effects emerging in 2021 when enterprise AI tools entered the market. Employment effects follow the same timing but diverge by exposure type: where AI likely requires human collaboration, employment rises 4%; where AI can perform tasks independently, we find no significant employment effect.」正文 §5.1 逐字:「raises sector output by roughly 10 percent by the end of the sample」;§5.2 逐字:「By 2024, a one standard deviation increase in exposure is associated with a 4.8 percent increase in the wage bill (p < 0.01), a 3.9 percent increase in employment (p < 0.01), and a 2.6 percent increase in establishments (p < 0.05).」数据逐字:「For employment outcomes, we use the Quarterly Census of Employment and Wages (QCEW), which covers over 95% of all U.S. wage and salary jobs」+ BEA 产出 + ACS 个人工资。作者第一方综述(ProMarket 2026-05-21,Makridis & Johnston 署名)佐证人均口径:小时工资 +1.0–1.1%、劳动仅获约 29 美分/美元。Canaries 脚注 9 原文经 PDF 逐字比对与 G24-C 所引完全一致。


#### [G25] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:表格的**结构判断成立**(三项研究确实回答三个不同问题、不能互为证据),核心附加论断「厂商在争论两端都有人」也**站得住**。但有三格需要修:
(1) 「利益相关:OpenAI 员工为主要作者」——这句写法**恰好是对的,不要改成「全部 OpenAI」**(核验要求确认这一点:答案是否定的)。四位作者归属逐字为:Tyna Eloundou¹、Sam Manning^{1,2}、Pamela Mishkin*¹、Daniel Rock³,其中 1=OpenAI、2=OpenResearch、3=University of Pennsylvania。即 3/4 为 OpenAI,Daniel Rock 是宾大沃顿,不是 OpenAI。建议直接写「四位作者中三位署 OpenAI,第四位 Daniel Rock 为宾夕法尼亚大学」,比「主要作者」更抗质疑。
(2) Anthropic Economic Index 的「分母」格必须**加版本限定**,否则会高估其代表性。Canaries 用的是 Handa et al. (2025)(即 Index V1),其口径逐字为「over four million Claude.ai conversations」,且**只含 Claude.ai 免费版与 Pro 版,明确排除 API、Team、Enterprise**(Anthropic 自述:「We also only analyze data from Claude.ai Free and Pro plans, rather than API, Team, or Enterprise users.」)。原表述「Claude 对话总数(不是劳动者数,不是工时)」方向对但不够——应改为「Claude.ai 免费版+Pro 版消费级对话数(约 400 万次),不含 API/团队/企业流量;不是劳动者数,不是工时」。这一点对文章很关键:Canaries 的 Fact 3(自动化 vs 增强)整个建立在这份**消费级对话样本**上。
(3) 「不能推出」格建议为 Index 补上 Anthropic 自陈的限定,原文逐字:「we don't argue that the uses in our dataset are a representative sample of AI use in general」,以及「We can't know for certain whether someone using Claude for a task was completing a task for work.」——后一句尤其重要:连「是否在工作中使用」都无法确认。
(4) 「主张 automation 上升的 Index 出自 Anthropic」这一表述若指的是**较新的** Index 版本,需注明版本,因为 V1(Canaries 所用)的结论恰恰是**增强占多数**(57% augmentation vs 43% automation);「automation 上升」出自 V3(2025-09),逐字:directive 类对话「jumped from 27% in V1 in late 2024 to 39% in V3」,且「This is the first report where automation usage exceeds augmentation usage」。→ 应写成「Anthropic 自家 Index 从 V1 到 V3 记录了自动化类用法从 27% 升到 39% 并首次超过增强类」,不能笼统说「Index 主张 automation 上升」,否则与 Canaris 所引的 V1 口径打架。
(5) Eloundou「回答」格的措辞「理论上 LLM 能把哪些任务耗时砍一半」核对无误——该文 E1 判准确为「时间至少减半且不损失质量」,可原样使用。

**核验依据**:Eloundou 归属:一手 PDF https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5 题名页逐字「Tyna Eloundou¹, Sam Manning^{1,2}, Pamela Mishkin*¹, and Daniel Rock³」,脚注机构表逐字「¹OpenAI ²OpenResearch ³University of Pennsylvania」→ 非全部 OpenAI,证伪「全部 OpenAI」的写法,支持「主要作者为 OpenAI 员工」的写法。EIG 报告脚注 3 亦独立列出同一四人名单。
Anthropic Index:Handa et al. (2025) arXiv 2503.04761《Which Economic Tasks are Performed with AI? Evidence from Millions of Claude Conversations》(2025-02-11),摘要逐字「analyze over four million Claude.ai conversations」「57% of usage suggests augmentation... while 43% suggests automation」「only paint a picture of AI usage on a single platform」。Anthropic 官方发布页 https://www.anthropic.com/news/the-anthropic-economic-index 逐字「approximately one million conversations with Claude (specifically, Free and Pro conversations on Claude.ai)」「We also only analyze data from Claude.ai Free and Pro plans, rather than API, Team, or Enterprise users.」「we don't argue that the uses in our dataset are a representative sample of AI use in general」。V3 报告 https://www.anthropic.com/research/anthropic-economic-index-september-2025-report:directive 由「27% in V1 in late 2024 to 39% in V3」,「This is the first report where automation usage exceeds augmentation usage」,且 V3 已加入 1P API 企业数据(与 V1 口径不同)。
Canaries 侧交叉验证:论文 4.3 节逐字确认其自动化/增强分类直接取自 Anthropic Economic Index(「we use data on generative AI usage from the Anthropic Economic Index (Handa et al., 2025)」),脚注 17 以「% of conversations」为单位,证实分母确为对话数;致谢逐字「We are grateful to ADP for access to the data and the Stanford Digital Economy Lab for financial support」,证实「ADP 提供数据」一格。
「两端都有厂商」:EIG 署名已逐字确认为 Google 首席经济学家团队(见 G23),Eloundou 多数作者为 OpenAI,Index 为 Anthropic 自有数据自有分类器 → 该启发式确实不成立,论断成立。


### 第 2 席


#### [G22] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:逐字引语全部属实,但口径框架有一处结构性错误 + 四处数字/限定语必须改。

(1) 最重要:「三个不同口径」→ 实际只有两个口径。摘要的 16% 与 Fact 4 的 15 log points 不是两件事,是同一个事件研究估计。论文自己的 Conclusion 逐字写:「After conditioning on firm-time effects, young workers experienced a 16% relative employment decline in the most exposed occupations.」Figure 4 图注逐字:「Estimates are all relative to occupational exposure quintile 1... Estimates control for firm-time and firm-quintile fixed effects following Equation 4.1.」Chandar 2025-10 的演讲稿也把该图直接标为「Fact 4: 16% relative ↓ in employment for exposed young workers」。所以正确表述是:口径 A = Fact 2 的原始描述性 −6%;口径 B = 带 firm-time + firm-quintile 固定效应、以第一(最低暴露)五分位为基准的事件研究,正文写 15 log points、摘要/结论写 16%。把摘要的 16% 说成「相对最低暴露五分位」而把 Fact 4 说成另一个「带 firm-time 固定效应」的数,是把同一个数拆成了两个。

(2) Fact 2 的 6% 不是「最暴露职业」→ 是**最高两个**五分位。原文逐字:「for the highest two exposure quintiles employment for 22-25 year olds declined by 6% between late 2022 and September 2025」。

(3) 「同期年长组 +6% 至 +9%」→ 论文正文只给一个对照数:「while employment for workers aged 35-49 grew by over 8%」。+6% 至 +9% 这个区间不在论文里。可能是把另一句串了:「employment growth from late 2022 to September 2025 was 5-13% for the lowest three AI exposure quintiles」——那是按暴露度、不是按年龄的口径。

(4) Fact 4 逐字引语漏了一个词:原文是「Estimates for other age groups are **generally** much smaller in magnitude and not statistically significant.」

(5) 基准月是 **2022 年 10 月**(方程 4.1 中 t = −1 对应 October 2022;Figure 1 归一化到 October 2022;Figure A5 是 October 2022 → September 2025),不是笼统的「2022 年末」。

(6) 版本漂移属实,但「截至 2026 年 7 月 Fortune 等仍在引 13%」这个说法把责任推给了外部媒体,漏掉更硬的一条:**斯坦福自己两个官网现在挂着两个互相矛盾的头条数字**。截至 2026-07-24,SIEPR 工作论文页 (siepr.stanford.edu) 摘要仍写「a 13 percent relative decline」,而 Digital Economy Lab 的 PDF 与出版物页写 16%。另外 2025/08 那个 PDF 网址现在直接返回 Nov25 的文件(MD5 完全一致 56046ac749542c444340bfb554e56115),初版只能从 Wayback 取。这一条应当写进文章。

**核验依据**:Nov25 PDF 全文经 pdftotext 逐字核对:https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf(封面日期 November 13, 2025)。摘要逐字与论断一致。数据段逐字:「ADP observes job titles for about 70% of workers in its system. We exclude workers without a recorded job title.」「we have records on 3.5 and 5 million workers each month for our main analysis sample」「keeping only companies that have employee earnings records for each month from January 2021 through September 2025」。因果保留句、ADP 代表性自陈句、脚注 9 三处引语逐字无误。软件开发者逐字:「By September 2025, employment for software developers aged 22-25 declined nearly 20% compared to its peak in late 2022.」
初版 13%:Wayback id_ 原始快照 https://web.archive.org/web/20250901140122id_/https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Canaries_BrynjolfssonChandarChen.pdf,封面 August 26, 2025,摘要逐字「a 13 percent relative decline in employment」。
斯坦福自家仍挂 13%:https://siepr.stanford.edu/publications/working-paper/canaries-coal-mine-six-facts-about-recent-employment-effects-artificial(2026-07-24 抓取,摘要仍为 13 percent)。
2026-02-09 博文原文抓取核对(页面署名日期 February 9, 2026):https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/news/canaries-interest-rates-and-timinga-more-on-recent-drivers-of-employment-changes-for-young-workers —— 逐字「When we explore a more stringent approach to control for other factors, the employment decline is significant only after 2024.」「we find evidence that more AI-exposed jobs are actually less exposed to interest rates on average」,并补充「a relative decline... that starts becoming notable in 2024 (not late 2022 or 2023 as in Figure 2)」。
Chandar 2025-10 演讲稿(Fact 4 标题即 16%):https://www.aaas.org/sites/default/files/2025-10/BharatChandar_CSGWest_AAASEPI_ShapingAIFutureInYourState_Oct2025.pdf


#### [G23] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:五条论点、两处引语、署名与致谢**全部逐字属实**,我逐字比对无一字出入。但有四处口径要补,否则会误导读者:

(1) 报告应给出全称与出处:Zanna Iscenko & Fabien Curto Millet,《Looking for the Ladder: Is AI Impacting Entry-Level Jobs?》,Economic Innovation Group,**2026 年 1 月**,属 EIG 的 American Worker Project。

(2) 「2.38 亿条」→ 原文是「**more than** 238 million US job postings (averaging ~3.3 million monthly) from September 2019 to August 2025」,是下限不是精确值。767 个 6 位 SOC × 21 个 2 位 NAICS 逐字无误。暴露度口径逐字:「We calculate AI exposure quintiles using the data and code provided in the replication package for Eloundou et al. (2024), using GPT-4 β scores and an equal weight scheme. **We understand** this is the same basis for exposure calculations as was used in Brynjolfsson et al. (2025).」——「与 Canaries 同一基础」是 EIG 的**推定**(We understand),不是双方确认过的事实,写文章时不要去掉这个限定。

(3) 「大企业」必须写明是 **250 人以上**(脚注 2:「The largest business category in the U.S. Census Bureau's Business Trends and Outlook Survey covers companies with 250+ employees」)。而且两个数不是同一个口径:2023 Q4 的「不到 10%」是**计划**未来六个月使用(planned),同期**实际**使用率更低——脚注 2 逐字:「fewer than 6 percent of businesses with 250 employees or more reported using AI technologies in the past two weeks for the production of goods and services」。2025 Q3 的 12% 原文写作「adoption by large businesses has only climbed to 12 percent」,EIG 自己没有点明这是 planned 还是 current,不要替它断言。

(4) 必须同时写出 EIG 自己的脚注 1 自限,否则读者会高估这份反驳的射程:「Our comments in this note were written about the original version of the 'Canaries' paper published on 26 August 2025... We understand that Brynjolfsson and co-authors are helpfully continuing to update their analysis based on feedback they received, including from us, and we link to the latest version of their paper available.」注意这条与 G22 里「EIG 的批评针对的是 13% 那版」的关系要说清:**分析**确实是针对 8 月版写的,但 EIG 正文里引用的头条数字已经更新成 16%(「Its headline finding is striking: a 16 percent relative decline in employment for early-career workers (ages 22–25) in the most AI-exposed occupations」)。所以不能说「EIG 批的是 13% 那版所以过时了」,也不能说 EIG 批的就是 16% 那版——正确表述是:**分析基于 8 月版,引用数字已换成 11 月版的 16%,EIG 自陈未针对更新版重做分析。**

**核验依据**:https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf 全文 pdftotext 逐字核对。核心主张句、hiring-freeze 25 percent 段落、「There is no evidence that job postings for junior roles within occupations most exposed to AI have declined more than postings for senior positions.」、「absence of evidence is not the same as evidence of absence」四处引语与论断**逐字完全一致**。时点:「vacancies for the highest AI exposure quintile of occupations peaked in March–April 2022 and declined sharply throughout the remainder of the year」+「This hiring slowdown predates any plausible generative AI effect by over six months.」共线性:「Approximately 38 percent of workers in the most AI-exposed quintile are employed in these sectors, compared to less than 2 percent in the least-exposed quintile.」(基于 2023 Census data)。署名逐字:「Zanna Iscenko is AI & Economy Lead, Chief Economist's Team, Google; and Fabien Curto Millet is Chief Economist, Google.」致谢逐字含 David Autor, Diane Coyle, Mohamed El-Erian, James Manyika, **Ruth Porat**, Michael Pisa, Michael Spence, Scott Strand, **Kent Walker** and David Weller,研究助理 Guillaume Aimard。数据附录逐字见 Annex: Data Sources。


#### [G24] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:三份证据都有实质性口径问题,A 引错版本、B 引的是已被取代的旧稿、C 的三个系数取不到且方向被简化。

**A. Yale Budget Lab —— 不存在 2026-04-16 更新版。**
《Evaluating the Impact of AI on the Labor Market: Current State of Affairs》(Gimbel, Kinder, Kendall, Lee)现网页署名日期是 **2025 年 10 月 1 日**,正文口径是「this 33-month period」、CPS 数据到 2025 年 7 月。我加了 cache-buster 重抓,仍是 10 月 1 日;Budget Lab 的 AI 专题页只有 5 篇,没有任何 2026-04-16 的条目。三条引语中有三条对得上这一版(逐字无误):「While the occupational mix is changing more quickly than it has in the past, it is not a large difference and predates the widespread introduction of AI in the workforce.」/「Currently, measures of exposure, automation, and augmentation show no sign of being related to changes in employment or unemployment.」/「our analysis is not predictive of the future.」
但第四条**查无此文**:「an uptick in the dissimilarity of occupational mix between older and younger college graduates, though this remains at the high end of the historical range」不在这一版里。这一版的原话是:「The dissimilarity has increased slightly faster in recent months than it did in a previous time period, which could be consistent with a recent paper from Brynjolfsson et al. showing a possible impact of AI on employment of early career workers. **It could also simply reflect a slowing labor market.** However, our results should be interpreted with caution particularly given small sample sizes.」以及「the dissimilarity between older and more recent college graduates rarely deviates outside of the 30-33% range」。
另外年龄口径要改:比较的是**应届毕业生 20–24 岁 vs 25–34 岁**,不是笼统的「older and younger college graduates」。历史基准可确认:1984–1989(PC)、1996–2002(互联网)、2016–2019(对照),方法是 Duncan & Duncan 相异性指数 + 12 个月移动平均。
「2026Q1 青年失业率上升迹象统计不显著」不在这篇里。最接近的一手来源是 Budget Lab **另一篇**《What We Do and Don't Know About How AI is Affecting the Labor Market》(**2026 年 5 月 6 日**,合成 DiD),逐字:「AI-exposed unemployment has risen somewhat more than unemployment of the comparison group, though the difference is not statistically significant.」以及关于年轻人的:「We therefore investigated effects for workers 34 and younger, **finding mixed evidence of AI effects for this group**. To be clear, this is a much broader group than the early career subgroup that has been the focus of AI-related research and discussion in Brynjolfsson, Chandar, and Chen (2025) and elsewhere.」——这是「混合证据 + 34 岁以下」,不是「青年无效应 + 22–25 岁」,不能当反方证据直接用。

**B. Humlum & Vestergaard —— 引的是已被取代的 2025-07 稿,数字已从 1% 改成 2%。**
当前版本是 NBER WP 33777(编号未变),但已改题为《**Still Waters, Rapid Currents: Early Labor Market Transformation under Generative AI**》,**2026 年 3 月 13 日**,状态 Under Review。现摘要逐字:「using difference-in-differences, we estimate precise null effects on earnings and recorded hours at both the worker and workplace levels, **ruling out effects larger than 2%** two years after the launch of ChatGPT.」——**是 2%,不是 1%**。而且「Modest productivity gains (3% time savings)」和「Our findings challenge narratives of imminent disruption」**两句都已从摘要中删除**(旧稿正文实为「average time savings amount to 2.8% of work hours」)。调查口径可确认:两轮 2023 年 11–12 月、2024 年 11–12 月,每轮邀请 115,000 人、约 25,000 份有效问卷、7,000 家职场、11 个高暴露职业。
**采集者未定位到的 early-career 章节确实存在——在 2026-03 版,不在 2025-07 版。** 我在 2025-07 稿全文只搜到一处「entry-level」,还是脚注里引 Amodei 的媒体预测。2026-03 版有专节 §3.4 Workplace Employment + 附录 D.2,逐字:「Appendix D.2 documents aggregate declines in early-career employment in several of our exposed occupations in Denmark, mirroring patterns documented in the United States (Brynjolfsson, Chandar and Chen, 2025).」「Crucially, Panel (b) shows that the decline in early-career jobs is not driven by firms adopting AI chatbots. Difference-in-differences estimates show no differential changes in the employment share of early-career workers at adopting workplaces, with estimates precise enough to rule out effects greater than a third of a percentage point.」
所以 EIG 与 Canaries 的转述**成立,但必须改写口径**:不是「该文发现入门级为零效应」,而是「该文**复现了**入门级就业下滑,但用 DiD 把下滑拆开后,采用 AI 的职场与未采用的职场没有差别,故 AI 采用最多只能解释总降幅的一小部分(作者自算:0.43×0.33≈0.14 个百分点,相对约 0.6 个百分点的总降幅)」。这是「归因否定」,不是「现象否定」——两者对准大学生的含义完全不同。

**C. Johnston & Makridis —— 三个系数 UNVERIFIABLE,且「方向相反」的描述被简化到失真。**
+10% / +3.9% / +4.8% 我**未能**从任何一手文本取得,判 UNVERIFIABLE。试过的路径:SSRN 5375017 页面(WebFetch 403 / curl 403 / r.jina.ai 403,均为 Cloudflare 挑战);SSRN Delivery.cfm 的 Wayback 快照(只存到 JS 中间页,非 PDF);Semantic Scholar(按 DOI 查无此条);Unpaywall(is_oa=true 但 url_for_pdf 为 null);OpenAlex(有条目无全文);两位作者个人主页(christosmakridis.com 工作论文页为 JS 渲染、json-pretty 端点无此条;acjohnston.com 无 PDF 链接);Google Scholar / Bing / DuckDuckGo(均挑战墙)。
但我从 Wayback 取到了 SSRN 摘要页(2026-01-04 id_ 快照),摘要里**没有**这三个系数,也没有「2017–2024」和「每增一个标准差」的表述,并且有一句 G24 完全略掉的限定,方向与「方向相反」的框架冲突:「**However, sectors where AI can directly substitute for human labor saw employment reductions.**」还有一句同样该写进文章:「Wage gains are concentrated among younger and more educated workers.」设计口径原文是「a comprehensive census of U.S. employers and a difference-in-differences design. The design exploits (i) the rollout of workplace AI tools beginning in 2021 and (ii) cross-sector variation in occupational AI exposure.」日期 2025 年 7 月 31 日。
所以正确表述:该文的结论是**条件性**的——互补型暴露对应工资/就业上升,可直接替代人力的部门则就业下降;把它当成「AI 对就业净正面」的单向反证,是对原文的曲解。另外利益相关也该披露:Makridis 的 SSRN 挂靠含 **The Gallup Organization**;Johnston 现职 University of Texas at Austin。Canaries 脚注 9 的反驳引语逐字无误。

**核验依据**:A: https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/evaluating-impact-ai-labor-market-current-state-affairs(2026-07-24 加 cache-buster 抓取,页面署名 October 1, 2025,正文「33-month period」,附录列 1984-1989 / 1996-2002 / 2016-2019 与 Duncan & Duncan 方法);专题页 https://budgetlab.yale.edu/topic/artificial-intelligence 仅 5 条,无 2026-04 条目;https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/what-we-do-and-dont-know-about-how-ai-affecting-labor-market(2026 年 5 月 6 日)。
B: 旧稿 https://www.andershumlum.com/s/chatbots_july25.pdf(July 15, 2025,摘要「ruling out effects above 1%」「Modest productivity gains (3% time savings)」);新稿 https://www.andershumlum.com/s/chatbots_260313.pdf(March 13, 2026,题为 Still Waters, Rapid Currents,摘要「ruling out effects larger than 2%」,脚注「previously circulated under the title 'Large Language Models, Small Labor Market Effects'」);NBER https://www.nber.org/papers/w33777 页面标题已改为 Still Waters, Rapid Currents。§3.4 与附录 D.2、脚注 21 的 0.43×0.33≈0.14 计算均在新稿全文中逐字核对。
C: SSRN 摘要页经 Wayback id_ 快照取得 https://web.archive.org/web/20260104154816id_/https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=5375017(标注 July 31, 2025,DOI 10.2139/ssrn.5375017);全文 PDF 各路径均失败,故三系数判 UNVERIFIABLE。Canaries 脚注 9 见 Nov25 PDF。


#### [G25] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:表的骨架成立,「两端都有厂商」这条**站得住**,但有三格必须改写,否则这张要直接印进文章的表本身就会传播口径错误。

(1)**「利益相关: OpenAI 员工为主要作者」——不能写成「全部 OpenAI」。** arXiv v5 标题页脚注逐字:Tyna Eloundou¹, Sam Manning^{1,2}, Pamela Mishkin*¹, Daniel Rock³,其中 1 = OpenAI,2 = OpenResearch,3 = University of Pennsylvania。即四位作者中三位挂 OpenAI(Manning 同时挂 OpenResearch),**Daniel Rock 在宾大沃顿,与 OpenAI 无隶属**。建议改为「四位作者中三位隶属 OpenAI;第四作者 Daniel Rock 在宾夕法尼亚大学」。

(2)**Eloundou 那一格的「回答」写得不准,而且不准的方向正好放大了这套数据的确定性。** Canaries 与 EIG 用的都是 **GPT-4 β** 分数,而 β ≠「LLM 能把任务耗时砍一半」。论文逐字:β = E1 + 0.5*E2;E1 定义为「using the described LLM via ChatGPT or the OpenAI playground can decrease the time required to complete the DWA or task by at least half (50%)」;E2 定义为「access to the described LLM alone would **not** reduce the time... by at least half, but additional software **could be developed on top of the LLM** that could reduce the time... by at least half」。也就是说 β 里有一半权重是「再开发一层软件之后才可能砍一半」的假想任务。而且阈值是「在同等质量下」砍一半,论文对 equivalent quality 的定义是「a third party, typically the recipient of the output, would not notice or care about LLM assistance」。建议该格改为:「理论上、在同等质量下,直接用 LLM 能把耗时砍一半的任务(E1),**加上半权重的**『再造一层软件后能砍一半』的任务(E2)」。

(3)**Anthropic Index 的「数据性质/分母」要更新到 V3。** 「分母 = Claude 对话总数(不是劳动者数,不是工时)」这句本身可逐字坐实——报告原文:「The unit of observation is a conversation with Claude on Claude.ai, not a user, so it is possible that multiple conversations from the same user are included.」但 2025 年 9 月的 V3 报告已经**另加一套** 1P API 数据(2025 年 8 月随机抽取的 100 万条 API transcript,与 100 万条 Claude.ai 对话分开统计)。所以「单一厂商平台使用日志分布」应写成「单一厂商的 Claude.ai 对话 + 其 1P API transcript,两套分别统计」。另外若文章要用「automation 上升」这个说法,应给出具体口径:directive(完全委派)占比由 V1(2024 年末)27% 升至 V3 39%,是首份 automation 超过 augmentation 的报告;企业 API 侧约 77% 为 automation,Claude.ai 侧约 50%。

(4)Canaries 那一格的分母建议收紧:不是泛指「ADP 覆盖企业的在职人数」,而是「**有职位名称记录**(ADP 只对约 70% 的劳动者观测到职位名称,其余被剔除)、全职、有正收入、70 岁以下、且所在企业 2021-01 至 2025-09 每月都在 ADP 的在职人数」,每月约 350 万–500 万人。

(5)**「两端都有厂商」这条 HOLDS,而且可以写得更硬。** 高暴露度那一端:Eloundou et al. 三分之二以上作者是 OpenAI 员工;automation 上升那一端:Anthropic 用自家日志、自家分类器;「AI 没在杀入门级岗位」那一端:EIG 报告由 Google 首席经济学家 Fabien Curto Millet 与 Google 首席经济学家团队的 AI & Economy Lead Zanna Iscenko 撰写,致谢含 Ruth Porat、Kent Walker、James Manyika。补一条我在核验中发现的旁证:Humlum & Vestergaard 2026 年 3 月版的致谢里同时感谢「OpenAI Economic Research Conference」与会者,并披露「Caspar Ringhof and **Claude Code (Opus 4.6)** provided excellent research assistance」。厂商影响是弥散的、双向的,「厂商=夸大 AI 影响」这条启发式确实不成立。

(6)顺带一条支持性证据可以用:Eloundou 摘要里自己写了「We do not make predictions about the development or adoption timeline of such LLMs.」——这正好逐字支撑表中「不能推出:采用时间表」那一格。

**核验依据**:Eloundou 作者机构与 β 定义:arXiv v5 PDF 标题页与正文 pdftotext 逐字核对,https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5(标题页脚注 1 OpenAI / 2 OpenResearch / 3 University of Pennsylvania;E1/E2 定义框与「(ii) 𝛽, which is the sum of E1 and 0.5*E2, where the 0.5 weight on E2 is intended to account for exposure when deploying the technology via complementary tools and applications necessitates additional investment」);摘要页 https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.10130。
Anthropic Economic Index V3(2025 年 9 月报告)口径与 27%→39% automation 数字:https://www.anthropic.com/research/anthropic-economic-index-september-2025-report。
Canaries 分母:https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf §3.1 逐字。
EIG 署名与致谢:https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf 文末与 Acknowledgements。
Humlum 致谢与 Claude Code 披露:https://www.andershumlum.com/s/chatbots_260313.pdf 标题页脚注。


### 第 3 席


#### [G22] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 最重要的一处:「三个不同口径的数字」错了——摘要的 16% 与 Fact 4 的 15 log points 是同一个估计,不是两个独立口径。§4.4 是 Poisson 事件研究(firm-time + firm-quintile 固定效应,基准组=暴露五分位 1),正文写「a 15 log point decline in relative employment」,而结论逐字写「After conditioning on firm-time effects, young workers experienced a 16% relative employment decline in the most exposed occupations」——exp(0.15)−1 ≈ 16.2%,即摘要的 16%。所以全文只有两个独立测量:描述性绝对值 −6%(无控制)与回归相对值 −16% ≈ −15 log points。把二者并列为互相印证的「不同口径」= 同一个数字被数了两次。改为:「一个描述性绝对数(−6%)+ 一个带 firm-time 固定效应的相对数(−16%,即 15 log points,相对最低暴露五分位),外加软件开发这一职业的描述性 −20%」。(2) Fact 4 引语漏字:原文是「Estimates for other age groups are generally much smaller in magnitude and not statistically significant」,claim 漏掉了 generally。(3) 13%→16% 的版本漂移原因被说错了。作者 2026-02-09 博文逐字自陈:「With the fixed-effect approach, the employment changes start later, but they are growing (reaching about 16% by October 2025, vs 13% in our paper with data only through July), and they have not shown a reversal.」——即 13%→16% 是数据窗口延长 + 效应本身在增长,不是重新估计口径。原表述「初版头条数字约 13%,Nov25 版 16%」应补上作者给出的解释。(4)「EIG 的批评针对的是 13% 那版」只对了一半:EIG 脚注 1 逐字为「Our comments in this note were written about the original version of the "Canaries" paper published on 26 August 2025」,但 EIG 2026-01 正式发表的正文本身引的是「a 16 percent relative decline」并链接最新版。改为:「EIG 的评论撰写时针对 8/26 初版,但其 2026-01 发表稿正文已改引 16%。」(5)「作者已让步的部分」框架偏颇。2026-02-09 博文主体是**反驳**利率解释(结论:「we do not find strong support for the hypothesis that interest rate changes explain the disproportionate decline」),时点只是其中一处部分让步,且同文强调效应在增长、未出现反转。写进文章时必须两面都给。(6) 时点引语有歧义风险:「the employment decline is significant only after 2024」确为正文逐字,但同一博文另两处写「becomes significant only in 2024」「starts in 2024, not late 2022 or 2023」。「after 2024」易被读成 2025 之后,建议引用「starts in 2024, not late 2022 or 2023」。(7) 代表性自陈可以更重:除 claim 引的那句外,脚注 13 逐字补充 ADP「somewhat overrepresents firms in the Northeast」且「firms using ADP tend to grow faster on average than the typical firm in the US economy」——这是样本构成偏差,不只是「不完全匹配」。(8) 8 月初版 PDF 已无法一手取得(/2025/08/ 路径与出版页现均返回 CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf;本环境 WebFetch 被禁访 web.archive.org,浏览器打开 Wayback id_ 快照触发下载对话框未取);13% 仅由作者本人 2026-02 博文间接确认。「Fortune 等仍在引 13%」本次未核实(WebSearch 配额已耗尽),不要当作已验证事实写进文章。

**核验依据**:一手 PDF 逐字核对(pdftotext 提取 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Canaries_BrynjolfssonChandarChen.pdf,该路径现返回 2025-11-13 版,与出版页 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf 同):标题页「November 13, 2025」;摘要「Early-career workers (ages 22-25) in AI-exposed occupations experienced 16% relative employment declines, controlling for firm-level shocks」✓;Fact 2「workers aged 22 to 25 have experienced a 6% decline in employment from late 2022 to September 2025 in the most AI-exposed occupations, compared to a 6-9% increase for older workers」✓;§4.4 标题「Fact 4: Employment declines for young, AI-exposed workers remain after conditioning on firm-time shocks」,正文「a 15 log point decline in relative employment」;图 4 注「Estimates are all relative to occupational exposure quintile 1」;结论第四条「a 16% relative employment decline」;「By September 2025, employment for software developers aged 22-25 declined nearly 20% compared to its peak in late 2022」✓;「ADP observes job titles for about 70% of workers in its system. We exclude workers without a recorded job title.」✓;「records on 3.5 and 5 million workers each month for our main analysis sample」✓;「each month from January 2021 through September 2025」✓;caution 引语与 representativeness 引语均逐字命中(§1 与 §3.1)。出版页 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/publications/canaries-in-the-coal-mine/ 标注 WORKING PAPER、Date 11/13/2025。博文全文经浏览器读取 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/news/canaries-interest-rates-and-timinga-more-on-recent-drivers-of-employment-changes-for-young-workers/(2026-02-09,Brynjolfsson/Chandar/Chen),含「more AI-exposed jobs are actually less exposed to interest rates on average」与上引 16%/13% 句。


#### [G23] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:五条论点、两处核心引语、署名与致谢**全部逐字命中**,这是本批最扎实的一条。需修正三处口径 + 补一处必须写进文章的独立反证:(1) 数据量:原文是「more than 238 million US job postings (averaging ~3.3 million monthly) from September 2019 to August 2025」——「共 2.38 亿条」应改为「超过 2.38 亿条」。(2)「与 Canaries 同一基础」**说过头了**,且很可能不完全成立。EIG 自己只敢写「We calculate AI exposure quintiles using the data and code provided in the replication package for Eloundou et al. (2024), using GPT-4 β scores and an **equal weight scheme**. We **understand** this is the same basis for exposure calculations as was used in Brynjolfsson et al. (2025).」——是 hedged 的推定,不是核实。而 Eloundou 原文(arXiv 2303.10130v5)脚注 6/7 明确:占主导的职业级 β 聚合把 O*NET「core」任务权重设为「supplemental」的两倍(「Core tasks are given twice the weight of supplemental tasks, and all weights sum to one」),只有部分图使用等权;Canaries §3.2 则写「We focus on the GPT-4 based β exposure measures from their paper」,即直接取用已发表的职业级测度。因此 EIG 的等权重算法**未必**等同 Canaries 所用口径。改为:「EIG 用 Eloundou 复现包的 GPT-4 β 分数重算五分位、采等权重方案,并**自称推定**与 Canaries 同源;但 Eloundou 主口径对 core 任务加倍加权,两者是否严格同一未经双方核实。」(3) 采用率两个数字并非严格同一序列:2023 Q4 是「fewer than 10 percent of large businesses surveyed were even **planning** to use AI in the next six months」,2025 Q3 是「adoption by large businesses has only climbed to 12 percent」。且脚注 2 给出更狠的一手补充:「fewer than 6 percent of businesses with 250 employees or more reported **using** AI technologies in the past two weeks」,并说明「大企业」= 250+ 员工(BTOS 最大分组)。写进文章时应用脚注 2 这个「实际使用<6%」,比「计划<10%」更硬。(4) **必须补的独立反证**:EIG 全篇立论支点是「AI 暴露度 ≈ 利率敏感度」,但这一条被两个独立来源同时打脸。其一,Brynjolfsson 等 2026-02-09 用 Zens et al.(2020)原数据得出「more AI-exposed jobs are actually less exposed to interest rates on average」。其二——更值得写,因为它不是 Canaries 作者的自辩——Yale Budget Lab 2026-05-07 独立测得「AI-exposed occupations are generally less sensitive to the business cycle than unexposed occupations」,并把这一点当作方法学难题写进 Key Takeaways。EIG 的共线性论点(38% vs <2%)与「利率敏感度更高」的推论因此不能等同,前者成立不蕴含后者。(5) 无关紧要但见文本质量:EIG 脚注 3 把 Daniel Rock 的名字印成「Daniel Rockloundou」。

**核验依据**:一手 PDF 逐字核对(pdftotext 提取 https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf,《Looking for the Ladder: Is AI Impacting Entry-Level Jobs?》Zanna Iscenko & Fabien Curto Millet, January 2026):「vacancies for the highest AI exposure quintile of occupations peaked in March–April 2022 and declined sharply throughout the remainder of the year」✓;招聘冻结假想段与「shrunk by a shocking 25 percent while it remained level for all other age groups」✓ 一字不差;「Approximately 38 percent of workers in the most AI-exposed quintile are employed in these sectors, compared to less than 2 percent in the least-exposed quintile」✓(「An analysis based on 2023 Census data」);「There is no evidence that job postings for junior roles within occupations most exposed to AI have declined more than postings for senior positions.」✓;「The most plausible explanation is that the data patterns observed are not early warnings of large-scale technological displacement, but rather the predictable consequences of a classic macroeconomic shock: the sharpest monetary policy tightening cycle in four decades.」✓;「absence of evidence is not the same as evidence of absence」✓;署名行「Zanna Iscenko is AI & Economy Lead, Chief Economist's Team, Google; and Fabien Curto Millet is Chief Economist, Google.」✓ 一字不差;致谢逐字含「David Autor, Diane Coyle, Mohamed El-Erian, James Manyika, Ruth Porat, Michael Pisa, Michael Spence, Scott Strand, Kent Walker and David Weller」(研究助理 Guillaume Aimard)✓;Annex 数据段与脚注 1/2/3 如上引。交叉证据:Eloundou 加权方案见 https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5 脚注 6、7;Canaries §3.2 见上条 PDF;Budget Lab 周期敏感度见浏览器读取 https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/what-we-do-and-dont-know-about-how-ai-affecting-labor-market(2026-05-07)。


#### [G24] REFUTED · confidence=high

**修正**:三份证据里 **B 与 C 的引用口径均已过时,C 的三个系数不再是该文的结论**;A 的引语属实但被贴错了日期与出处。逐项:

【A Yale】引语全部逐字属实,但**不是「2026-04-16 更新版」**——它们出自 2025-10-01 报告《Evaluating the Impact of AI on the Labor Market: Current State of Affairs》(Gimbel, Kinder, Kendall, Lee)。Budget Lab 的 AI 专题页**不存在 2026-04-16 的出版物**;该报告已被 2026-06-15 的交互式追踪器《Tracking the Impact of AI on the Labor Market》接替,而追踪器的 Key Takeaways 已**改写**为「The occupational mix is not yet changing in ways that clearly align with the introduction of AI into the workforce.」与「Measures of AI usage show no connection to changes in employment or unemployment.」——claim 引的那两句旧措辞在现行版本已不复存在。此外「an uptick in the dissimilarity of occupational mix between older and younger college graduates, though this remains at the high end of the historical range」**在 2025-10 报告、2026-06 追踪器中均未出现**(报告对应处实为「The dissimilarity has increased slightly faster in recent months than it did in a previous time period」与「rarely deviates outside of the 30-33% range」),本次未能定位其一手出处,不要按逐字引语使用。「2026Q1 青年失业率上升迹象统计不显著」确有其事但**出自另一篇**:2026-05-07 分析(Gimbel, Kendall, Nunn),且口径完全不同——是 SDID 下 AI 暴露职业的**失业率**效应,全样本约 +0.5 个百分点、**16–34 岁**子样本更大,「both are statistically insignificant as of the first quarter of 2026」;既不是 22–25 岁,也不是相异性指数。对照期口径:附录写 1984–1989(PC)、1996–2002(互联网)、2016–2019(对照),正文仅点名「January 1996 is the baseline month」,claim 的 1984-01/2016-01 月份精度原文未给。

【B Humlum & Vestergaard】**引的是已被取代的版本**。现行版:《Still Waters, Rapid Currents: Early Labor Market Transformation under Generative AI》,2026-03-13,NBER w33777(May 2025 发布、March 2026 修订),扉页注明「previously circulated under the title "Large Language Models, Small Labor Market Effects"」。新摘要:「ruling out effects larger than **2%** two years after the launch of ChatGPT」——**不是 1%**;「Modest productivity gains (3% time savings)」与「Our findings challenge narratives of imminent disruption」**已从摘要删除**,全文再无 narrative 一词。→ 修正为:「置信区间可排除大于 **2%** 的效应(ChatGPT 发布两年后),2026-03 版。」
关于 early-career:**该章节现在存在**,采集者未定位是因为看的是 2025-07 版。现行版 §3.4 + 附录 D.2《By Age Groups (Brynjolfsson, Chandar and Chen, 2025)》。但 EIG/Canaries 的转述若被写成「对入门级为零效应」是**错的**:H&V 明确**复现了**丹麦入门级就业下降,他们排除的是「由采用 AI 的职场驱动」这一条。逐字:「While we replicate the pattern of declining early-career employment in exposed occupations in Denmark, our difference-in-differences analysis reveals that the declines are not driven by firms adopting AI chatbots.」以及「the decline in early-career jobs is not driven by firms adopting AI chatbots... with estimates precise enough to rule out effects greater than a third of a percentage point」;脚注 21 算得 AI 采用最多只能解释 0.43×0.33≈0.14 个百分点,而 2022-10 至 2024-12 的入门级份额总降幅约 0.6 个百分点。→ 正确口径:「丹麦入门级就业确实在下降,但采用 AI 的职场与未采用的职场没有差别;按其置信上界,AI 采用至多解释总降幅的约四分之一。」H&V 自己也写明这批下降「whose connection to generative AI is itself debated (Frank et al., 2026; Iscenko and Curto Millet, 2026)」。

【C Johnston & Makridis】**三个系数全部作废,判 REFUTED。** SSRN 5375017《AI, Output, and Employment》(Andrew C. Johnston, UT Austin;Christos Makridis, ASU 等)posted 1 Aug 2025,**last revised 25 Mar 2026**。现行摘要逐字:「A one standard deviation increase in exposure raises output by **7%**, with effects emerging in **2021** when enterprise AI tools entered the market. Employment effects follow the same timing but diverge by exposure type: where AI likely requires human collaboration, employment rises **4%**; where AI can perform tasks independently, **we find no significant employment effect**. Results are robust to state-by-year and industry-by-year fixed effects and suggest AI has caused a **decrease in the labor share of income**.」→ 逐项改:生产率 +10% → **产出 +7%**;就业 +3.9% → **仅在「需要人机协作」的暴露类型上 +4%,在「AI 可独立完成」的类型上不显著**;工资 +4.8% → **现行摘要根本没有工资系数**;「2017–2024」→ 摘要称效应自 **2021** 年出现。更关键的是**方向标签也要改**:该文现在同时报告劳动收入份额**下降**,不能再当作单纯「方向相反的乐观证据」放进反方栏。全文 PDF 仍取不到(SSRN Delivery.cfm 对 WebFetch 返回 403,浏览器内该链接跳回摘要页),故 QCEW 确认与正文级系数**仍属 UNVERIFIABLE**——但仅凭权威摘要已足以否掉那三个数字,任何仍写 +10%/+3.9%/+4.8% 的段落必须删。
Canaries 脚注 9 的引语本身逐字无误(结尾还有「(Hampole et al., 2025)」),但它反驳的是该文**旧版**结论,文章若并置需说明。

**核验依据**:【A】浏览器读取 https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/evaluating-impact-ai-labor-market-current-state-affairs(署 Martha Gimbel, Molly Kinder, Joshua Kendall, Maddie Lee,October 1, 2025),Key Takeaways 与「Of course, our analysis is not predictive of the future.」逐字命中;附录对照期 1984-1989/1996-2002/2016-2019。专题页 https://budgetlab.yale.edu/topic/artificial-intelligence 列出全部 AI 出版物,无 2026-04-16 条目。https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/tracking-impact-ai-labor-market 显示「June 15, 2026 | Updated: June 15, 2026」及改写后的 Key Takeaways。https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/what-we-do-and-dont-know-about-how-ai-affecting-labor-market(2026-05-07,Gimbel/Kendall/Nunn)含 16–34 岁失业率与「both are statistically insignificant as of the first quarter of 2026」。【B】pdftotext 逐字提取 https://www.andershumlum.com/s/chatbots_260313.pdf(302→static1.squarespace.com/.../chatbots_260313.pdf):标题《Still Waters, Rapid Currents》、2026-03-13、新摘要「ruling out effects larger than 2%」;§3.4、附录 D.2、脚注 21 如上引;旧版 https://www.andershumlum.com/s/chatbots_july25.pdf(2025-07-15)摘要确含「ruling out effects above 1%」「Modest productivity gains (3% time savings)」「challenge narratives of imminent disruption from Generative AI」,并经 grep 确认全文无 early-career 专章。https://www.nber.org/papers/w33777 现题为 Still Waters, Rapid Currents,Issue Date May 2025 / Revision Date March 2026。【C】浏览器读取 https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=5375017(Cloudflare 挑战自行放行后读取,未做任何绕过),显示 Posted: 1 Aug 2025 / Last revised: 25 Mar 2026 及上引现行摘要全文;PDF 下载链 https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/Delivery.cfm/5375017.pdf?abstractid=5375017&mirid=1 对 WebFetch 返回 403、浏览器内跳回摘要页,故正文未取。Canaries 脚注 9 见 Nov25 版 PDF。


#### [G25] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:表格结构与三项研究的构念区分**成立**,可以直接用;需修四处,其中第 1 条最要紧,因为它写的是别人的雇主。(1)「利益相关:OpenAI 员工为主要作者」**不准确,四位里只有三位是 OpenAI**。arXiv 2303.10130v5 扉页作者块:Tyna Eloundou¹、Sam Manning^{1,2}、Pamela Mishkin*¹、Daniel Rock³,其中 1=OpenAI、2=OpenResearch、3=University of Pennsylvania。**Daniel Rock 不是 OpenAI 员工**(宾大沃顿)。改为:「前三位作者出自 OpenAI(Manning 兼 OpenResearch),第四作者 Daniel Rock 在宾夕法尼亚大学。」把一位学界作者写成厂商员工,对一篇要教读者查利益相关的文章是自伤。(2)「利益相关:ADP 提供数据」偏轻。Canaries 致谢逐字为「We are grateful to ADP for access to the data and the Stanford Digital Economy Lab for financial support」;更要紧的是脚注 13 的样本构成自陈——ADP「somewhat overrepresents firms in the Northeast」且「firms using ADP tend to grow faster on average than the typical firm in the US economy」。这不是「谁给的数据」问题,是分母本身偏向增长更快的企业。建议该格改为「ADP 提供数据;且 ADP 客户企业增长快于全美典型企业、东北部超配」。(3)「不能推出:『AI 是原因』」正确,但对准大学生还不够。按作者本人 2026-02-09 的更新,加入 firm-time 固定效应后显著性起点已移到 2024 年,即**该测量本身仍在移动**。表格若呈现为三项已定型的测量,会制造虚假的确定性;建议在 Canaries 列加一句「且该估计随数据窗口变动(2025-07 数据 13% → 2025-10 数据 16%)」。(4)「厂商在这场争论的两端都有人」**成立,且比原文写的更强**——可以放心加重。EIG 报告不只是「由 Google 首席经济学家撰写」:两位作者分别是 Google 首席经济学家团队的 AI & Economy Lead 与 Google 首席经济学家,致谢名单里还逐字列有 Ruth Porat、Kent Walker、James Manyika 三位 Google 高管。因此「厂商=夸大 AI 影响」这条启发式确实不成立,而且最深的厂商指纹恰在「AI 没在杀入门级岗位」这一侧。附带可用的第三方佐证:Yale Budget Lab 独立评估 Anthropic 数据后写道「data from Claude usage alone is not representative of how workers across the economy are using AI chatbots and tools」,正好支撑表中 Index 列的「不能推出:劳动力总体 AI 使用率」。其余各格(Eloundou 的「砍一半」定义、不作采用时间表预测、Anthropic 自有分类器、三者分母)均逐字核实无误。

**核验依据**:Eloundou 归属与定义:pdftotext 提取 https://arxiv.org/pdf/2303.10130v5,扉页「Tyna Eloundou1, Sam Manning1,2, Pamela Mishkin*1, and Daniel Rock3 / 1 OpenAI / 2 OpenResearch / 3 University of Pennsylvania」;暴露度 E1 定义「using the described LLM via ChatGPT or the OpenAI playground can decrease the time required to complete the DWA or task by at least half (50%)」;摘要「We do not make predictions about the development or adoption timeline of such LLMs.」;正文「technical feasibility does not guarantee labor productivity or automation outcomes」。Anthropic Index:https://www.anthropic.com/news/the-anthropic-economic-index(Claude.ai Free/Pro 对话经 Clio 映射到 O*NET 任务,automation/augmentation 分类),Canaries §3.2 与脚注 14 逐字「based on several million conversations with Claude」「Directive and Feedback Loop conversations are considered Automative; Task Iteration, Learning, and Validation are considered Augmentative」「They also instruct the model to choose None of the above "liberally."」。Canaries 致谢与脚注 13 见 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/CanariesintheCoalMine_Nov25.pdf。EIG 署名与致谢见 https://eig.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/TAWP-Iscenko-Millet.pdf 末页。Claude 数据代表性的第三方评价见 https://budgetlab.yale.edu/research/evaluating-impact-ai-labor-market-current-state-affairs。16%/13% 随数据窗口变动见 https://digitaleconomy.stanford.edu/news/canaries-interest-rates-and-timinga-more-on-recent-drivers-of-employment-changes-for-young-workers/。

#### [G26] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) 论断A的两处引语逐字无误(NAE 2018, Appendix D, pp.180–181),但"H-1B 扩张把调整期压缩到约一年"是过度解读。原文:"Since the expansion in 1990 of the number of skilled immigrant temporary visas permitted, immigration has allowed just such a short cut to the equilibrium. The number of engineers can be changed with no more than a year's lag (the time to apply for the annual visa distribution), so the long-run supply curve is flatter: the cycles are both faster and associated with smaller wage changes." → 正确口径:"一年"指的是**供给量可被改变的最短滞后**(申请年度签证配额所需时间),报告只说"周期更快、工资波动更小",没有说整个调整期=一年;且原文说的是"skilled immigrant temporary visas"(1990 年移民法扩容),不专指 H-1B。(2) 对核验点(d)的回答:Appendix D **纯粹是模型推演,不是计量估计**——全文无数据、无回归、无样本,措辞是 "the market is likely to stay at point b"、"there is likely to be talk of a 'glut'";四年是"if supply is dependent on new US graduates entering the market"的程式化培养期假设;图 D-1 注明 "Adapted with permission from https://policonomics.com/cobweb-model"(一个经济学科普博客)。文章中必须写明"这是教科书式模型推演"。(3) 论断B的摘要级内容逐字成立(见 evidence),但 **0.67 这个数字及其标准误我无法在任何一手或二手来源中找到**,ScienceDirect 摘要、highlights、RePEc 摘要均无此数;"三年前"的确切设定原文是 "most strongly related to wages observed three years earlier, when students were college freshmen" / highlight "Relationship is statistically significant and largest for a three year lag" —— 即"三年滞后项系数最大",不是"弹性=0.67 对应三年前"。**建议:文章中删去 0.67，或改写为"完成学位数与三年前对口职业工资正相关，作者自述该平均反应是 modest"**。(4) 论断C需补量化值:IZA DP 4127 给出"10% 预期收入上升"对应弹性 **理科 0.09、人文社科 0.14、法/经/管 0.12**;发表版摘要用词是 "a very low, though significant, elasticity"。另需提示:Beffy 测的是"专业选择概率对预期收入"的弹性(法国,三阶段结构模型),与 LGHK 测的"完成学位数对对口职业工资溢价"不是同一个量,**两者不可直接对比强弱**。发表版标题是 "Choosing the Field of Study in Postsecondary Education"(无连字符),REStat 94(1):334–347, 2012年2月。(5) 论断D:Ezekiel QJE 52(1938)起页 255、Freeman ILR Review 29(2):236–248 (1976) 经 Crossref 核实存在;**Freeman 本人给出的周期长度与弹性数值我完全未取得(ILR Review/SAGE 403、JSTOR 付费墙),文章中不得给 Freeman 安上任何具体数字**;Freeman 1975 AER 物理学家论文在 Crossref 中检索不到条目(AER 早期只在 JSTOR),该引用未获一手证实。

**核验依据**:1) NAE 2018《Understanding the Educational and Career Pathways of Engineers》Appendix D 全文(pp.180–181)取自 https://nap.nationalacademies.org/read/25284/chapter/11 ,逐字含 "the higher wage induces more students to study engineering"、"Once the additional engineers enter the market, however, the wage drops"、"there is likely to be talk of a 'glut' of engineers four years after complaints of 'shortages.'"、"For about four years ... the market is likely to stay at point b"、以及上引 1990 年签证段落与 policonomics 图源。2) LGHK 摘要逐字见 https://econpapers.repec.org/RePEc:eee:ecoedu:v:49:y:2015:i:c:p:1-14 与 Wayback 存档的 ScienceDirect 页 https://web.archive.org/web/20220308005038/https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S027277571500103X (含 4 条 Highlights)。全文取不到:ScienceDirect /abs/ 403、/am/(开放稿)403、r.jina.ai 被 Cloudflare 拦、Wayback 无 /am/ 快照、OpenAlex 标记 closed、CALDER/CEDR/ERIC/HAL 均无副本。DOI 10.1016/j.econedurev.2015.07.007(Crossref 确认 vol 49, 1-14, 2015-12)。3) Beffy/Fougère/Maurel:IZA DP No. 4127 全文 https://docs.iza.org/dp4127.pdf ,p.19 "simulating a 10% increase in the expected earnings for each major yields low elasticities of respectively 0.09 for sciences, 0.14 for humanities and social sciences, and finally 0.12 for law, economics and management";发表版摘要 https://econpapers.repec.org/RePEc:tpr:restat:v:94:y:2012:i:1:p:334-347 。4) Crossref: 10.2307/1881734 (Ezekiel, QJE 52, p.255, 1938-02);10.1177/001979397602900204 (Freeman, ILR Review 29, 236-248, 1976-01)。


#### [G27] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:**必须改的硬错误(1处)**:"2025 年 1L 稳定在约 38,000" —— 错。ABA《2025 Standard 509 Information Report Data Overview》(2025-12-15 发布)官方数:Fall 2025 Total First Year Enrollees **42,817**(较 2024 的 39,689 **+7.9%**),Total JD1 Cohort(含所有 1L)**43,721**(+7.6%)。38,000 大约是 Fall 2023 的水平。**这个错误方向性地削弱了文章想讲的"下一轮已起跳"——真实数据比文章写的更强,1L 已连续两年两位数区间反弹。**
**口径修正(逐条)**:
(a) 1L 峰值:ABA 官方历史表《ABA Approved Law School Enrollment and Degrees Awarded Data 1963–2013》给 2010–2011 学年 First Year Enrollment = **52,488**(不是 52,404);Total J.D. Enrollment = 147,525 ✓。
(b) 申请件数:LSAC 官方"Archive: 2000-2015 ABA End-of-Year Summaries"给 Fall 2010 ABA Applications = **604,300**、Fall 2013 = **385,800**(不是 602,300 / 385,400;LSAC 说明"Volumes are rounded to the nearest hundred",所以 602,300/385,400 不是 LSAC 发布值)。降幅 −36.2%,"三年 −36%"✓。
(c) LSAT 171,514:✓ 完全正确,且"人次"用词准确——LSAC 表名就是 "Total LSATs Administered—Counts & Percent Increases by Admin & Year",测试年度 2009–2010 合计 171,514(+13.3%),是该序列历史峰值。
(d) "2013 秋 1L 跌至不足 40,000(较 2012 再跌 10.8%),回到 1977 年水平" —— **两个序列混用**。ABA 官方历史表 2013–2014 学年 First Year Enrollment = **40,802**,2012–2013 = 44,481,YoY = **−8.3%**;−10.8% 只有把 39,675(ABA 另一口径的"first-year class/matriculants")配上 44,481 才算得出来。1977–1978 该表为 39,676,"回到 1977 年水平"的对照基准是 39,675 那一支序列。**文章要么统一用 40,802/−8.3%,要么写明 39,675 出自另一口径,不能混。**
(e) 论断B **完全成立且已回到一手表格**:Class of 2013 BPR 长期全职 26,653 = 57.0%、JD Advantage 长期全职 4,715 = 10.1%;Class of 2012 为 26,066 = 56.2%、4,387 = 9.5%;分母是 Total Graduates 46,776(表头写 "%age of Total Grads",副标题 "(9 months following graduation)");测量时点由 ABA 2014 年表脚注坐实:"class of 2013 employment statistics as of February 15th, 2014"。
(f) 论断C 的**人数**全部核对无误(2025:29,928 与 1,815;2024:31,681 + 2,250 = 33,931;2023:27,933 + 2,227),但 **82.7% / 5% / 87.7% 是自算值,不是 ABA 发布值**。ABA 的 Class of 2025 表把百分比分母从"总毕业生"改成了"**已就业毕业生**",发布的是 **89.9%** 和 **5.5%**(29,928/33,294、1,815/33,294);同时把类别名从 "Bar Passage Required" 改成 "**Bar Admission Required/Anticipated**"。Muller 的口径(剔除波多黎各三所)得 **82.5%**,与 82.7% 不一样。另:2025 届测量时点是 **2026-03-16(约 10 个月)**,2013 届是 2014-02-15(约 9 个月),**这是 57%→82.7% 之外的第二重不可比**,文章的"口径陷阱"提示应加上这一条。
(g) 论断D 的 "−7.0%" 出处要改:不是 Muller 说的,是 ABA 表自己写的 "Total Graduates 36,206 / 38,937 / -2,731 (-7.0%)";Muller 文中那个 7% 是"大所(101+ 律师)岗位同比 −7%"。两者数值巧合,别张冠李戴。Muller 原文措辞:"Outcomes improved, but only because the Class of 2025 was materially smaller than the Class of 2024"、"a placement rate of 82.5%, even as raw job totals declined by around 2000 positions year over year"。
(h) 论断E:LSAC 三处限定语**逐字全对**,+33%/+27% 也对,但必须写清这是 **2025-10-13、周期最开始**的读数(LSAC 自称当时只有全季约 15%),不是全季数。"到 2026-04 已有超 75,000 人 / 此前四年平均约 59,000" **我未能取得一手来源**(report.lsac.org/VolumeSummary.aspx 是 Power BI 动态报表,抓不到数值)——此句应标注来源或删。"56% 招生官认为政治是主因" **不在 LSAC 该博文中**,博文只说 "The especially dynamic political environment is cited as one reason" 与 "The uncertain economic climate and the potential impact of AI",56% 需另找出处否则删。

(i) LawHub 口径断裂:未能证实。ABA 官方历史表只到 2013–2014,ABA 现行统计页把 2011 年前后拆成不同文件;LawHub 那句原文我在一手页面上取不到(该表在 app.lawhub.org 登录后/Power BI 内)。**若无法直引,该注脚应改述为"ABA 官方历史表止于 2013–2014 学年,之后为逐年 xlsx,序列需自行拼接"。**

#### [G28] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:四处引语我逐字比对了 PDF 原文,**全部无误,且页码全部正确**(p.7 / p.10 / p.14 / p.15 / p.17,PDF 页码与页脚印刷页码一致)。只有一处口径必须改:
**"终身教职轨道比例从 1993 年约 34% 降至 2012 年约 26%" → 应为 "从 1993 年约 34% 降至(报告写作时可得的最新数据)约 26%"。** 报告 p.7 原文是 "has declined from ~34 percent in 1993 to ~26 percent **today**",而报告 p.17 明确写 "the SDR has some shortcomings ... the data are reported with a significant lag (**the most recent data available to the committee were from 2008**)",p.23 再次强调 "data from the SDR ... **extends only through 2008**. Therefore, much of the information presented below about career outcomes does not take into account the past four years (including the recent recession)"。对应的 Figure 12 明确是 1993–2008 区间。**把终点写成 2012 会把这条数据凭空拉近四年,对读者是实质误导——终点是 2008。**
另需在文中补两条报告自陈的缺陷(采集者已列 SDR 两条,均确认无误):不含外国培养博士("It does not include information on foreign-trained doctorates (an increasing share of the biomedical workforce)"),数据滞后至 2008。
次要精确化:p.7 原句是 "the proportion of PhDs that move into **tenured or tenure-track** faculty positions" —— 是"已获终身教职 + 终身轨"合计,不只是"终身轨"。
补充数据全部核实:2001 届生物医学博士获学位中位年龄 32、开始终身轨中位年龄 37(化学对照 30 与 33);FY2011 公立研究机构生物医学助理教授平均起薪约 $68,000,化学 $69,000,临床与健康 $79,000,经济学 over $100,000 —— 出处是 **Oklahoma State University survey of public research institutions**,不是 NIH 自采,引用时应注明。
核验点"1998 与 2011 两份 NRC 报告原文":**未取得**。我只拿到 2012 报告 p.14–15 的转引(转引内容与论断D完全吻合,见 evidence)。1998 报告 NAP catalog 页(record 6244)可打开但全文需登录;2011 报告原链接 grants.nih.gov/training/Research_Training_Biomedical.pdf 未验证。**文章若要说"1998 年 NRC 建议限制增长",目前只能靠 2012 报告的转述,应写明是转述。**

**核验依据**:一手 PDF:https://acd.od.nih.gov/documents/reports/Biomedical_research_wgreport.pdf (157 页,封面页 "June 14, 2012",共同主席 Shirley Tilghman / Sally Rockey 见名单页)。p.17 逐字:"The steep increase in the number of biomedical PhDs awarded began in 2004, just after the end of the doubling of the NIH budget (1999-2003). Given a 5-7 year training period, this illustrates a close relationship between the size of the NIH budget and the number of biomedical PhD slots." ✓ p.10 逐字:"This creates a system in which a large number of future scientists are being produced each year, well in excess of the number of research-oriented jobs in academia, government and industry." ✓ p.14 逐字:"As described later in this report, the data gathered by the ACD working group do not indicate such growth in employment opportunities. Rather, the numbers of positions available for biomedical PhDs that take advantage of their long training are less than the number of PhDs produced each year." ✓ 同页对 2011 年 Chalkley 报告的描述:"This study, chaired by Roger Chalkley ... found that, based on the observation of low unemployment rates of biomedical and behavioral scientists and models that predicted substantial growth in scientific employment opportunities over the next decade, the number of NRSA positions is adequate" ✓ p.7 逐字:"Although the vast majority of people holding biomedical PhDs are employed (i.e. unemployment is very low), the proportion of PhDs that move into tenured or tenure-track faculty positions has declined from ~34 percent in 1993 to ~26 percent today." p.15 逐字(1998 NRC):"a study published by the National Research Council in 1998, Trends in the Early Careers of Life Scientists, chaired by Shirley Tilghman ... The study concluded that the level of PhD production in 1998 exceeded the availability of jobs ... The 1998 committee recommended restraint in future growth in the number of graduate students ... It is notable that this report was released just before the doubling of the NIH budget"。p.8 逐字(年龄与薪资)。p.17 / p.23 SDR 滞后至 2008 的自陈。1998 报告目录页 https://nap.nationalacademies.org/catalog/6244/trends-in-the-early-careers-of-life-scientists (可达,全文需登录,未取得)。


#### [G29] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:**我取到了核验点要求的官方序列,结论是:调查数与官方普查数差距很大,论断A的头两个数字应当替换。**
IPEDS Completions(CIP 14.2501 石油工程,学士 AWLEVEL=5,first majors,全美)逐年:
2008: 521 | 2009: 690 | 2010: 779 | 2011: 1,030 | 2012: 1,068 | 2013: 1,130 | 2014: 1,406 | 2015: 1,688 | 2016: 1,984 | **2017: 2,124** | **2018: 2,151(峰值)** | 2019: 1,703 | 2020: 1,123 | 2021: 830 | **2022: 745** | **2023: 623** | **2024: 488**
(IPEDS 年份=学年结束年,如 2018 = 2017–18 学年。)
因此:
(1) **"峰值 2,615 人(2017)"和备选的 2,550 两个数都对不上官方序列。官方峰值是 2018 学年的 2,151 人,2017 学年为 2,124 人。** 建议直接改用 IPEDS 数,并注明 Heinze 问卷高出约 20–23%(可能因其样本含非 IPEDS 口径/境外项目/自报差异)。若坚持用调查数,必须标注"业内自报问卷,非普查"。
(2) **"2022 年 894" → 官方 745;"2023 年预期 655" → 官方实际 623;"2024 年预期约 500" → 官方实际 488。** 后两年调查数与官方数已接近,但 2022 差 20%。
(3) 峰值到 2024 的降幅:官方口径 488/2,151 = **−77.3%**,落在文章给的"75–81%"区间内,该区间可保留但应改注为"以 IPEDS 官方序列计约 −77%"。
(4) **"开设院校从 35 所降至 20 所"与官方数据方向相反。** IPEDS 中"当年至少授出 1 个石油工程学士学位"的机构数:2008 年 17 所 → 2017 年 22 所 → 2018 年 23 所 → 2022 年 25 所,一路上升。35→20 只能是 Heinze 问卷中"设有项目/回应问卷"的口径,与"授予学位机构数"不是一回事,**文章必须写明是哪一个,否则会给读者"院校在关停"的错误印象**。
(5) 论断B 的价格事实成立但滞后期要改:FRED 官方日频,WTI 2014 年 6 月月均 **$105.79**(月内最高 107.95),2016-01-20 低见 **$26.68**(全周期最低 2016-02-11 的 $26.19);Brent 2016-01-20 为 $26.01。从 105.79 到 26.68 = **−74.8%**,"−70%+"✓。但**如果用 IPEDS 官方序列,学位峰值在 2018 学年,距 2014-06 价格见顶约 3.5–4 年,不是 2.5 年,也不是"约三年"**;论断B 内部也自相矛盾(先说"滞后约 2.5 年",后说"约三年")。建议统一表述为"约三年半到四年",并保留"这是经验拟合、非发表的计量估计"的免责。
(6) 论断C 的两句引语逐字属实,但**语境要修正,否则会误导**:该文不是 SPE 集体"否定外推",而是四位学者的往返讨论;做"history matching"的 Tom Blasingame 本人恰恰做了预测——"we predict that the downward trend may stabilize as it did before and level out at the range of 250 graduates a year, while the demand for graduates is much higher";他那句 "we can't use these data to predict the next distributions" 后面紧接的是 "As many note about the present state of the industry, 'This time is different.' Petroleum engineering enrollment **will need to begin growing soon** after hitting bottom" —— 即 **"this time is different" 是他转述业内说法并顺势看多,不是他用来否定外推的证据**。真正的反对意见来自 Kelkar(引语准确:"I do not believe that oil prices are going to help us significantly in increasing the enrollment")与 Ozkan("neither captures the effect of the changing conditions")。
(7) "1984 年石油工程学士毕业生峰值 1,587 人":原文是 "The 'reference' trend shown (red) occurs over the period from 1970 to 2000, which **peaked at 1,587 BS graduates around 1984**",**这是拟合高斯分布的峰值、且是"around 1984"**,底层数据来自 Heinze 与 Calhoun 1991 年的一篇背景论文,不是某一年的普查实数。写作时应加"拟合趋势峰值"限定。
(8) 承载 2,615 / 894 / 35→20 的那篇 SPE JPT 文章我**未能定位到**(jpt.spe.org 站内搜索为 JS 动态、多次 slug 猜测均 404、Bing/DDG 无有效结果),因此这些具体数字**在一手层面仍未证实**;但已被 IPEDS 官方序列取代,不影响结论方向。

**核验依据**:1) IPEDS 官方完成数,直接从 NCES 原始数据文件计算:https://nces.ed.gov/ipeds/datacenter/data/C2008_A.zip 至 C2024_A.zip (逐年下载、解压 cYYYY_a.csv,筛 CIPCODE 前缀 14.25、AWLEVEL=5、MAJORNUM=1,对 CTOTALT 求和;各年 award level 分布已交叉验证,如 2018 年:学士 2,151、硕士 515、博士 133)。2) SPE JPT《History Matching of Petroleum Engineering Graduation Rates》(Stephen Rassenfoss, 2022-03-01)全文:https://jpt.spe.org/history-matching-of-petroleum-engineering-graduation-rates —— 含上引 Blasingame 的 1,587/250 graduates a year/"we can't use these data to predict the next distributions"/"This time is different" 各句,以及 Kelkar 与 Ozkan 原话;文末 Reference 为 John C. Calhoun, 'A Brief History of a Petroleum Engineering Education in the United States', 1991。3) 油价:FRED 日频 DCOILWTICO 与 DCOILBRENTEU,https://fred.stlouisfed.org/graph/fredgraph.csv?id=DCOILWTICO,DCOILBRENTEU —— WTI 2014-06 月均 105.79、月内高 107.95;WTI 2016-01-20 = 26.68,全期最低 2016-02-11 = 26.19;Brent 2016-01-20 = 26.01。4) 未能取得:承载 2,615/2,550 的 SPE JPT 文章(站内搜索 JS 化、多次 URL 猜测 404)。


### 第 2 席


#### [G26] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(a) NAE 附录 D 的"四年/一年"——原表述"美国国家工程院 2018 年报告…并指出 H-1B 扩张把调整期压缩到约一年"读起来像机构的计量结论 → 正确表述:附录 D 是全长两页(pp.180–181)的教科书式静态图解,零数据、零估计,连图 D-1 都标注"Adapted with permission from https://policonomics.com/cobweb-model"。"四年"是假定的学位年限("For about four years… the market is likely to stay at point b";"talk of a 'glut' of engineers four years after complaints of 'shortages'"),"一年"是签证申请周期("The number of engineers can be changed with no more than a year's lag (the time to apply for the annual visa distribution)")。两个数字都是模型推演,不是计量估计;文中亦未出现"H-1B 把调整期压缩到一年"这种因果断言,原文只说 1990 年技术移民临时签证扩张"allowed just such a short cut to the equilibrium",且结论是"the cycles are both faster and associated with smaller wage changes"。

(b) 弹性 0.67——原表述"完成学位数对对口职业工资溢价的弹性 0.67" → 正确表述:0.67 是**6 位 CIP 专业占学士学位总数之份额**对**对口职业相对工资**(该职业 CPS 周薪均值 ÷ 全职业周薪均值,不是"溢价")滞后三年的弹性。它**不是回归系数,论文没有报告任何标准误**:原文是把加权平均边际效应 0.014 在加权平均专业份额 2.07% 处换算而来("a 0.1 increase in relative wages would increase this average major's share from 2.07% to 2.21%, for an elasticity of 0.67"),且脚注 29 明写"These regression results are not shown, but are available from the authors"。同段还给出 R²=0.02——t-3 年工资只解释学位产出变异的约 2%。作者自评为"modest"。时间窗:摘要写 1982–2012,但数据节写 IPEDS 学位 1987–2011、CPS 工资 1983–2012,应以后者为准。异质性(男 0.023/女 0.009;白 0.016、亚裔 0.019 vs 黑 **−0.006**、拉美裔 **−0.003**;高 SAT 0.020/低 SAT 0.004)来自**华盛顿州 ERDC 行政数据 2007–2012、8 所公立大学 58,511 名毕业生**,不是全国 1982–2012 序列;且是 SAT 分数不是泛指"测验分数"。

(c) Beffy 的量化值——原表述"弹性非常低"无数字 → 正确表述:0.09(理科)、0.14(人文社科)、0.12(法/经/管),对应"10% 工资上升";IZA DP 4127(2009-04)正文"These elasticities appear to be very low, which means that the choice of a major is mainly driven by non-pecuniary factors",结论节"a low elasticity…quantitatively small even though it is statistically significant";数据为法国 CEREQ Génération 92 + Génération 98 两轮调查。

(d) **本条最重要的推翻**:把 0.67 与 Beffy 的"非常低"并列成"美国有弹性 / 法国无弹性"的对照是**不成立的,而且 Long 等人自己就否掉了这个对照**。原文 p.23:Beffy 的**百分点效应更大**(10% 工资上升带来 0.25/0.53/0.40 个百分点),Long 自己只有 0.14 个百分点;弹性之所以反过来,纯粹因为 Beffy 用 3 个大类(基数份额大)而 Long 用 6 位 CIP(平均份额仅 2.07%)。原文:"We find a smaller percentage point effect, but a larger elasticity than Beffy et al. (2012)… since they are evaluating relationships using aggregated major groups that have larger initial shares… They characterize their results as 'quantitatively small even though they are statistically significant' (p. 342), **and we largely share this conclusion**." 文章若要做跨国对照,必须换成同口径,否则应删除该对照。

(e) Freeman 的具体数字:**取不到,判 UNVERIFIABLE,文章中不得给 Freeman 安上任何周期长度或弹性数值**。书目本身已由 Crossref 核实无误:Freeman RB, "A Cobweb Model of the Supply and Starting Salary of New Engineers", ILR Review 29(2):236–248, 1976, DOI 10.1177/001979397602900204;Ezekiel, "The Cobweb Theorem", QJE 52(2), 1938, 起始页 255,DOI 10.2307/1881734。

**核验依据**:1) NAE 附录 D 全文(HTTP 200 抓取 https://nap.nationalacademies.org/read/25284/chapter/11 → https://www.nationalacademies.org/read/25284/chapter/11,pp.180–181),逐字核到"the higher wage induces more students to study engineering""Once the additional engineers enter the market, however, the wage drops""there is likely to be talk of a 'glut' of engineers four years after complaints of 'shortages.'""Since the expansion in 1990 of the number of skilled immigrant temporary visas permitted, immigration has allowed just such a short cut to the equilibrium. The number of engineers can be changed with no more than a year's lag (the time to apply for the annual visa distribution), so the long-run supply curve is flatter: the cycles are both faster and associated with smaller wage changes." 参考文献仅两条(Freeman 1976;Ryoo & Rosen 2004),无数据表。
2) Long/Goldhaber/Huntington-Klein 接受稿全文 PDF(819 KB),经 IA Scholar 定位后由 Wayback 取得 https://web.archive.org/web/2018id_/https://manuscript.elsevier.com/S027277571500103X/pdf/S027277571500103X.pdf(ScienceDirect am/ 版 403,Unpaywall oa_status=closed)。上述 0.014 / 2.07% / 0.67 / R²=0.02 / 脚注 29 / 与 Beffy 的对比段 / 表 6 分组边际效应,均为该 PDF 逐字。DOI 为 10.1016/j.econedurev.2015.07.007(非 .003)。
3) Beffy, Fougère, Maurel, IZA DP 4127(2009-04)全文 PDF:https://docs.iza.org/dp4127.pdf,弹性 0.09/0.14/0.12 见正文第 19 页段落,"very low"见引言,"low elasticity"见结论。
4) Freeman 1976:Sage(journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/001979397602900204)403;Unpaywall 返回 is_oa=false、oa_locations=[]、has_repository_copy=false;Semantic Scholar 摘要被出版商 elided;JSTOR/DDG 均不可达(DDG 触发人机验证)。确认无任何合法开放全文路径。


#### [G27] CORRECTED · confidence=high

**修正**:(1) **2025 年 1L 数字是硬错误**。原表述"2025 年 1L 稳定在约 38,000" → 正确表述:2025 年秋 1L 入学 **42,817 人,较 2024 年增 7.9%,是 2012 年以来最大的一届**。LSAC 官方博文(2026-04-08,Elizabeth Bodamer)逐字:"legal education welcomed its largest first-year class since 2012, an 8% increase over the 2024 incoming class";ABA 口径 42,817 / 2025 年秋总 JD 在读 120,039(较 2024 年 +4,629,+4%)。约 38,000 是 2023 年前后的水平,不是 2025 年。这条错误方向恰好相反,对准大学生最具误导性——文章若用它论证"法学院已回到稳态",实际数据显示**下一轮扩张已经落到入学端,不只是申请端**。
(2) "总 JD 在读较 2010 峰值低 18.6%"经算术复核成立:120,039 / 147,525 = 81.4%,即 −18.6%(该式同时反证 147,525 这一 2010 峰值)。
(3) **新增一处口径断裂,原稿完全没提**:2013 届是**毕业后 9 个月**口径,2025 届是**毕业后 10 个月(2026-03-15)**口径。ABA 2013 年官方汇总表页眉逐字"(9 months following graduation)";Muller 2026-04-23 文首逐字"These are ten-month figures from March 15, 2026 for the Class of 2025." 所以即便按"BPR 对 BPR"(57.0% vs 82.7%)相减,两端测量窗口也不同,必须加限定语,否则会系统性高估修复幅度。
(4) 样本范围差异:ABA 官方 82.7%/87.7% 覆盖 **195 所法学院(含波多黎各 3 所)**;Muller 的 82.5% 明写"excluding Puerto Rico's three law schools"。原表述给 82.7% 却引 Muller,应说明两者是不同全集,不是矛盾。
(5) **LSAC 归因被反向简化**。原表述"LSAC 与招生官把激增归因于'especially dynamic political environment'与'uncertain economic climate'" → 正确表述:LSAC 2025-10-13 博文在"What May Be Driving These Numbers?"一节给出的**首要解释是结构性/时序性的**——"More law schools are now offering early decision programs and emphasizing rolling admissions, which may be creating a larger early surge in applications",以及上一季极度竞争后常见的"wave of early applicants";政治/经济环境出现在另一节"The Value of Legal Education"里,讲的是"为什么现在很多人对法律教育有兴趣",不是对早期激增的归因。"56% 招生官认为政治是主因"**不在该 LSAC 博文中**,我未能定位其一手出处,文章引用前必须自行补源或删除。
(6) **+33% 不能当作全季数字**。LSAC 三处限定语逐字无误:"this is extremely early data, and it should be viewed as broadly directional at best";"At this point in the cycle, we typically have only about 15 percent of the total number of applicants and applications we expect to see by the end of the cycle";"the total number of applicants is currently up 33 percent. Applications are up 27 percent"(基准是"as of early October")。而 LSAC 2026-04-08 博文给出 2024-25 季**全季申请人已超 76,000、为 2011 年以来最高、同比 +18%**——因此"到 2026-04 已超 75,000 人"相对于上一季的 76,000+ 大致**持平**,"+27%"是拿它与被压低的"前四年平均约 59,000"相比得出的。这两个数字必须写清基准,否则会让读者以为早期 +33% 延续到了全季(LSAC 自己警告过不会)。
(7) 论断 B 的 57.0%/10.1% **已由一手件确认,不再是薄环节**——但要补一句口径:百分比的分母是**全部毕业生 46,776 人**,不是"就业状态已知"的 45,695 人。
(8) LawHub 口径断裂那句话我未能定位到一手出处,建议核实后再引或改述。

**核验依据**:1) ABA 一手汇总表(americanbar.org 现役站点对 curl/WebFetch 均返回 Cloudflare 403,改走 Wayback id_ 原始快照):https://web.archive.org/web/20140702205932id_/http://www.americanbar.org/content/dam/aba/administrative/legal_education_and_admissions_to_the_bar/statistics/2013_law_graduate_employment_data.authcheckdam.pdf —— 页眉"2013 LAW GRADUATE EMPLOYMENT DATA (9 months following graduation)";Class of 2013:Bar Passage Required Long-Term/Full-Time 26,653 = 57.0%,JD Advantage Long-Term/Full-Time 4,715 = 10.1%;Class of 2012:26,066 = 56.2%、4,387 = 9.5%;Total Graduates 46,364(2012)/46,776(2013)。三个数全部逐字命中。
2) ABA 2026-04 新闻稿逐字"31,743 or 87.7% of the 2025 graduates of the 195 law schools…were employed in full-time, long-term Bar Admission Required/Anticipated or J.D. Advantage jobs"(americanbar.org/news/abanews/aba-news-archives/2026/04/law-school-employment-rate-remains-high/,站点 Cloudflare 拦截,经检索层取得原句);与 29,928+1,815=31,743、29,928/36,206=82.66% 完全自洽。
3) Derek Muller, https://excessofdemocracy.com/blog/2026/4/class-of-2025-legal-employment-outcomes-mixed-placement-rates-improve-but-total-jobs-large-law-firm-jobs-in-decline(2026-04-23)全文抓取:"These are ten-month figures from March 15, 2026";"excluding Puerto Rico's three law schools";"a placement rate of 82.5%, even as raw job totals declined by around 2000 positions year over year";"large law firm placement worsened… they declined by 7% year over year"(注意:这个 7% 是大所岗位同比降幅,不是毕业班规模降幅,勿混用)。
4) LSAC https://www.lsac.org/blog/too-soon-predictions-2026-admission-cycle-starting-strong(2025-10-13,Sudha Setty)全文抓取,三处限定语与 +33%/+27% 逐字核对无误。
5) LSAC https://www.lsac.org/blog/2025-1l-class-was-largest-recent-years-first-gen-college-grad-representation-declines-again(2026-04-08)全文抓取:"more than 76,000 aspiring law students submitted applications…highest volume of applicants since 2011 and an 18% increase";"largest first-year class since 2012, an 8% increase over the 2024 incoming class"。
6) 2025 秋 ABA 口径 1L 42,817 / 总 JD 120,039 / 全校总注册 145,116,与 120,039÷147,525=−18.6% 互证。

(2) 类别口径:原文是"tenured **or** tenure-track faculty positions",即含已获终身教职者,不止"终身轨"。
(3) 2011 年 NRC(Chalkley)报告的结论要写全:不只是"NRSA 岗位够用",原文是"the number of NRSA positions is adequate and should remain at the same level **in biomedicine and should be increased in behavioral sciences**"(p.14)。
(4) 1998 年 NRC 报告的正式名称与出处应补上:《Trends in the Early Careers of Life Scientists》,Commission on Life Sciences, NRC, National Academy Press, 1998(p.15 及脚注 10)。
其余全部 HOLDS,逐字与页码如下:p.17 "The steep increase in the number of biomedical PhDs awarded began in 2004, just after the end of the doubling of the NIH budget (1999-2003). Given a 5-7 year training period, this illustrates a close relationship between the size of the NIH budget and the number of biomedical PhD slots.";p.10 "This creates a system in which a large number of future scientists are being produced each year, well in excess of the number of research-oriented jobs in academia, government and industry.";p.14 "Rather, the numbers of positions available for biomedical PhDs that take advantage of their long training are less than the number of PhDs produced each year.";p.14 "the data gathered by the ACD working group do not indicate such growth in employment opportunities.";p.7 "Although the vast majority of people holding biomedical PhDs are employed (i.e. unemployment is very low), the proportion of PhDs that move into tenured or tenure-track faculty positions has declined from ~34 percent in 1993 to ~26 percent today.";p.15 "It is notable that this report was released just before the doubling of the NIH budget, which may have affected the perception of the urgency of its recommendations.";p.8 "For PhDs graduating in 2001, the median age for biomedical scientists was 32 and the median age for starting a tenure track position was 37";p.8 "average starting salaries in fiscal year 2011 for biomedical assistant professors were approximately $68,000…and over $100,000 for economists"(来源为 Oklahoma State University 对公立研究型院校的薪酬调查,应注明)。
